Alberto(VO)：我让你们Elorza家随便找个人当Isadora的丈夫来敷衍我，那就要付出代价！
Alberto (VO): So the Elorzas think they can just find some random guy to pose as Isadora’s husband and brush me off? Then they’ll have to pay the price!
△Julián松了一口气，得意地哈哈大笑。
Julián：Damián，怎么样？你不是喜欢笑吗？怎么不笑了！
Julián: Well, Damián? You were all smiles before. What happened? Not laughing anymore?
△Isadora拦在Damián面前，盯着Julián。
Isadora：（冷声）不就是三千万美元吗？我给。
Isadora (coldly): It’s only thirty million dollars. I’ll pay.
△Isadora刚要拿出支票，被Damián拦住。
Damián：（淡淡）要给，也是他们给。
Damián: (With a faint smile) If anyone’s paying, it’ll be them.
△Isadora疑惑地看着Damián。

第二十六集
26-1夜 内 宴会厅
人物：Damián、Isadora、Julián、Humberto、Alberto、专家、宾客
△Isadora疑惑地看着Damián。
Isadora：（担忧）老公，你是学计算机的，不懂宝石也正常，我赔得起。
Isadora: (worried) Damián, you studied computer science. It’s perfectly normal if you don’t know much about gemstones. Don’t worry—I can afford to pay the thirty million.
△Damián摇了摇头。
Damián：相信我。
Damián: Trust me.
△Isadora看着Damián自信的眼睛，犹豫了一下，点了点头。
△Humberto皱了皱眉。
△Damián向前一步，看向两个专家。
Damián：（淡淡）两位专家，据我所知，Julián佩戴的这枚胸针是由Severino Vega大师亲手打造的对吧。
Damián: (With a faint smile) Gentlemen, as far as I know, the brooch Julián is wearing was handcrafted by Master Severino Vega. Is that correct?
△众人脸色一变，震惊地看着Damián。
△Alberto皱眉，开始认真打量着Damián。
Humberto：（惊喜）你还知道Severino Vega？
Humberto: (surprised) You know about Severino Vega as well?
△专家一诧异地点了点头。
专家一：是这样的，同时这也是Severino Vega的遗作。
Expert 1: That's correct. This was also Severino Vega's final work.
△Damián点了点头。
Damián：Severino Vega的作品都是手工雕刻，红钻又是纯天然的宝石，在灯光下会有自然的火彩，手摸上去的触感是冰凉的。
Damián: Severino Vega’s pieces were all carved by hand. And since red diamonds are natural gemstones, they should show a natural fire under the light and feel cool to the touch.
△专家们一边听，一边频频点头赞同。
△Julián脸色难看。
Julián：（怒）Damián，你什么意思？人家专家都说我的胸针是真的了，你还要坚持吗？
Julián: (Angry) Damián, what’s your point? The experts already confirmed my brooch is genuine. Are you seriously still claiming it’s fake?
△Damián表情丝毫不慌。
Damián：别急，我这就拆穿你。
Damián: Not so fast. I’m about to prove that brooch is a fake.
△Damián拿起胸针，对准灯光，胸针的钻石闪着光。
Damián：首先，这胸针是由十三颗红钻组成的，除了中间那颗最大的，其余十二颗的火彩都很好。可中间这颗却没有那么亮眼。
Damián: First, this brooch is set with thirteen red diamonds. Except for the largest stone in the center, the other twelve all show excellent fire. But the center stone doesn’t have nearly the same brilliance.
△Damián又对着胸针哈了口气，小钻石表面的雾气很快散去，大钻石上的雾气散得很慢。
Damián：其次，中间这颗的雾气散得也很慢，（伸手摸了摸钻石）也没有钻石天然的凉感。
Damián: Second, the fog on the center stone takes much longer to dissipate. (reaches out and touches the stone) And it doesn’t feel naturally cool to the touch like a real diamond should.
△Julián浑身颤抖，表情心虚。
Julián：你胡说！你一个平民，懂什么珠宝钻石？别装了！
Julián: That’s nonsense! You’re just a commoner. What could you possibly know about jewelry and diamonds? Stop pretending you know what you’re talking about!
△Alberto不满地看着Humberto。
Alberto：我们Salazar家族流传百年，镇宅之宝更不可能是假货！Humberto，Damián故意找碴，你们到底想干什么！
Alberto: The Salazar family has stood for over a century! There’s no way our most prized heirloom could be a fake! Humberto, Damián is clearly trying to stir up trouble. What exactly are you Elorzas trying to pull?!
△Humberto淡淡地看了眼Alberto。
Humberto：你这话就不对了，我们可是世交，Damián也只是说出了自己看到的而已。
Humberto: Come on, Alberto. Our families have been close for generations. Damián’s simply pointing out what he noticed.
△Damián点了点头。
Damián：我还没说完呢。还有最后一点，所有经手Severino Vega的钻石都会被他用细小的刻针留下“SV”的水印标记，但据我观察，中间这颗并没有。
Damián: I’m not finished. There’s one final detail. Every diamond Severino Vega worked on bears his signature “SV,” engraved with an extremely fine-point tool. But the center stone has no such mark..
△Julián僵在原地，Alberto皱眉瞪着Julián。
Damián：所以，这根本就是一大块红色玻璃，而非红钻。
Damián: So there you have it. The center stone isn’t a red diamond at all. It’s nothing but a chunk of red glass.
△众人哗然。

第二十七集
27-1夜 内 宴会厅
人物：Damián、Isadora、Julián、Humberto、Alberto、Eduardo Vega、专家、宾客
△Julián恼羞成怒的盯着Damián。
Julián：你胡说！
Julián: You're talking nonsense.
△宴会大门被推开，Eduardo Vega 戴着由珠宝点缀的眼镜，缓缓走了进来。【人名：Eduardo Vega｜Severino Vega传人，珠宝大师】
Eduardo Vega | Severino Vega’s Successor, Master Jeweler
△Humberto眼睛一亮。
Humberto：说这么多干什么，Severino Vega的传人Eduardo Vega先生来了，让他来看一眼，一切都真相大白了！
Humberto: No need to keep arguing about it. Eduardo Vega, Severino Vega’s successor, is here. Let him take a look, and we’ll know the truth once and for all.
△Isadora上前扶着Eduardo Vega。
Eduardo Vega：（皱眉）你们讨论什么呢？这么激烈？
Eduardo Vega: (frowning) What’s going on here? What’s everyone arguing about?
△Alberto眼睛一亮，连忙招手，指着Damián。
Alberto：老朋友，你快来看看，这小子非说我们家的红蜂胸针是红玻璃！
Alberto: Eduardo, my old friend, you’ve got to take a look at this! This kid insists that our family’s Red Bee Brooch is just a piece of red glass!
△Eduardo Vega一顿，有些不满地看着Damián。
Eduardo Vega：你？你是谁？
Eduardo Vega: You? Who are you?
△Isadora笑着介绍。
Isadora：Eduardo Vega先生，这是我的丈夫，Damián。
Isadora: Mr. Eduardo Vega, this is my husband, Damián.
△Damián点头示意。
Damián：我的意思是，这胸针是假的。
Damián: What I'm trying to say is, this Red Bee Brooch is fake.
△Eduardo Vega皱眉拿过胸针。
Eduardo Vega：不可能，这是我太爷爷亲手……
Eduardo Vega: That’s impossible. My great-grandfather handcrafted this himself...
△Eduardo Vega看着胸针一愣，随后浑身颤抖，表情愤怒。
△Julián紧张地攥紧了拳头。
△Alberto紧张地看着Eduardo Vega。
Alberto：Erhard，怎么了？
Alberto:Erhard, what's wrong?
△Eduardo Vega愤怒地瞪着Alberto和Julián。
Eduardo Vega：（怒）你们怎么敢的！竟然敢私自拆卸我太爷爷的心血！还用这么一块廉价的红玻璃来恶心人！
Eduardo Vega: (angry) How dare you! You tampered with my great-grandfather’s masterpiece without permission—and replaced his work with a cheap piece of red glass! What an insult!
△众人表情震惊，随后一阵哗然。
△Humberto惊喜地看着Damián。
Humberto：（笑）这不就跟Damián说的一模一样！Alberto啊，看来你们家进贼了啊！
Humberto: (laughs) Well, that’s exactly what Damián said! Alberto, looks like you’ve got a thief in the family!
△Alberto脸色难看，一阵红一阵白，气得浑身颤抖。
△Julián被吓得低着头，脸色惨白。
Julián：爷爷……
Julián: Grandpa……
△Alberto一巴掌搭在Julián脸上。
Alberto：（怒）我都不知道我们Salazar家族已经落魄到要卖传家宝换钱的地步了！
Alberto: (angry) Since when has the Salazar family fallen so low that we have to sell our own family heirlooms for money?!
Julián：（捂着脸委屈）爷爷，我不知道。
Julián: (Covering his face in frustration) Grandpa, I didn't know about any of this!
△Alberto吹胡子瞪眼地看着Julián。
Alberto：你不知道？这胸针一直放在你那里，你还敢说你不知道！立刻滚回家，说清楚红钻到底去哪了！
Alberto: You didn’t know?! You’ve had that brooch this whole time, and you still dare claim you know nothing about it? Get home right now and tell me where that red diamond went!
△Alberto刚要带着Julián离开，Humberto笑着用手杖拦住二人。
Humberto：（笑）Alberto，你们是不是忘了什么东西？
Humberto: (laughs) Alberto, have you forgotten something?
△Damián上前一步，笑着看着Alberto，摆了摆手。
△Alberto强压怒气，从怀里掏出支票，递给Damián。
Alberto：（皱眉）年轻人，我相信你，支票给你，金额你自己填好了！
Alberto: (frowning) Young man, you’ve proven your point. Here’s the check. Fill in whatever amount you see fit.
△Damián毫不客气地结果支票。
Damián：（笑）谢了，老头。
Damián: (laughs) Thanks, old man.
△Alberto被气得瞪了眼Damián。
△Isadora捂着嘴在一边偷笑。
Humberto：（不痛不痒，笑着提醒）Damián，这是你长辈，不能没礼貌。
Humberto: (Nonchalant, but with a smile, he reminds him) Damián, he’s your elder. Mind your manners.
△Damián点了点头。
Damián：爷爷，我知道了。
Damián: Grandpa, got it.

第二十八集
28-1夜 内 宴会厅
人物：Damián、Isadora、Julián、Humberto、Alberto、宾客
△Alberto瞪着Damián，不满地看向Humberto。
Alberto：老朋友，无论怎么说，当初Isadora和Julián的婚约已经定下来了，你们现在爽约，是不是该补偿Julián啊？
Alberto: Humberto, no matter how you look at it, Isadora and Julián’s marriage was settled years ago. Now that you’ve gone back on the agreement, shouldn’t you compensate Julián?
Alberto(VO)：就算Isadora结婚了，还有Samanta呢，不能让Julián空手回去！
Alberto (VO): Isadora may already be married, but there’s still Samanta. I can’t let Julián go home with nothing.
△Humberto眯着眼睛盯着Alberto。
Humberto(VO)：这个Alberto，刚在我这吃了亏，现在就要找回来啊。
Humberto (VO): That Alberto... he just lost out to me, and now he’s already trying to get even.
△Damián挑眉看着Alberto，假装震惊。
Damián：Salazar先生，你这话是什么意思啊？我记得Julián不是已经找到真爱了吗？
Damián: That Alberto... he just took a loss from me, and now he’s already trying to get even.
△Julián惊恐地瞪大了眼睛。
△周围宾客好奇观望。
△Julián咬牙威胁Damián。
Julián: Damián!你不要乱说！
Julián: Damián! Don’t make things up!
△Alberto不可置信。
Alberto：（皱眉）什么真爱？Julián一直在国外读书，单身一个，怎么可能有真爱？
Alberto (frowning): True love? What are you talking about? Julián’s been studying abroad and he’s been single this whole time. How could he possibly have fallen in love?
△Julián立刻点头附和。
Julián：没错，Damián，空口无凭。就算你是为了刚才的事情报复我，可我们已经给你钱了，你大可不必故意抹黑我！
Julián: Exactly, Damián. You have no proof. Even if you’re just trying to get back at me for what happened earlier, we’ve already paid you. There’s no need to deliberately smear my name!
△Damián笑了笑，双手插兜，往前一步。
Damián：（讥讽）Julián，你跟我前女友出轨的视频我可有备份，难道你要大家一起看看吗？
Damián: (Mockingly) Julián, I’ve got a backup of the video of you cheating with my ex-girlfriend. You want me to play it for everyone?
△Julián僵在原地。
△Damián看了眼愣住的Alberto。
Damián：（笑）闹了半天，你做的那些见不得人的事都是瞒着家里人的啊。
Damián: (laughs) So all this time, you’ve been keeping all your dirty little secrets from your family.
△Julián恼羞成怒的指着Damián。
Julián：你算什么东西！Damián，我忍你很久了！去死吧！
Julián: Who do you think you are? Damián, I am sick of your stupid face! Go to hell!
△Julián抬手打向Damián。
△Damián躲都没躲，抬手握住Julián的手，用力一捏。
△骨头碎裂的声音传来。
Julián：（面目狰狞，惨叫连连）啊啊啊！我的手！好疼！你快松手！
Julián: (With a menacing expression, he lets out repeated screams) Ah-ah-ah! My hands! You're hurting me! Let go of me now!
△Damián松了手，耸了耸肩，表情毫不在意。
Damián：不好意思，下意识反应，我也控制不了。
Damián: Sorry, it was just a reflex. I couldn’t help it.
△Damián从兜里拿出一沓照片递给Alberto。
Damián：Salazar先生，证据都在这，你自己看吧。
Damián: Mr. Salazar, all the evidence is right here. See for yourself.
△Alberto皱眉翻阅照片，手越来越抖，愤怒地瞪向Julián。
△Julián慌神。
Julián：爷爷，你听我解释！
Julián: Grandpa, let me explain! 
△Alberto又打了Julián一巴掌。
Alberto：（沉声）联姻的事情就此作罢！
Alberto: (in a grave voice) The marriage arrangement is hereby called off.
△Alberto快步离开。
Julián：（捂着脸大喊）爷爷！爷爷你等等我！
Julián: (Holding his face and shouting) Grandpa! Wait for me!
△Julián捂着脸追了上去。

第二十九集
29-1夜 内 宴会厅
人物：Damián、Isadora、Humberto、宾客
△Humberto笑呵呵地看着Damián。
Humberto：（满意）好，不错！你以后要对Isadora好一点！
Humberto: (Satisfied) Good. That’s what I like to hear. Make sure you treat Isadora well from now on.
△Damián点头。
Damián：是！爷爷！
Damián: Of course! Grandfather!
△Isadora羞涩一笑，挽住Damián。
Isadora：好了，别那么严肃，咱们聊聊天。
Isadora: Come on. Don’t be so serious—let’s chat.
29-2夜 内 车内
人物：Julián、Alberto
△Alberto脸色难看地打开车门径直坐了进去，Julián捂着脸，一脸委屈地跟了上去。
Julián：爷爷，你听我解释啊！
Julián: Grandpa, let me explain!
△Alberto冷声打断。
Alberto：别叫我爷爷！果然，不是我们Salazar家族的血脉，就养不出好东西！白瞎这么多年家族对你的培养了！连个Isadora都拿不下，我要你有什么用！
Alberto: Don’t call me Grandpa! I knew it. Without the Salazar bloodline, you could never amount to anything! All those years of the family investing in you have gone to waste! You can’t even win over Isadora. What good are you to me?!
△Julián脸色难看。
Julián：我也不知道Damián家里还会有监控，Eloísa也没告诉我啊！，那个蠢货，我一会儿就让她滚出我家！
Julián: I had no idea Damián’s place had security cameras! Eloísa never told me! That idiot—I’m kicking her out of my house as soon as I get back!
△Alberto震惊地瞪着Julián。
Alberto：你才是那个蠢货！竟然让这样的女人进了你家？Julián，我警告你，从今天开始，你的信用卡全部停掉！之后要是再犯错，就滚出我们家！
Alberto: You’re the idiot! You actually let a woman like that into your home? Julián, I’m warning you—starting today, all your credit cards are cut off! If you screw up again, get out of this family!
△Julián求饶似的看着Alberto。
Julián：爷爷，你再给我一次机会，别停我的信用卡啊！
Julián: Grandpa, please give me one more chance! Don’t cut off my credit cards!
△Alberto无奈地闭上眼睛，长长叹了一口气。
Alberto：（咬牙）Damián，我们家彻底因为你成了笑话了！
Alberto: (grinding his teeth) Damián, you’ve turned our entire family into a laughingstock!
29-3夜 内 宴会厅
人物：Damián、Isadora、Samanta、Beatriz、Serafina、Humberto、宾客
△Beatriz和Isadora、Samanta站在Humberto身边，Humberto爽朗大笑。
Humberto：哈哈哈，Alberto这回怕不是要被气个半死了，哈哈哈！
Humberto: Hahaha! Alberto must be absolutely livid this time! Hahaha!
Beatriz：（笑）爸，你之前还嫌弃Damián是个平民，现在知道这孩子的优秀了吧？
Beatriz: (laughs) Dad, you used to look down on Damián as just an ordinary guy—now you finally realize how outstanding this kid is!
△Humberto点了点头。
Humberto：的确啊，之前是我小瞧了他。（四处看）Damián那孩子呢？
Humberto: Indeed, I underestimated him. (Glances around) Where is Damián?
Isadora：他去卫生间了。
Isadora: He went to the bathroom.
△一阵悠扬的钢琴声响起，众人看向钢琴。
△Damián坐在钢琴面前，优雅地弹着。
△Samanta震惊地张大了嘴巴。
Samanta：（诧异）那是……Damián？他还会弹钢琴？
Samanta: (surprised) That's... Damián? He can play the piano?
△Isadora眼神充满爱意地看着Damián。
△宾客们也齐齐赞叹。
宾客一：（惊）左手持续低音八度颤音！右手弹奏高音区赋格！就连顶级的钢琴家也不是能很好地做到！
Guest 1: (startled) A sustained low-octave tremolo with the left hand, while the right hand plays a fugue in the upper register! Even the greatest pianists would struggle to pull this off!
宾客二：（赞叹）这是改变了李斯特的鬼火？这全球能弹完整鬼火并且不错音的不出二十个吧！还能改编得这么和谐的！太厉害了！
Guest 2: (Exclaiming) Is this Liszt’s “Ghost Fire” with a new twist? There can’t be more than twenty pianists in the world who can play the entire piece without a single wrong note! And he’s rearranged it so beautifully! That’s incredible!
△Serafina站在角落里，失神地看着Damián。
Serafina：（惊）Damián什么时候这么有才华了？我之前究竟错过了一个什么样的人？
Serafina: (startled) When did Damián become so talented? What kind of person have I actually missed out on all along?
△Isadora崇拜地看着Damián。
Isadora：（笑）又懂钻石，又会钢琴。我就说他是个天生的贵族！
Isadora: (laughs) He knows diamonds and plays the piano. I told you—he’s a natural-born aristocrat!
△钢琴曲结束，Damián起身鞠躬，周围响起雷鸣般的掌声。

第三十集
30-1夜 内 Julián家
人物：Eloísa、Jairo、Margarita、Mateo
△Eloísa坐在沙发上，给Julián打着电话。
△Jairo和Margarita坐在一旁吃着高档水果。
△Mateo坐在地上玩着玩具。【人名：Mateo Salazar｜Julián Salazar私生子】
Mateo Salazar | Julián Salazar’s Illegitimate Son
Eloísa：（撒娇）Julián哥哥，你今晚怎么还没回来啊？
Eloísa: (coquettishly) Julián, why aren't you home yet?
Julián(OS)：（暴怒）Eloísa，你这个贱人还敢给我打电话？我都要被你害死了！
Julián (OS): (Angry) Eloísa, you shameless bitch! How dare you call me after what you’ve done? You’ve nearly gotten me killed!
△Julián挂断了电话。
△Eloísa愣住，Jairo和Margarita也凑了过来。
Eloísa：（疑惑）Julián，好好地骂我干嘛？一点都没有Damián温柔！
Eloísa: (confused) Julián, why are you being so mean to me? You’re nowhere near as sweet as Damián!
△Mateo把玩具扔到Eloísa身上。
Mateo：（撒泼）我要吃水果！
Mateo: (Whining) I want fruit!
△Eloísa不耐烦。
Eloísa：这不是有吗！
Eloísa: Isn't this the case?
△Mateo疯狂把玩具扔到Eloísa身上，砸得Eloísa拼命躲闪。
Mateo：我要吃现切的！我要吃！我要吃！你这个贱女人！赶紧给我切！
Mateo: I want freshly sliced! I want it! I want it! You damn woman! Cut it for me right now!
△Eloísa无奈地起身走进厨房。
△Margarita皱眉。
Margarita：你说我们女儿之后怎么办？Damián不要她了，Julián也不给她好脸色。
Margarita: What’s going to happen to our daughter now? Damián doesn’t want her anymore, and Julián isn’t exactly treating her kindly either.
△Jairo毫不在意。
Jairo：Julián肯定比Damián好啊，这么好的房子，我是不想搬出去了。咱们想想办法，让Julián不得不跟咱们女儿结婚就好了。
Jairo: Julián is definitely a better choice than Damián. I’m not leaving a house this nice. Let’s figure out a way to make Julián marry our daughter, whether he likes it or not.
△Margarita惊喜地看着Jairo。
Margarita：老公，还是你聪明！
Margarita: Honey, you really are the smart one!
30-2夜 内 宴会厅
人物：Damián、Isadora、Samanta、Beatriz、Serafina、Humberto、宾客
△Damián拿着杯子，站在一旁，Serafina走了上去。
Serafina：（娇羞）Damián，你好厉害啊。
Serafina: (blushing) Damián, you're amazing!
△Damián皱眉向后撤了一步。
Damián：（无语）我已经结婚了，你还是别缠着我了。
Damián: (Stunned) I'm already married—stop pestering me!
△Damián看着远处的Isadora眼睛一亮。
Damián：老婆！
Damián: Darling！
△Damián小跑着跑到Isadora身旁。
△Serafina站在原地，攥紧拳头。
Serafina（VO）：我说Damián怎么突然这么有钱了，原来是和Isadora结婚了。这个废物凭什么跟我一个阶级？我一定要拆散他们，让废物滚回贫民窟！
Serafina (VO): So that’s how Damián suddenly got rich. He married Isadora. What makes this loser think he belongs in my league? I’ll break them up and send him crawling right back to the slums where he belongs!
△Serafina拿出手机，给Julián打电话。
Serafina：（阴沉）Julián，我们合作吧。
Serafina: (Sullen) Julián, let's work together!
Julián(OS)：（诧异）Serafina？你要跟我合作什么？
Julián (OS): (surprised) Serafina? What do you want?
Serafina：（眯眼）我们都想要Damián和Isadora分开。我们两个合作，拆散他们。明天是Casa Elorza公司的招商会，我现在有个计划，你要加入吗？
Serafina: (squinting her eyes) We both want Damián and Isadora to split up. If we work together, we can break them up. Tomorrow is Casa Elorza’s investor event. I have a plan. Are you in?
△Julián沉思了片刻。
Julián(OS)：（咬牙）好！
Julián (OS): (grinding his teeth) Okay.
△Serafina盯着Damián的背影，冷笑着勾起嘴角。
第三十一集
31-1夜 内 庄园书房
人物：Damián、Isadora、Samanta、Humberto
△书房正面墙都是书柜，上面摆满了书。中央放着一张古老的公爵画像。
△Humberto坐在书房里，Damián坐在Humberto对面，Humberto笑着看着Damián。
Humberto：Damián，我知道Isadora一开始跟你结婚是为了应付和Julián的婚约。但今晚我也能看出来，Isadora是真的喜欢你。
Humberto: Damián, I know Isadora originally married you to get out of her engagement to Julián. But after tonight, I can see that her feelings for you are real.
△Humberto从抽屉里拿出一份文件。
Humberto：现在只有我们两个男人在，我准备替Isadora补偿你，Casa Elorza公司百分之5的股份怎么样？
Humberto: It's just you and me here, so let me compensate you on Isadora's behalf. How would you like a five-percent stake in Casa Elorza?
△Damián一愣，然后笑了笑。
Damián：爷爷，不管Isadora是出于什么目的跟我结婚的，她现在是我的老婆，我也喜欢她，我在乎的也只有她。所以，这个股份我就不要了。
Damián: Grandpa, no matter why Isadora married me, she is my wife now, and I love her. She is all I care about, so I cannot accept the shares.
△Humberto挑了挑眉，意外地看着Damián。
△Damián起身。
Damián：爷爷，你要是没什么事我就先走了，Isadora还在等我呢。
Damián: Grandpa, if that is all, I should go. Isadora is waiting for me.
△Humberto满意地点了点头，Damián转身离开。
△Damián关上门。
Humberto：（宠溺）好了，你们两个，出来吧。
Humberto: (fondly) All right, you two. You can come out now.
△Isadora和Samanta掀开窗帘走了出来。
Isadora：（得意）Samanta，你现在放心了吧，Damián真的没有目的，别担心了。
Isadora: (proudly) See, Samanta? Damián is not after anything. You can stop worrying now.
△Samanta有些不服气。
Samanta：谁知道他是不是装的！反正我会持续关注他的！
Samanta: How do we know he is not putting on an act? I am still keeping an eye on him!
△Humberto笑着看着二人。
Humberto：你们年轻人的事我就不掺和了。不过，Isadora，你眼光很好，随手就挑了个懂的宝石鉴赏，钢琴技术也媲美一流大师的老公。
Humberto: I'll stay out of you youngsters' affairs. But Isadora, you chose well. Your husband knows fine jewelry and plays the piano like a world-class pianist.
△Isadora笑了笑。
Isadora：可能是我运气好吧。
Isadora: Maybe I just got lucky.
△Humberto点了点头。
Humberto：或许这就是上帝早就决定好的。
Humberto: Or perhaps this was God's plan all along.
31-2夜 内 庄园卧室
人物：Damián、Isadora
△Damián穿着睡衣躺在漆黑的卧室里。
Damián(VO）：（期待）明天是Casa Elorza的招商会，不知道在招商会上能签到领到什么好东西！
Damián (VO): (excited) Tomorrow is Casa Elorza's investor showcase. I wonder what reward I'll get for checking in there!
△Isadora穿着丝绸吊带睡衣，从被里钻了出来，趴在了Damián身上。
△Damián笑着一把抱住了Isadora。
Damián：（宠溺）还要？明天不是招商大会吗？你还有精力？
Damián: (affectionately) Again? We're having the investor showcase tomorrow. You still have the energy?
△Isadora捧着Damián的脸亲了一口，坐直身子，妩媚的挽了一下头发，手不老实的在Damián的伸手游走。
Isadora：（挑逗）说好了今晚要看看你的身材到底有多好的，再奖励一下，你今晚表现得很好。
Isadora (teasingly): You promised you'd show me just how good your body really is tonight. And you've been such a good boy, so I think you deserve another reward.
△Damián笑着看着Isadora，一把把Isadora翻身压在身下。
Isadora：（尖叫）啊！
Isadora: (screams) Ah!
△Damián盯着Isadora的眼睛，眼神侵略。
△Isadora表情兴奋。
Damián：那你可别后悔！
Damián: Do not say I did not warn you!
△Damián亲上Isadora。

第三十二集
32-1日 内 会议室
人物：Damián、员工
△Damián坐在中间看着下面的员工们。
Damián：今天是我们Elorza公司的招商展览会，咱们公司做产品的、做技术的都会展示自己的实力，我们网络安全部也有自己的展位。
Damián: Today is Casa Elorza's investor showcase. Every product and technology division will be presenting its work, and Cybersecurity has a booth of its own.
△Damián起身，拍了拍手，给众人鼓气。
Damián：大家不能给咱们部门丢人啊！
Damián: Let's make our department proud!
△员工们纷纷点头。
△Damián扫视了一圈，看向了门口的空位。
Damián：（皱眉）Eloísa呢？部门开会她不知道吗？
Damián: (frowning) Where is Eloísa? Does she not know we have a department meeting?
△空位旁边的员工尴尬一笑。
员工：她说她肚子疼，去厕所了。
Employee: She said her stomach hurt and went to the restroom.
△Damián不耐烦地皱起眉头。

32-2日 内 公司卫生间
人物：Eloísa
△卫生间门口挂着暂停使用的牌子。
△Eloísa靠在卫生间洗手池的台面上，拿着手机偷偷摸摸地打电话。
Eloísa：（不可置信）Julián你说什么？让我给Damián下药？这不好吧。
Eloísa: (in disbelief) What did you say, Julián? You want me to drug Damián? I'm not sure about this.
Julián（OS）：（咬牙切齿）今天全世界都在，正是让Damián出丑的大好时机！如果你不帮我，现在就带着你爸妈滚出我家！
Julián: (through gritted teeth) The whole world is watching today. This is our best chance to humiliate Damián! If you won't help me, take your parents and get the hell out of my house!
△Eloísa咬着嘴唇，深深呼出一口气。
Eloísa：（无奈）行吧。
Eloísa: (reluctantly) Fine.
△Eloísa挂了电话，冷哼一声。
Eloísa（VO）：Damián，是你先对我不好的，所以你这是活该！
Eloísa (VO): Damián, you turned on me first. Whatever happens now, you deserve it!
△Eloísa一拍手，像是突然想到什么。
Eloísa：对了，今天的展览会可以以员工身份带家属。让爸妈也去，还能混一顿饭吃，他们这几天吃得也不怎么好。
Eloísa: Oh, right. Employees can bring their families to today’s showcase. I’ll bring Mom and Dad, too. At least they can get a decent meal. They haven’t been eating well these past few days.
△Eloísa哼着歌，收起手机，打开门走了出去。
32-3日 内 会场
人物：Damián、Eloísa、宾客
△会场大厅里摆着不少展台，展台上标着各部门的名字。
△正中间的大屏上放着PPT，内容标题是“Elorza公司招商展览会”，大屏旁边还有一个巨大的幕布，把后面的东西挡得严严实实。
△宾客们穿着正装和礼裙在大厅里交涉，手里拿着Elorza公司的产品宣传单，看着展台上的模型和图纸。
△Damián带着安全部的员工走进会场，每个人身上都挂着工牌，Eloísa跟在后面。
△戒指的虚影出现在Damián面前。
戒指（OS）：（低沉）命运节点已记录。节点：Elorza公司招商展览会。命运馈赠——百毒不侵体质优化卡，启用后百毒不侵。
Ring (OS): (deep) Fate checkpoint registered. Location: Casa Elorza's investor showcase. Reward: Total Toxin Immunity. Activate to become immune to all poisons.
△Damián挑了挑眉。
Damián（VO）：（嘀咕）体质优化？这个不错啊，看来今天会有大事发生啊！
Damián (VO): (muttering) Physical enhancement? Nice. Looks like something big is about to go down today!
△戒指化作一阵绿光从Damián的脑门钻了进去，Damián整个人被绿光包围。
△Damián闭起眼睛，表情享受。
Damián：（长舒一口气，睁眼）感觉整个人都舒畅了。
Damián: (takes a deep breath and opens his eyes) I feel incredible.

第三十三集
33-1日 内 会场
人物：Damián、Eloísa、Julián、员工、宾客
△Damián表情欣喜地看着自己的双手。
△Julián站在角落里，阴沉着脸瞪着Damián。
△Julián拉着Eloísa走到角落里，把手里的小药瓶怼到了Eloísa手里。
Julián：（阴狠）这个，你拿好！
Julián: (menacingly) Here. Take this.
△Eloísa疑惑地看着手里的药瓶。
Julián：Eloísa，你最好能把这件事做好！这药很强的，吃下去就能昏迷。你找机会给Damián吃下去。
Julián: Eloísa, you'd better get this right. This drug is strong enough to knock someone unconscious. Find a chance to slip it to Damián.
Eloísa：（不解）为什么非要我来做这件事啊？
Eloísa: (confused) Why do I have to do it?
△Julián脸色难看。
Julián：你不做难道我做？
Julián: Who else is going to do it, me?
△Julián咬牙切齿。
Julián：你别搞不清楚，今天的招商会可是全球直播！Damián必须名誉扫地！谁让他这个混蛋羞辱我！
Julián: Don’t forget what’s at stake! Today’s investor showcase is being livestreamed worldwide! Damián has to be ruined! That bastard humiliated me!
△Julián看了眼表情犹豫的Eloísa，强忍住不屑，拉住Eloísa的手。
Julián：（含情脉脉）Eloísa，你相信我，只要你帮我对付了Damián，以后我绝不会放任你不管的！
Julián: (tenderly) Eloísa, trust me. Help me take down Damián, and I promise I will take care of you in the future.
△Eloísa点了点头，期待地看着Julián。
Eloísa：那你会和我结婚吗？
Eloísa: Then will you marry me?
△Julián尴尬地收回了手。
Julián：放心，等我搞定了爷爷就会跟你结婚的，你放心吧。
Julián: Do not worry. Once I convince Grandpa, we will get married. I promise.
△Eloísa重重地点了点头。
△Julián松了口气。
△Damián在展台旁边的电脑前操作，一个戴着口罩，胸前挂着工牌的员工小跑着跑到Damián身边。
员工：（试探）请问是信息安全部的Cortés先生吗？
Employee: (tentatively) Are you Mr. Cortés from Cybersecurity?
△Damián点头。
Damián：我是。
Damián: I am.
员工：我是汽车部门那边的，我们的展示台上的系统出了点问题，我们部长让我来请你去看看。
Employee: I’m with the automotive division. There’s a problem with the system at our display booth, and my manager sent me to ask if you could take a look.
Damián：（点头）行，我跟你去一趟。
Damián: (nods) All right. Show me.
33-2日 内 操作室
人物：Damián、Eloísa、员工
△Damián跟着员工走进了操作室，操作室里有一台巨大的显示器，Eloísa坐在电脑前。
△Damián看到Eloísa皱起了眉头。
Damián：Eloísa？她怎么在这？
Damián: Eloísa? What is she doing here?
△员工在一旁解释。
员工：我们先找的Roldán女士，可她搞不定，所以……
Employee: We asked Ms. Roldán first, but she couldn't fix it, so...
△Damián勾起嘴角，摆了摆手。
Damián：这不是胡闹吗，她能搞得定，那随便一个人都能搞定了。
Damián: This is ridiculous. If she can handle it, then anyone could.
△员工尴尬一笑。
员工：那就麻烦Cortés先生了。
Employee: Thank you for helping us, Mr. Cortés.
△Damián走到Eloísa身边。
Damián：（淡淡）你还不让开？
Damián: (flatly) Are moving or now?
△Eloísa魂不守舍地看了眼Damián。
Eloísa：啊？
Eloísa: Huh?
△Eloísa猛地回神，起身。
Eloísa：Damián，你来了，你先坐，我去给你倒水。
Eloísa: Damián, you are here. Take a seat. I will get you some water.
△Eloísa心事重重地走开。
△Eloísa走到饮水机前，接了一杯水，从兜里拿出一小瓶药，左右看了看，倒进了水杯里。

第三十四集
34-1日 内 会场
人物：Isadora、Mirella、宾客
△Isadora和Mirella结伴走进了会场，Isadora扫视一圈。
Isadora：（皱眉）Damián呢？
Isadora: (frowning) Where is Damián?
△Mirella摇了摇头，看向手里的展台图。
Mirella：可能在自己的展厅那边吧。
Mirella: He is probably at his department's booth.
△Isadora笑了笑。
Isadora：今天看情况，找机会告诉他，他老婆就是Elorza公司的总裁。
Isadora: I’ll see how things go today. If I get the chance, I’ll tell him his wife is the CEO of Casa Elorza.
△Mirella附和地点了点头。
△周围的宾客看到Isadora出现，纷纷围了上去。
34-2日 内 操作室
人物：Damián、Eloísa、Julián
△Damián正坐在电脑前敲着键盘。
△Eloísa一脸忐忑地把水递给Damián。
Eloísa：（心虚）你喝点水吧。
Eloísa: (nervously) Have some water.
△Damián手上动作一顿，又快速敲起键盘，看都没看，拿起水杯一饮而尽。
△Damián刚喝完水，顿了一秒。
Damián（vo）：水味道不对。
Damián（vo）：Doesn't taste right...
Damián眼睛一转，然后装作晕倒，倒在了键盘上。
△Eloísa看着Damián松了口气。
△Julián不耐烦地推门走了进来。
Julián：（咋舌）怎么这么慢？外面都快拖不住了！
Julián: (impatiently) What took so long? I can barely keep things under control out there!
Eloísa：（讪笑）这是不是起药效了？我可以走了吧。
Eloísa: (forcing a smile) Is the drug taking effect? Can I leave now?
△Eloísa刚想离开，被Julián一把拉住了。
Julián：（皱眉）走？你想去哪里？
Julián: (frowning) Leave? Where do you think you are going?
△Eloísa惊恐地看着Julián，想要挣脱。
△Julián死死抓着Eloísa，从兜里拿出一瓶矿泉水。
Julián：（冷笑）Eloísa，你不是想跟我结婚吗？乖乖喝下这瓶水我就跟你结婚。怎么样？
Julián: (coldly) Eloísa, you want me to marry you, don't you? Drink this water, and I will. How does that sound?
△Eloísa努力挣扎。
Eloísa：Julián，如果你要和我结婚的话，怎么忍心让我喝这种迷药？
Eloísa: Julián, if you’re going to marry me, how could you ask me to drink something laced with a sedative?
△Julián冷哼一声。
Julián：（凶狠）Eloísa，你不会真以为自己会和我结婚吧？我们Salazar家可是百年贵族家族，我结婚也是跟Isadora结婚，你算什么东西！还敢妄想我们家的财产！
Julián: (viciously) You didn’t seriously think I was going to marry you, did you? The Salazars are a centuries-old aristocratic family. If I’m getting married, it’ll be to Isadora. Who the hell do you think you are, dreaming about getting your hands on our family fortune?
△Eloísa不可置信地愣在原地，震惊地看着Julián。
Eloísa：（怒）Julián，你这个混蛋！
Eloísa: (furious) Julián, you bastard!
△Julián毫不留情地握住Eloísa的下巴，把矿泉水灌了进去。
△Eloísa挣扎不脱，喝进了不少，身子一软，倒在了地上。
△Julián看着Eloísa，冷哼一声。
Julián：只要把你和Damián放到幕后，再操控幕布升起，所有人都会知道你们乱搞，这样Isadora也会跟Damián离婚，跟我结婚的！
Julián: Once I put you and Damián behind the curtain, all I have to do is raise it in front of everyone. They’ll think you two were fooling around. Then Isadora will divorce him and marry me!
△Julián一转身，就看到Damián站在他身后，似笑非笑地看着Julián，拍了拍手。
Damián：厉害啊，会玩。
Damián: Impressive. You really thought this through.
△Julián被吓得身子一颤。
Julián：（瞪大了眼睛）你你你，你怎么还站着？
Julián: (eyes wide) Y-you... Why are you still standing?
△Damián点了点头，表情认可。
Damián：很好，你的想法我知道了，我现在就帮你实现。
Damián: Excellent. I see your plan. Now let me help you carry it out.
△Julián惊恐地想跑开。
△Damián起身一脚踹了过去。

第三十五集
35-1日 内 会场
人物：Isadora、Jairo、Margarita、Alberto、Mirella、Julián、Eloísa、Serafina、宾客、记者
△宾客们都坐在了台前，后面还站着一排举着相机的记者。
△Isadora坐在最中间四处张望。
Isadora：（疑惑）Damián去哪了？
Isadora: (confused) Where did Damián go?
△Alberto坐在Isadora身边，瞥了眼Isadora冷哼一声。
△Jairo和Margarita坐在最后，Margarita四处张望。
Margarita：（皱眉）Eloísa呢？她让我们来凑热闹，自己还不出面。
Margarita: (frowning) Where is Eloísa? She invited us here, then never showed up.
△Jairo吃着手里的蛋糕。
Jairo：管她呢，咱们吃饱了再说。
Jairo: Who cares? Let us eat first.
△Mirella手里拿着电动钥匙，走上了台，手里拿着麦克风。
Mirella：感谢大家参加Casa Elorza的招商会，招商会将进行全球直播。接下来，我将向大家展示我们的最终产品，请大家看向幕布。
Mirella: Thank you all for joining Casa Elorza’s investor showcase, which is being livestreamed worldwide. Now, I’d like to unveil our final product. Please turn your attention to the curtain.
△灯光聚集在幕布上，众人看向幕布。
△Mirella按下钥匙，幕布缓缓升起。
△众人的目光逐渐震惊，惊呼出声。
△Julián光着上半身，Eloísa穿着小吊带，两人相拥躺在幕布后，灯光聚集在两人身上。
宾客一：（惊呼）好像是一男一女啊！这俩人是谁啊？
Guest 1: (gasps) That looks like a man and a woman! Who are they?
宾客二：（震惊）女的不认识，男的好像是Salazar家的Julián啊！
Guest 2: (shocked) I do not recognize the woman, but the man looks like Julián Salazar!
宾客三：（刺激）好看爱看，这也太刺激了，是免费就能看的吗！
Guest 3: Now this is entertaining. Are we really getting this show for free?
△记者们纷纷举起相机疯狂拍照，闪光灯不断闪烁。
△Alberto不可置信地站起身子，看清Julián后，气得浑身颤抖，捂着胸口指着台上的Julián。
Alberto：（怒）这个混蛋，我们Salazar家的脸都要被他丢光了！赶紧给我把他抬走！
Alberto: (furious) That bastard has humiliated the entire Salazar family! Get him off that stage, now!
△几个黑衣保镖冲上台，抬起Julián匆匆下台。
△Isadora震惊地看着台上的两人。
△躲在角落里的Serafina，表情愕然。
Serafina：（震惊）怎么是Julián？Damián呢？为什么会这样？
Serafina: (shocked) Why is it Julián? Where is Damián? How did this happen?
△Serafina攥紧双拳，咬牙切齿。
Serafina：Julián这个蠢货，这点小事都办不好！我看你怎么收场！
Serafina: That idiot Julián could not even handle one simple job! Let us see how he gets out of this mess.
△Jairo和Margarita本来站在最后看热闹，在看到旁边记者相机里的照片后脸色一变，冲上台去。
△两人跪在女儿身边。
Margarita：（哀号）Jairo，你快把Eloísa弄下去！
Margarita: (wailing) Jairo, get Eloísa out of there!
△Jairo震惊地拍了拍Eloísa的脸。
Jairo：Eloísa，你快醒醒！这可是全球直播，你完了！
Jairo: Eloísa, wake up! This is streamed worldwide. You are ruined!
△Eloísa缓缓睁开眼睛，四处看了看。
Eloísa：（懵）爸？妈？这里是哪里？Damián呢？
Eloísa: (dazed) Dad? Mom? Where am I? Where is Damián?
△Margarita大声哭嚎。
Margarita：还管什么Damián啊，你刚刚和Julián躺在这里了，全世界都看到了！
Margarita: Forget Damián! The entire world just saw you lying there with Julián!
△Eloísa愣住，脸色惨白。
Eloísa：什么？Julián!
Eloísa: What? Julián!
△Eloísa再次晕了过去。

第三十六集
36-1日 内 会场
人物：Damián、Isadora
△Damián慢悠悠走了出来，Isadora一脸焦急地跑到Damián身边。
△Isadora抓着Damián来回打量。
Isadora：Damián，你没事吧？
Isadora: Damián, are you all right?
△Damián表情懵。
Damián：怎么了？
Damián: What happened?
Isadora：没什么，就是Julián和Eloísa刚刚一起躺在幕布后面。（叹气）这次各大家族的掌势人都在，现在Julián闹出了性丑闻，Salazar家算是完了。
Isadora: Nothing, except Julián and Eloísa were just found half-dressed behind the curtain. (sighs) Every major business family witnessed the scandal. The Salazar family’s reputation is in ruins.
△Damián耸了耸肩。
Damián：（无所谓）没事，跟我无关。
Damián: (indifferently) That has nothing to do with me.
Damián（VO）：他们是想迷晕我，然后让我跟Eloísa在一起，和Isadora离婚。我才是受害者。
Damián (VO): They planned to knock me out, put me beside Eloísa, and make Isadora divorce me. I was the one they targeted.
△Damián冷哼一声。
Damián（VO）：那我就让你们在一起，也算是做了好事一桩。
Damián (VO): So I put the two schemers together instead. You could say I did them a favor.
36-2日 内 单人豪华病房
人物：Julián、Alberto、管家
△Julián躺在病床上，管家冷着脸站在一旁。
△Julián睁开眼睛，猛地坐起身。
Julián：（惊）等等？发生什么了？
Julián: (startled) Wait. What happened?
管家：Julián少爷，全世界的人都看到那个叫Eloísa Roldán的女人和你一起衣衫不整地出现在展示台上。Salazar先生让我转告你，准备好和这个女人结婚吧。
Butler: Señor Julián, the entire world saw you and Eloísa Roldán half-dressed on the display platform. Señor Salazar says you should prepare to marry her.
△Julián愣在原地，表情僵住。
Julián： （抗拒）不！我是被陷害的！我要和Isadora结婚！和Elorza家族的女人结婚！Eloísa她根本不配！
Julián: (resisting) No! I was set up! I am supposed to marry Isadora--an Elorza! Eloísa is beneath me!
△Julián脸色一白，力竭地倒了下去。
△Julián眼神愤恨，咬牙切齿。
Julián：（怒）Damián！都是你因为你！我才不得不跟Eloísa结婚！我不会放过你的！
Julián: (furious) Damián! This is all your fault! Because of you, I have to marry Eloísa! I will make you pay!
36-3日 内 普通病房
人物：Eloísa、Margarita
△Eloísa坐在病床上。
△Margarita给Eloísa削着水果，贴心地喂到Eloísa嘴边。
Margarita：（喜）好女儿，我让你出点手段，没想到你手段这么高明。Salazar家的意思是找一天，你和Julián结婚。
Margarita: (delighted) My clever girl. I told you to use a few tricks, but you outdid yourself. The Salazars want you and Julián to marry soon.
△Eloísa微微蹙眉，有些不可置信。
Eloísa：你是说，我要和Julián结婚了？可……
Eloísa: You mean I am going to marry Julián? But...
△Margarita笑着打断Eloísa，表情向往。
Margarita：女儿，这是好事。只要你和Julián结婚，咱们家也就飞黄腾达了。
Margarita: Sweetheart, this is a good thing. Once you marry Julián, our whole family will be set for life.
△Eloísa担忧地低下了头，表情纠结。
Eloísa（VO）：Julián的意思是他根本不想娶我，真正对我好的好像只有Damián。现在这个结果真的是我想要的吗？
Eloísa (VO): Julián made it clear he never wanted to marry me. The only man who ever truly cared for me was Damián. Is this really what I want?
△Eloísa摇了摇头，坚定目光。
Eloísa（VO）：Damián对我好又怎么样，还不是穷鬼一个！只有Julián才是我真正应该嫁的人！
Eloísa (VO): So what if Damián treated me well? He is still poor! Julián is the man I should marry.
△Margarita皱眉看着Eloísa。
Margarita：Eloísa，你在想什么？
Margarita: Eloísa, what are you thinking about?
△Eloísa笑了一下。
Eloísa：没什么，妈，我很开心。
Eloísa: Nothing, Mom. I am happy.

第三十七集
37-1日 内 会议室
人物：Alberto、管家
△Alberto表情凝重地站在落地窗前，不断拨动着手中的祖母绿戒指。
△管家推门走了进来，恭敬地走到Alberto身边。
管家：先生，Julián已经醒了。
Butler: Sir, Julián is awake.
△Alberto眯了眯眼，眼神狠戾。
Alberto：这个不成器的Julián，就让他跟那个女人结婚吧。这几天找个时机把他手里的家族核心产业都收回来。
Alberto: Let that good-for-nothing marry that woman. Over the next few days, find the right opportunity to take the family’s core businesses back from him.
管家：（点头）是。
Butler: (nods) Yes, sir.
Alberto：（叹了口气，眼神怅然）对了，要加速寻找我的亲孙子了，毕竟，Julián那个废物不堪大用，Salazar家族还需要人来继承。
Alberto (sighs wistfully): We need to speed up the search for my real grandson. After all, that good-for-nothing Julián is clearly unfit for the job. The Salazar family still needs an heir.
△管家一愣，点了点头。
管家：明白。
Butler: Understood.
△管家递上一份文件。
管家：先生，这是Damián的资料。根据我们找到的消息，他是单亲家庭长大，没什么背景，还是个孤儿。
Butler: Sir, this is Damián's file. According to what we've found, he was raised by a single father, has no known family background, and is an orphan.
△Alberto接过文件，眉头一皱。
Alberto：孤儿？
Alberto: An orphan?
管家：（点头）是的，他父亲在路边发现了他，然后把他带回家抚养。
Butler: (Nods) Yes. His father found him by the roadside and took him home to raise him.
△Alberto皱眉沉思。
Alberto：（喃喃）那孩子的岁数似乎也差不多。
Alberto: (Murmurs) The boy seems to be about the right age, too.
△管家面色一变。
管家：先生的意思是……
Butler: Sir, are you thinking...?
Alberto：（眼神深邃）你想办法搞到Damián的DNA。
Alberto: (His gaze darkens) Find a way to get Damián's DNA.
管家：是的，先生。
Butler: Yes, sir.
37-2日 内 信息部办公室
人物：Damián、Eloísa、员工
△员工们坐在工位上窃窃私语。
员工一：（咋舌）我有个姐姐在Salazar公司上班，听说Salazar家族准备让Eloísa和Julián结婚，Salazar老先生都快被气死了。
Employee 1: My sister works for the Salazars. She says the family is making Eloísa marry Julián, and Mr. Salazar is furious about it.
员工二：Eloísa要飞上枝头变凤凰，变成真少奶奶了，羡慕啊！
Employee 2: Eloísa is about to marry into one of the richest families in this city. I am so jealous!
△Eloísa趾高气昂地走了进来，众人齐齐看向Eloísa。
Eloísa（VO）：（得意）Damián，让你这段时间看不上我，我马上就要嫁入豪门了，你就后悔去吧！
Eloísa (VO): (smugly) You have looked down on me long enough, Damián. I am about to become a Salazar. Now you will regret losing me!
△Damián打开办公室的大门，皱眉扫视一圈。
Damián：都愣着干嘛呢？怎么不工作？
Damián: Why is everyone standing around? Get back to work.
△Eloísa得意地走到Damián面前。
Eloísa：Damián，我要结婚了。
Eloísa: Damián, I am getting married.
△Damián面无表情地看着Eloísa。
Damián：哦，恭喜啊，Julián嘛，我知道。
Damián: Oh. Congratulations. It is Julián right? I've heard about it.
△其他员工们也纷纷附和。
员工一：是啊是啊，恭喜，你们什么时候结婚啊？
Employee 1: Yes, congratulations! When is the wedding?
△Damián低着头看着手机处理工作，毫不在乎。
△Eloísa咬着牙，忿忿地看着Damián。
Eloísa：（怒）Damián！你不是说你结婚了吗，我希望我结婚的时候你能把她带来，让我看看你说的究竟是真是假！
Eloísa: (angrily) Damián! You said you were married too. Bring your wife to my wedding so I can see whether she is even real!
△Damián不耐烦地看着Eloísa。
Damián：（皱眉）都说了恭喜，你们最好永远在一起，行吧。
Damián: (frowning) I already congratulated you. May the two of you stay together forever.
△Eloísa咬着牙，不甘心地看着Damián。
Eloísa（VO）：Damián，在你眼里我们之前一年多的感情到底算什么！
Eloísa (VO): Damián, did our year together mean nothing to you?
△Eloísa攥紧拳头。
Eloísa：（冷哼）Damián，你已经失去我了，你就用你的下半生后悔去吧！
Eloísa: (snorts) You have lost me, Damián. Spend the rest of your life regretting it!
△Eloísa的手机响起，是Julián的电话，Eloísa接起。
Julián（OS）：（冷漠）你现在来Salazar家庄园。
Julián (OS): (coldly) Come to the Salazar estate now.
△Eloísa点头，声音甜美。
Eloísa：好的，亲爱的，我现在就来。
Eloísa: Of course, darling. I am on my way.
△Eloísa挂了电话，挑衅似的看了眼Damián。
Eloísa（VO）：（冷哼）我要嫁入豪门了，而你永远只能是个穷光蛋！
Eloísa (VO): (scoffs) I am marrying into the Salazar family, while you will always be a nobody!
△Eloísa转身离开。

第三十八集
38-1日 内 Elorza庄园
人物：Damián、Beatriz、Samanta、Fernando
△客厅里，Fernando和Beatriz坐在真皮沙发上一起看着报纸，两人说说笑笑。
△Damián走了进来，看到二人打招呼。
Damián：爸，妈，我先回来了，Isadora晚上有应酬，我一会再去接她。
Damián: Dad, Mom, I am home. Isadora has a business dinner tonight, so I will pick her up later.
△Beatriz笑着招呼Damián坐在旁边。
Beatriz：Damián回来了，过来坐。
Beatriz: Welcome home, Damián. Come sit with us.
△Fernando凑到Damián身边，表情戏谑。
Fernando：听说那个Eloísa又闹笑话了？
Fernando: I heard Eloísa made a fool of herself again.
△Damián挑了挑眉。
Damián：可不是吗，其实Eloísa和Julián早就在一起了。
Damián: She certainly did. Actually, Eloísa and Julián have already been together for a while.
△Beatriz叹了口气。
Beatriz：没想到Alberto Salazar精明了一辈子，老了竟然有个这样的孙子。现在Salazar家已经开始匆忙准备婚礼了。
Beatriz: Alberto Salazar has been shrewd all his life. Who would have thought his grandson would cause a scandal like this? The Salazars are already rushing to arrange the wedding.
△Samanta拿着请柬走了进来。
Samanta：妈，Salazar家的请柬送过来了，好简陋啊。
Samanta: Mom, the Salazars sent an invitation. It looks so cheap.
Beatriz：Salazar家没请多少人，这婚礼也只是喂了堵住别人的嘴巴，想让这件事赶紧翻篇。
Beatriz: They invited very few people. The wedding is only meant to contain the scandal and put the whole affair behind them.
△Beatriz随手接过请柬，扔到茶几上，没人再注意。
38-2日 内 Salazar家Julián卧室
人物：Eloísa、Julián
△Julián脸色阴沉地看着窗外，Eloísa一脸欣喜地推门走了进来。
Eloísa：（开心）亲爱的，爷爷说让我们尽快办婚礼！你开心吗？
Eloísa: (happily) Darling, Grandpa wants us to marry as soon as possible! Are you excited?
△Julián阴沉着脸毫不留情地打了Eloísa一巴掌。
△Eloísa一脸懵地捂着脸。
Julián：（怒）Eloísa，你以为是爷爷喜欢你吗？放屁！那是为了堵住别人的嘴！这次婚礼的规模小得可怜，而且他已经开始收回我手中的核心项目了！
Julián: (furious) You think Grandpa likes you? Fucking moron! He only wants to silence the gossip! This wedding is embarrassingly small, and he has already started taking the family's key business away from me!
△Eloísa震惊地看着暴怒的Julián。
△Julián开始疯狂摔东西。
Julián：废物！你这个废物！你给我滚出去！
Julián: Useless! You are completely useless! Get the fuck out!
△Eloísa红着眼睛捂着脸跑了出去。
38-3日 外 Salazar家后花园
人物：Eloísa
△Eloísa躲在花园的大树下，打着电话蹲着掩面哭泣。
Eloísa：怎么办，我好后悔没有珍惜Damián啊！Julián他只当我是绊脚石，对待我像对待垃圾一样！
Eloísa: What am I going to do? I regret not valuing Damián. Julián treats me like a burden--like garbage!
△女性朋友的声音从电话那头传出。
女性朋友（OS）：Eloísa，你不能这么想。Damián只是个小职员，可Julián可是贵族后代。就算Julián再不喜欢你，你也是Salazar家的一员了，以后你的零花钱肯定少不了！
Female friend: Eloísa, don't think that way. Damián is only a junior employee, while Julián comes from the old-money. Even if he does not love you, you will still be a Salazar. You will never have to worry about money again!
△Eloísa渐渐停止哭泣。
女性朋友（OS）：你这生活是多少人梦寐以求的啊！而且，Julián可是Salazar家的继承人，Salazar先生气一段时间也就不气了。
Friend: Most people would dream of having your life. Besides, Julián is still the Salazar heir. Mr. Salazar will calm down eventually.
Eloísa：（擦眼泪）你说得对，起码我变成Salazar家的一员了。而且，这一切都怪Damián，要不是他不坚定，我也不会这样！都怪他！
Eloísa: (wipes her tears) You are right. At least I will be part of the Salazar family. And this is all Damián's fault! If he had fought harder for me, none of this would have happened!

第三十九集
39-1夜 外 饭店门口
人物：Damián
△Damián靠在跑车旁，时不时看手表上的时间，已经晚上十点了。
Damián：（皱眉）这么晚了，怎么还没应酬完？
Damián: (frowning) It's so late. Why is she not home yet?
△Damián走进了饭店。
39-2夜 内 饭店包厢
人物：Damián、Isadora、Mirella、合作商
△Isadora红着脸有些醉，但强撑着坐在中间，旁边都是大腹便便的中年西装男人。
△离Isadora最近的合作商拿起酒杯给Isadora倒了一杯。
合作商：（笑）Isadora，今天你们招商会上出的丑闻可是连带着影响了我们公司的股票，难道，你不该表示表示？
Business Partner: (smiling) Isadora, the scandal at today's investor event hurt our stock price. Don't you think you owe us something?
△Mirella一脸担忧地伸手要拿酒杯。
Mirella：她有些喝多了，我来替她喝吧。
Mirella: She has already had too much. I will drink on her behalf.
△合作商不满地隔开Mirella。
合作商：（皱眉）你算什么东西？让你们老板喝！让我们亏钱了，这是她应该做的！
Business Partner: (frowning) Who do you think you are? Your boss is drinking. We lost money because of her, so she owes us!
△Isadora轻轻拉了拉Mirella。
Isadora：（摇头）没事，我还可以。
Isadora: (shakes her head) It's alright. I can keep going.
△Isadora拿起酒杯刚要喝，Damián突然出现抓住Isadora的手，夺过酒杯一饮而尽。
△Isadora震惊地看着Damián。
Isadora：Damián？你？
Isadora: Damián? What are you doing here?
△Damián拿起红酒又给自己倒了满满一杯，冲着合作商们举起酒杯。
Damián：我是Isadora的老公，大家想喝我就陪大家喝！这一杯我干了！
Damián: I'm Isadora's husband. If you gentlemen want to drink, I'll drink with you. Bottoms up!
△Damián再次将酒一饮而尽。
△合作商们震惊地看着Damián，面色稍微缓和。
合作商：（轻咳）我也不是为难你们，只是今天的事情确实对我们造成了损失。
Business Partner: (clears his throat) We are not trying to make this difficult. What happened today genuinely cost us money.
△Damián擦了擦嘴，看向合作商。
Damián：这件事确实是我们疏忽了，但现在的事态已经稳住了，对各位造成的损失我们会尽快弥补。
Damián: We did make a mistake, but the situation is under control. We will compensate you for any verified losses as quickly as possible.
△Isadora护住Damián。
Isadora：不好意思啊各位，我老公性子急。但他说的也正是我想说的。
Isadora: Please excuse my husband. He can be impulsive, but he speaks for me as well.
△合作们面面相觑，纷纷起身。
合作商：行，大家合作了这么久，我们也只是有气，没真的想对你们怎么样。Isadora女士，既然你老公都来了那我们也先走了。
Business Partner: All right. We have worked together for years. We were angry, but we never meant to threaten you. Ms. Elorza, since your husband is here, we will leave you two alone.
△合作商们纷纷离开。
△Damián松了口气，抓起桌上的水喝了下去。
Damián：这是什么酒，怎么这么烈。
Damián: What kind of wine is this? So strong.
△Isadora卸了力，瘫坐在椅子上，眼神迷离地看着Damián。
Isadora：（语气不自觉撒娇）你怎么来了？
Isadora: (unconsciously pouting) What are you doing here?
Damián：我来接你，等了半天还不见你出来，我就进来看看。
Damián: I came to pick you up. It's getting late and you still didn't leave, so I came inside to check on you.
△Damián有些生气。
Damián：（埋怨）这帮人跟你一个助理撒什么气？Samanta也是的，这种场合她这个总裁怎么不出现？
Damián: (complaining) Why are those men taking their anger out on an assistant? And where is Samanta? If she is the CEO, she should be handling meetings like this.
△Mirella看了看两人，悄悄退了出去。
△Damián担忧地看着Isadora。
Damián：你喝多了？感觉怎么样？
Damián: You have had too much to drink. How do you feel?
△Damián靠近Isadora。
△Isadora抬手搂住Damián的脖子，直接亲了上去。

第四十集
40-1夜 内 饭店包厢
人物：Damián、Isadora
△Isadora松开Damián，眼神迷离，面色泛红，轻轻喘着气。
△Damián压抑着，两人头顶头，鼻尖轻磨，Damián盯着Isadora，舔了下嘴唇。
Damián：（轻声）Isadora，我带你回家好不好？
Damián: (softly) Isadora, may I take you home?
△Isadora点了点头。
△Damián直起身子，单手公主抱起Isadora，另一只手拿起Isadora的包离开了包房。
40-2日 内 信息安全部
人物：Damián、Eloísa、员工
△Eloísa站在员工堆里，手里拿着请柬给每个人分发。
员工一：（恭维）Eloísa，你的命真好，嫁到 Salazar家做富太太了。我们可羡慕死了！
Employee 1: (flattering her) Eloísa, you're so lucky. You're marrying a Salazar and joining one of the city's wealthiest families. We're so jealous!
△Eloísa开心一笑。
Eloísa：Salazar家太喜欢我了，着急让我和Julián结婚，所以就定在今晚了。你们记得一定要来啊！
Eloísa: The Salazars adore me and could not wait for Julián to marry me, so the wedding is tonight. You guys have to come!
△Damián走了进来，众人瞬间噤声。
△Eloísa傲慢地走向Damián，嫌弃地打量Damián，递出一张请柬。
Eloísa：Damián，你来我们婚礼吧。毕竟我们之前好过，现在我和豪门结婚了，以后就不是一个世界的人了。
Eloísa: Damián, come to our wedding. We were together once, but now I am marrying into one of the city's richest families. We belong to different worlds from now on.
△Eloísa挑衅一笑。
Eloísa：来吃个饭吧，就当是给你的青春留下点念想。
Eloísa: Come have dinner—for old times' sake.
Eloísa（VO）：我就不信你还能那么淡定！
Eloísa (VO): Let us see how long you can pretend not to care!
△Damián淡淡地扫了眼请柬。
Damián：（无动于衷）我早就说过，我祝福你们，你们最好永远在一起。
Damián: (unmoved) I already wished you well. I sincerely hope the two of you stay together forever.
△Eloísa震惊地看着Damián。
Eloísa：（咬牙）你！（冷哼）好！记得把你老婆带上！你最好别临时找一个！
Eloísa: (through gritted teeth) You--! (scoffs) Fine! Bring your wife, and do not hire some stranger at the last minute!
△Damián无视Eloísa径直走进办公室。
△Eloísa盯着Damián的背影，咬牙跺脚。
Eloísa：（怒）我倒要看看你能找个什么样的老婆！
Eloísa: (furious) I cannot wait to see what kind of wife you managed to find!
40-3日 内 部长办公室
人物：Damián、员工
△Damián坐在椅子上，腿翘在桌子上，打着游戏。
Damián：（咋舌）这游戏也太没挑战了，充点钱这么快就通关了。
Damián: This game has no challenge at all. A few in-app purchases and I have already beaten it.
△办公室的门被敲响。
△Damián放下腿，坐直身体，把手机放到一边。
Damián：请进。
Damián: Come in.
△员工推门走了进来。
员工：Damián先生，我们要出发了，你跟我们一起走吗？
Employee: Mr. Cortés, we are leaving for the wedding. Are you coming with us?
△Damián摇了摇头。
Damián：你们先去吧，我还要等我老婆。
Damián: Go ahead. I am waiting for my wife.
△员工点了点头，退出了办公室。
△Damián手机亮起，是Isadora打来的电话。
Isadora（VO）：下来吧，我在车库等你。
Isadora: Come downstairs. I am waiting in the garage.
Damián：（笑）好。
Damián: (smiles) On my way.
△Damián拿起外套笑着离开办公室。
40-4日 内 地下车库
人物：Damián、Isadora
△Damián走到地下车库，看到一辆红色的保时捷停在中央。
△Isadora穿着红色礼裙靠在车旁，披散着卷发，气质慵懒。
△Damián星星眼的看着Isadora，缓缓走进。
△Isadora看向Damián，勾起嘴角，表情妩媚。
Isadora：Damián，来了？
Isadora: Damián, ready?

第四十一集
41-1日 内 地下车库
人物：Damián、Isadora
△Damián绕着Isadora转圈，表情惊艳。
Damián：（赞叹）怎么这么隆重？
Damián: (admiringly) Why are you dressed so elegantly?
△Isadora替Damián拉开车门。
Isadora：去你前女友的婚礼不隆重点怎么行呢。上车吧，你的衣服在车上呢。
Isadora: We are going to your ex-girlfriend's wedding. Of course I had to make an entrance. Get in, your suit is in the car.
Damián：（笑）好。
Damián: (smiles) All right.
41-2夜 内 Salazar家礼堂
人物：Jairo、Margarita、宾客
△礼堂里众人穿着礼服站在礼堂里，礼堂放着优雅的音乐。
△礼堂两侧放着两张贯穿礼堂，上面摆着各种各样的食物，宾客拿着盘子和酒来回穿梭。
△Margarita浑身堆满了珠宝，神情高傲。
△Jairo穿着燕尾服，胸口别着闪耀的胸针。
△几个宾客站在一旁看着二人窃窃私语。
宾客一：（惊讶）这Roldán家走了什么狗屎运啊，竟然能跟Salazar家族结婚。
Guest 1: (astonished) How did the Roldáns get lucky enough to marry into the Salazar family?
宾客二：就是，怪不得看不上Eloísa之前的那个男朋友，原来是Eloísa实力强悍啊。
Guest 2: No wonder Eloísa dumped her old boyfriend. She certainly knew how to trade up.
△Margarita和Jairo一脸骄傲，挺直了胸膛。
Jairo：（得意）老婆，我们的人生来了啊。
Jairo: (smugly) Darling, our new life is finally beginning.
△Margarita畅想着。
Margarita：（期待）你说，Eloísa跟Julián结婚，Salazar家能给我们多少钱啊？会不会直接找个庄园给我们？
Margarita: (dreamily) How much do you think the Salazars will give us after Eloísa marries Julián? Maybe they will even buy us an estate.
△Jairo冷哼一声，不屑的眼神扫过一些宾客。
Jairo：反正我知道，今晚是我们跟这些穷亲戚交流的最后时刻，以后就不用再搭理他们了。
Jairo: One thing is certain: tonight is the last time we will ever have to mingle with these poor relatives.
△Margarita贪婪地盯着装食物的银盘子。
Margarita：先别说这些了，我看那些盘子像是银的，咱们悄悄藏着点在衣服里，待会带走。
Margarita: Forget that for now. Those serving trays look like real silver. Let us hide a few under our clothes and take them home.
△Jairo点了点头，拉着Margarita，两人鬼鬼祟祟地往餐桌旁凑。
41-3夜 内 书房
人物：Alberto、管家
△Alberto阴沉着脸坐在椅子上。
△管家恭敬地走了进来。
管家：先生，这边准备得差不多了，就是Roldán家的人手脚不干净，拿走了好多纯银餐盘，害得我们的人手忙脚乱的。
Butler: Sir, everything is almost ready. The Roldáns have sticky fingers, though. They have taken so many silver trays that the staff can barely keep up.
△Alberto表情无语。
Alberto：（皱眉不耐）随他们去！看到这帮人我就心烦，婚礼赶紧结束，然后把Julián给我赶出去！
Alberto: (irritated) Let them! I am sick of looking at those people. Finish this wedding and throw Julián out!
△Alberto眯了眯眼睛。
Alberto：（冷哼）他身为养子养在Salazar家这么多年，一直按照继承人的要求培养他。可他呢？除了花天酒地还会干什么？
Alberto: (scoffs) We raised him as a Salazar and trained him to be the heir. And what has he done besides party and chase women? 
△Alberto越说越生气。
Alberto：我们Salazar家不欠他什么！
Alberto: The Salazar family owes him nothing!
△管家鞠躬，语气惶恐。
管家：是，先生。
Butler: Yes, sir.

第四十二集
42-1夜 内 化妆间
人物：Eloísa、Margarita、化妆师
△Eloísa穿着不合身的婚纱，兴高采烈地坐在化妆镜前，看着镜子中的自己。
△化妆师在一旁收拾东西。
△Margarita咧着嘴推开化妆间的门。
Margarita：Eloísa啊，你看我刚才在大厅里拿了这么多银盘子！
Margarita: Eloísa, look at all the silver trays I took from the hall!
△Margarita向Eloísa展示包里的银盘子。
Eloísa：（皱眉）妈，你拿这些东西的时候小心点，别被人看到了！
Eloísa: (frowning) Mom, be careful. Do not let anyone see you!
△Margarita毫不在乎地摆了摆手。
Margarita：（不在乎）谁敢管我？
Margarita: (dismissively) Who would dare stop me?
△Eloísa拿起桌上的珍珠项链戴在脖子上。
Eloísa：妈，Damián来了吗？
Eloísa: Mom, is Damián here yet?
△Margarita挑拣着桌上的珠宝往自己手上戴。
Margarita：（漫不经心）你还想着他？这可不行，你就给我乖乖当新娘，和Julián结婚！
Margarita: (casually) You are still thinking about him? Stop it. Be a good bride and marry Julián.
△Eloísa摇了摇头。
Eloísa：我只是鼓励鼓励他，虽然他这辈子也找不到像我一样的美女，但也不能气馁，要加油。
Eloísa: I only want to encourage him. He will never find another woman as beautiful as me, but he should not give up on life.
△Eloísa挑了挑眉，欣赏地看着镜子里的自己。
Eloísa：（自信）说不定他还想抢婚呢。
Eloísa: (confidently) He may even try to stop the wedding.
△化妆师一脸嫌弃地看了眼Eloísa和Margarita。
化妆师（VO）：她们难道不知道Salazar家定的是最便宜的套餐吗？还在这做梦呢，真是天真。
Makeup Artist (VO): Do they not realize the Salazars booked the cheapest wedding package? They are still daydreaming. How pathetic.
△化妆师转身离开。
42-2夜 内 化妆间
人物：Julián、管家
△Julián坐在昏暗的化妆间里喝着酒，领带松松垮垮地挂在脖子上，地上摆着不少空酒瓶。
Julián：（苦闷）呵呵，我完了，我的人生完了，Eloísa？呵呵。
Julián: (bitterly) It is over. My life is over. And I have to marry Eloísa. What a joke.
△管家推开门，恭敬地走了进来，脸上闪过嫌弃。
管家：少爷，已经准备好了，差不多可以开始了。
Butler: Señor Julián, everything is ready. The ceremony is about to begin.
△Julián猛地把酒瓶扔了出去，冲着管家怒吼。
Julián：（愤怒）你那是什么态度？嘲讽我吗？
Julián: (furious) What is that tone? Are you mocking me?
△管家不动声色地向后撤了一步。
管家：（淡淡）Julián少爷，时间到了，你也不希望先生久等吧？
Butler: (flatly) It's time, Señor Julián. You wouldn't want to keep Señor Salazar waiting.
△Julián眼睛一亮。
Julián（VO）：对了，我再去求求爷爷，就算我不是亲生的，看在这么多年相处的份上他也会放过我的对吧？
Julián (VO): That is right. I can beg Grandpa again. I may not be his biological grandson, but after all these years, he cannot cast me aside... can he?
△Julián死死抓着管家的手臂。
Julián：（咬牙）带我出去！
Julián: (through gritted teeth) Take me out there!
42-3夜 内 礼堂
人物：Damián、Isadora、Julián、Eloísa、Beatriz、Samanta、宾客
△Julián和Eloísa手挽着手彩排，Julián表情不耐烦，Eloísa表情期待。
△宾客们在下面围观。
宾客一：（不屑）这个Julián不知道检点一点，现在好了，直接拿起石头砸自己的脚。
Guest 1: (disdainfully) Julián never knew how to behave, and now his own recklessness has ruined him.
宾客二：（冷哼）就是。听说这个新娘也不是什么善茬，Julián这下彻底完了。Salazar家这一代的继承人肯定要换了。
Guest 2: Exactly. I hear the bride is no saint either. Julián is finished. The Salazars will have to choose a new heir.
△Eloísa满心欢喜地靠近Julián，Julián嫌弃地后撤了一步。
管家（OS）：Elorza家到！
Butler (OS): The Elorza family has arrived!
△宾客们纷纷看向门口。
△穿着淡雅白裙的Beatriz带着Isadora和服装休闲的Samanta走了进来，身着高定西装的Damián站在Isadora身旁，Isadora微笑着挽着Damián。
△Eloísa抬头看去，表情僵在脸上。
Eloísa：（不可置信）Damián？
Eloísa: (in disbelief) Damián?

第四十三集
43-1夜 内 礼堂
人物：Damián、Isadora、Julián、Eloísa、Beatriz、Samanta、宾客
△Eloísa震惊地看着Damián和Isadora。
Eloísa：（震惊）他怎么跟Elorza家的人一起来了？
Eloísa: (shocked) Why did he come with the Elorzas?
△Eloísa脸色难看，不断说服自己。
Eloísa：（喃喃）一定是路上遇到的，对！肯定是这样的！
Eloísa: (muttering) They must have run into each other on the way. Yes, that has to be it!
△宾客们看着Damián窃窃私语。
宾客一：那是Elorza家的大女儿的老公？长相和气质都很不错啊。
Guest 1: Is that the husband of the eldest Elorza daughter? He is handsome and carries himself well.
宾客二：是啊，和Isadora走在一起完全不逊色。
Guest 2: He certainly looks like he belongs beside Isadora.
△Eloísa目瞪口呆地站在原地。
Eloísa：（皱眉）什么？这是什么意思？
Eloísa: (frowning) What? What are they talking about?
△Julián看着Eloísa冷哼一声，表情讥讽。
Julián：搞了半天，你还不知道？Damián是你们公司老板的老公。
Julián: You still do not know? Damián is married to your company's CEO.
△Eloísa震惊地站在原地。
△Julián讥讽地笑了出来。
Julián：你以为人家在骗你？太天真了，Eloísa！人家早就结婚了！
Julián: You thought he was lying to you? How naive, Eloísa. He has been married this whole time!
Eloísa：（不可置信）不，这不可能！他肯定在骗我！
Eloísa: (in disbelief) No, that is impossible! He must be lying!
△站在Eloísa身后的Margarita也目瞪口呆。
Margarita：（震惊）那个废物竟然能和这么厉害的人家结婚？
Margarita: (shocked) That loser married into a family this powerful?
△Julián看着Eloísa和Margarita，冷笑。
Julián：怎么了Eloísa？你现在后悔和我在一起了吗？
Julián: What is wrong, Eloísa? Regretting your affair with me now?
△Eloísa惨白着脸，咬着嘴唇，死死盯着Damián，攥紧了拳头。
Eloísa（VO）：Damián，你凭什么过得比我好？你就不能一直不幸吗！
Eloísa (VO): How dare you live better than me, Damián? Why could you not stay miserable forever?
△一帮员工围到Damián面前，表情震惊。
员工一：Damián先生，没想到你竟然是总裁的老公！
Employee 1: Mr. Cortés, I had no idea you were the CEO's husband!
△Damián一脸疑惑。
Damián：总裁？
Damián: The CEO?
△Damián震惊地指了指Isadora。
△员工缓缓点了点头，表情疑惑。
△Damián转头看向Isadora，表情诧异。
Damián（VO）：（呆）我去，我老婆竟然是千亿总裁！
Damián (VO): No way. My wife runs a company worth a hundred billion pesos!
△Isadora有些尴尬，羞愧地抓住Damián的手臂。
Isadora：Damián，我其实一直想告诉你的，但总被打断……你，不会要跟我离婚吧？
Isadora: Damián, I have tried to tell you so many times, but we were always interrupted. You are not going to divorce me, are you?
△Samanta表情有些不自在。
Samanta：你别生我姐的气，是我让她骗你，试探你的。
Samanta: Do not be angry with my sister. I asked her to hide the truth so I could test you.
△Damián震惊地站在原地。
Damián（VO）：我靠，千亿总裁！我要靠老婆发达了！
Damián (VO): My wife is a billionaire CEO! I hit the jackpot!
△Isadora看着不说话的Damián，表情担忧，甩了甩Damián的手臂。
Isadora：（语气哀求）亲爱的！你别生气了，你想要什么我都给你买还不行吗！
Isadora: (pleadingly) Darling, please do not be angry. I will buy you anything you want, all right?

第四十四集
44-1夜 内 礼堂
人物：Damián、Isadora、Julián、Eloísa、Beatriz、Samanta、管家、宾客
△Isadora抱着Damián的手臂撒娇。
△一旁的人看着两人的互动悄悄移开了眼睛。
△Samanta双手抱在胸前，嫌弃地翻白眼。
Samanta：秀什么秀！
Samanta: Stop showing off!
△Beatriz笑着打断二人。
Beatriz：好了，今天是来参加Salazar家的婚礼的，你们就别喧宾夺主了。
Beatriz: Enough. We came for the Salazar wedding. Do not steal the bride and groom's spotlight.
△Damián回过神，嘿嘿一笑。
△戒指的虚影突然出现。
△戒指（OS）：（低沉）命运节点已记录。节点：Salazar家婚礼礼堂，命运馈赠——千杯不醉，启用后即无视酒精度数，永不会醉。
Ring (OS): (deep) Fate checkpoint registered. Location: the Salazar wedding hall. Reward: Alcohol Immunity. Once activated, no amount of alcohol can make you drunk.
△酒杯的虚影出现在Damián的头顶，杯中的酒缓缓倒出，酒水包裹着Damián，被Damián吸收。
Damián（VO）：（疑惑）千杯不醉？以后还能帮Isadora挡挡酒。
Damián (VO): Immunity to alcohol? At least I can drink in Isadora's place from now on.
△婚礼进行曲响起，众人纷纷坐下。
△Eloísa挽着Julián缓缓走过红毯，走到中央的台子旁。两人的表情都和难看，貌合神离。
△Eloísa的眼神一直瞟向台下的Damián。
△Damián坐在Isadora身旁，专心致志地吃着牛排。
△Samanta坐在Isadora另一侧，靠近Isadora，看了看Eloísa，又顺着Eloísa的眼神看向Damián，小声说话。
Samanta：（认真）姐，你看那Eloísa一直盯着Damián。他这么招女生喜欢你真能放心？不如我给他那个了！
Samanta: (seriously) Look, Isadora. Eloísa will not stop staring at Damián. Can you really trust a man who attracts this much attention? Maybe I should deal with him for you!
△Samanta一边说，一边用手比剪刀开合。
△Isadora按住Samanta的手，一脸无语。
Isadora：好了，他是我丈夫，你客气点！
Isadora: Enough. He is my husband, show him some respect!
△Samanta不甘心地瞪了眼Damián，正好和嘴里叼着牛排的Damián对视。
△Damián表情疑惑。
△Samanta翻了个白眼，移开视线。
△Julián带着Eloísa走到Damián身边，举起一旁的酒杯，冲着Damián，表情挑衅。
Julián：Damián，没想到你真的敢来，咱们喝一杯吧。
Julián: Damián, I am surprised you had the nerve to come. Let's have a drink.
△Eloísa看着Damián。
Eloísa：Damián，我也恭喜你。
Eloísa: Congratulations to you too, Damián.
△跟在Julián身后的管家出声提醒。
管家：Julián少爷，今天是你结婚的日子，喝太多不好。
Butler: Señor Julián, today's your wedding. You shouldn't drink too much.
△Julián没有理会管家，死死盯着Damián。
Julián：Damián，你真是废物，这么点酒都喝不了。
Julián: You are pathetic, Damián. You cannot even handle one glass.
△Damián冷哼一声，起身拿起酒杯看着Julián。
Damián：这么好的酒，我当然要喝。
Damián: This is excellent wine. Of course I will finish it.
△Isadora担忧地扯了扯Damián。
Isadora：这酒度数很高的，你少喝点。
Isadora: That is very strong. Take it easy.
△Damián拍了拍Isadora的手示意她放心，看向Julián，表情挑衅。
Damián：不过，Julián，你别晚上在床上不行的时候赖现在喝酒喝多了。
Damián: Just do not blame the alcohol tonight when you cannot satisfy your bride, Julián.
△Julián愤怒。
Julián：Damián!你说什么！
Julián: Damián! What did you just say?

第四十五集
45-1夜 内 礼堂
人物：Damián、Isadora、Julián、Eloísa、Beatriz、Alberto、宾客
△Damián表情不变，依旧挑衅地看着Julián。
Julián：（怒）Damián！你就是个得志的小人罢了！我看你就是不敢跟我比！
Julián: (furious) You are nothing but an opportunist, Damián! You are too scared to compete with me!
△Damián笑了笑。
Damián（VO）：正好试一试我新获得的能力。
Damián (VO): Perfect. Time to test my new ability.
Damián：好啊，那就比一下。
Damián: All right. I accept your challenge.
△Damián看向旁边的众人，提高音量。
Damián：大家看好了！新郎要跟我比酒量了！
Damián: Everyone, pay attention! The groom has challenged me to a drinking contest!
△宾客们窃窃私语。
宾客一：（笑声）这俩人看着有仇啊！
Guest 1: (laughs) Those two clearly hate each other.
宾客二：对啊，要不是这人突然出现，跟Isadora结婚的可就是Julián了，哪里轮得到现在的新娘。
Guest 2: Of course they do. If Damián had not appeared and married Isadora, Julián would be marrying her instead of this bride.
△Eloísa脸色难看地瞪了眼说闲话的宾客。
Eloísa（VO）：我看这帮人就是嫉妒我！管他们怎么说，最后变成豪门的还不是我！
Eloísa (VO): They are all jealous of me! Say whatever you want--I am still the one marrying into the Salazar family!
△Julián挥了挥手，一个服务员拿着一大瓶红酒走了过来。
△Isadora担忧地跟Damián嘱咐。
Isadora：你酒量一般，少喝点，这可是烈酒！
Isadora: You are not a strong drinker. Slow down, this is hard liquor!
△Damián摇了摇头。
Damián：放心吧，我有分寸。
Damián: Do not worry. I know my limits.
△Julián不屑。
Julián：Isadora，你就这么维护他？不过是点酒而已，这都喝不了，真是废物一个。
Julián: You're really protective, aren't you, Isadora? This is merely alcohol. What kind of man cannot handle a few drinks?
△Damián眯了眯眼，似笑非笑地看着Julián。
Damián：Julián，既然你都这么说了，那我们只喝酒也太没意思了，不如赌点什么。
Damián: If that is how you feel, pure drinking would be boring. Let us put something on the line.
Julián：（猖狂）赌什么？交换女人吗？哈哈哈哈！
Julián: (wildly) What should we bet--our women? Hahaha!
△众人一片哗然，表情震惊。
宾客一：这Julián怎么回事？竟然说这种话！
Guest 1: What is wrong with Julián? How can he say something like that?
宾客二：这完全是不顾Salazar家家族的面子，和Salazar先生的尊严啊！甚至连新娘和Isadora都侮辱了！真是人渣！
Guest 2: He has humiliated the Salazar name, insulted Mr. Salazar, and degraded both the bride and Isadora. What a disgrace!
△Alberto坐在一旁，冷哼一声，脸色阴沉。
Alberto：这个蠢货！
Alberto: That idiot!
△Eloísa脸色难看地看着Julián，伸手要抓Julián。
Eloísa：Julián!你说什么呢？
Eloísa: Julián! What are you saying?
△Julián愤怒地甩开Eloísa。
Julián：贱人！别烦我！
Julián: Shut up, you bitch!
△Eloísa表情尴尬。
△Beatriz不满地皱眉。
Beatriz：这就是Salazar家的家教吗？
Beatriz: Is this how the Salazar family raises its children?
△Julián恶狠狠的盯着Damián。
Julián：（挑衅）Damián，你不会是怕了吧？
Julián: (tauntingly) What is wrong, Damián? Are you scared?
Damián：（冷哼）别瞎扯了，就你这女人送人都没人要。
Damián: (scoffs) Stop bluffing. No one would take your bride even if you gave her away.
△Eloísa死死咬住嘴唇。
Damián：不如这样，如果你输了，让我打一拳如何？
Damián: How about this? If you lose, I get to punch you once.
△Eloísa着急。
Eloísa：Damián，你！
Eloísa: Damián, you--!
△Julián抬手拦住Eloísa。
Julián：好！但如果我赢了，我要你下跪亲我的鞋尖！
Julián: Fine! But if I win, you kneel and kiss the tip of my shoe!
△Damián勾起嘴角，表情不屑。
Damián：行啊，就这么说好了。
Damián: That's a deal.

第四十六集
46-1夜 内 礼堂
人物：Damián、Isadora、Julián、Eloísa、Beatriz、Alberto、Samanta、宾客
△Damián似笑非笑地拿起酒杯，倒满红酒，一口喝了下去，神情动作优雅，喝完后倒扣酒杯，展示一圈，笑着看向Julián。
Damián：（笑）Julián，到你了。
Damián: (smiles) Your turn, Julián.
△Julián看着Damián喝酒，着急地拿起一杯酒灌了下去，不少酒顺着嘴边撒了一身。
△Julián恶狠狠的盯着Damián，擦了擦嘴。
△Damián游刃有余的的接着拿起一杯酒，继续喝。
△Julián手忙脚乱，眼睛看着Damián，手上不停拿着酒杯往嘴里送，红酒撒了一身，非常狼狈。
△两人一杯接一杯，Damián依旧优雅，Julián逐渐喝多，酒越撒越多。
Julián（VO）：（不可置信）Damián的酒量怎么这么厉害？
Julián (VO): (in disbelief) How can Damián hold his liquor this well?
△Isadora担忧地盯着Damián。
△Samanta靠近Isadora，小声说。
Samanta：（撇嘴）Damián为了维护你还真是拼了。
Samanta: (pouts) Damián is certainly going all out to defend you.
△没一会两瓶空的红酒瓶摆在桌上。
△Julián脸色泛红，脚步虚浮，眼神迷离，震惊地看着Damián，下一秒捂着嘴跑到墙角。
Julián（OS）：（呕吐）呕……
Julián: (vomits) Ugh...
△宾客们嫌弃地捂着鼻子纷纷远离Julián。
△Damián慢条斯理地喝完杯中酒。
Damián（VO）：多亏了戒指给我的千杯不醉，要不今晚我还真被Julián喝趴下了。
Damián (VO): Thank goodness the ring made me immune to alcohol. Otherwise, Julián might actually have drunk me under the table tonight.
△Julián擦了擦嘴，转过身恶狠狠地盯着Damián。
Julián：这个不算，再来……
Julián: That one does not count. Again...
△Damián大步上前，一把打上了Julián的脸。
△Julián重重地摔倒在地上。
△礼堂里一片寂静。
△Damián甩了甩手，表情戏谑。
Damián：手感不错啊，新郎的脸就是结实，打得我好爽啊！
Damián: That felt great. The groom has a solid jaw--very satisfying to punch!
△Eloísa愣在原地，反应过来之后冲到Damián面前。
Eloísa：（怒）Damián！你干什么？我知道你因为我和Julián结婚心里不舒服，但你也不能打我老公啊！
Eloísa: (furious) Damián! What are you doing? I know you are upset that I married Julián, but you cannot hit my husband!
△Eloísa瞪着眼睛。
Eloísa：难不成是你不死心，还想抢婚？我告诉你，省省吧！这是不可能的！爱是放手你不知道吗！
Eloísa: Do you still want me so badly that you came to stop the wedding? Get a clue! It will never happen! If you love me, you have to let me go!
△Isadora咬着牙扇了Eloísa一巴掌。
△Eloísa的脸被闪到一边，表情震惊。
△周围不断出现凉气倒吸的声音。
△Isadora惊讶地收回了自己的手，不好意思地朝周围笑了笑。
Isadora：不好意思啊各位，我刚刚实在是忍不住，这女人太贱了。
Isadora: Sorry, everyone. I couldn't help myself. This woman is unbearable.

第四十七集
47-1夜 内 礼堂
人物：Damián、Isadora、Julián、Eloísa、Beatriz、Alberto、Samanta、宾客
△Isadora揉了揉手，表情无奈。
Isadora：这女人平时就爱纠缠我老公不说，现在在自己的婚礼现场说出这种话，我实在忍不住了。
Isadora: She is constantly harassing my husband, and now she says this at her own wedding. I simply lost my patience.
△Eloísa一脸委屈地捂着脸看向坐在一旁的Alberto。
Eloísa：爷爷，你就眼睁睁看着他们这么欺负我和Julián吗？他们这是没把Salazar家放在眼里啊！
Eloísa: Grandpa, are you really going to stand there while they bully Julián and me? They are insulting the entire Salazar family!
△Alberto嫌恶地移开脸。
Alberto：（淡淡）是你们自己跟人家赌酒的，输了就要愿赌服输。
Alberto: (flatly) You challenged them to the drinking contest. If you lost, accept the consequences.
△Eloísa脸色难看，表情不可置信。
△宾客们面面相觑，气氛尴尬。
△Alberto瞥了眼倚在墙边，已经眼神迷离的Julián。
△管家立刻上前，架着Julián离开。
△Alberto起身。
Alberto：（严肃）大家继续吃饭吧。
Alberto: (sternly) Everyone, please continue with dinner.
△Eloísa不情愿地走到Alberto身边坐下。
△Damián和Isadora坐了下来。
△Isadora凑到Damián耳边。
Isadora：看来Salazar家是准备放弃Julián了。
Isadora: It looks like the Salazar family is ready to give up on Julián.
△Damián表情疑惑。
Damián：放弃Julián？这是什么意思？
Damián: Give up on him? What do you mean?
Isadora：（耐心解释）Julián其实是Salazar家的养子，当作继承人培养的。但看今晚Salazar先生对Julián的态度，应该是彻底失望了。
Isadora: (patiently) Julián was adopted and raised to be the Salazar heir. Judging from Mr. Salazar's attitude tonight, he has completely lost faith in him.
△Damián点了点头。
Damián（VO）：这就是贵族的无情吗？只要弱，说丢弃就被丢弃。
Damián (VO): Is this how ruthless old-money families are? The moment someone proves weak, they cast him aside.
△Samanta凑了过来。
Samanta：所以，要是你弱的话，我姐姐也会立刻抛弃你的！
Samanta: Which means if you ever become useless, my sister will dump you immediately!
△Damián无语地夹了块牛排放到Samanta的盘子上。
Damián：吃东西都堵不住你的嘴！
Damián: Even food cannot keep you quiet!
△Samanta瞪了眼Damián。
47-2夜 内 Salazar家书房
人物：Eloísa、Alberto
△Alberto坐在书房里，门被敲响。
△Eloísa推门走了进来。
Eloísa：爷爷，我……
Eloísa: Grandpa, I...
△Alberto眼神严肃地看了过去，Eloísa被吓得愣在原地。
Alberto：（语气生硬）你不用叫我爷爷，Julián以后也不会再待在Salazar家的。
Alberto: (coldly) Do not call me Grandpa. Julián will no longer be part of the Salazar family.
△Eloísa震惊地愣在原地，表情疑惑。
Eloísa：爷爷，你这是什么意思？
Eloísa: Grandpa, what do you mean?
△Alberto不耐烦地眯起眼睛。
Alberto：我就直说了，我能看出来，你是为了钱。
Alberto: Let me be direct. I know you married him for money.
△Eloísa被噎住，表情尴尬。
Eloísa：爷爷，我……
Eloísa: Grandpa, I...
△Alberto抬手打断Eloísa。
Alberto：你不用急着反驳，反驳也没用。我只是想告诉你，你和Julián的想法我都知道。可惜，你看错人了。
Alberto: Do not bother denying it. I know exactly what you and Julián wanted. Unfortunately, you chose the wrong man.
△Eloísa努力勾起嘴角，皱着眉头。
Eloísa：爷爷，你在说什么？
Eloísa: Grandpa, what are you talking about?
Alberto：Julián只是我们Salazar家的养子。
Alberto: Julián was only an adopted son of the Salazar family.
△Eloísa僵在原地，不可置信。

第四十八集
48-1夜 内 Salazar家书房
人物：Eloísa、Alberto、管家
△Alberto表情讥讽。
Alberto：虽然曾经我拿他当继承人培养，但他的所作所为太让我失望了。所以，Julián已经被我从继承人之列剔出去了。
Alberto: I once raised him to inherit the family business, but his behavior has disappointed me beyond measure. Julián is no longer my heir.
△Alberto看着面色惨白的Eloísa。
Alberto：也就是说，他不会再拿到Salazar家的任何一点资源。而你，和豪门结婚的梦想也失败了，明白了吗？
Alberto: He will receive no further support or assets from the Salazar family. Your dream of marrying into wealth is over. Do you understand?
△Eloísa不可置信地踉跄了几步。
Eloísa（VO）：（不可置信）什么？Julián只是个养子？他之前怎么敢骗我？我放弃了Damián才能和Salazar家结婚，怎么会失败呢？
Eloísa (VO): (in disbelief) What? Julián was adopted? How could he hide that from me? I gave up Damián to become a Salazar. How did everything go wrong?
△Eloísa崩溃地冲出书房。
△Alberto阴沉的靠在椅背上，表情阴沉。
△管家敲了敲门，恭敬的走了进来。
管家：先生，Damián刚才在婚宴上用过的东西都已经收集好了。Butler: Sir, we've collected everything Damián used at the wedding reception.
△Alberto点了点头。
Alberto：好，尽快送到机构去鉴定，我要加急。Alberto: Good. Send them to the lab for testing as soon as possible. I want it expedited.
△管家点了点头，抬头看Alberto的脸色。
管家：先生，如果结果不尽如人意怎么办？Butler: Sir, what if the results aren't what we hope for?
△Alberto苦笑了一下。
Alberto：（叹气）那就是我跟那孩子没有缘分了。Alberto: (Sighs) Then I guess that child and I just weren't meant to be family.
△Alberto抬头看向远方，眼神怅然。
Alberto：不知道是不是心理作用，我越看那孩子越觉得他很像我那个早逝的儿子。Alberto: I don't know if it's just my imagination, but the more I look at him, the more he reminds me of my son who died so young.
△Alberto面容沧桑。
管家：（担忧）先生，等结果出来就知道了，你别太担心。Butler: (Concerned) Sir, we'll know once the results are in. Try not to worry too much.
△Alberto叹了口气，摆了摆手。
△管家退了出去。
48-2夜 内 卧室
人物：Julián、Eloísa
△Julián穿着满是红酒渍的西装躺在床上，慢慢睁开眼睛。
△Julián抱住自己的头，表情痛苦。
Julián：完蛋了，现在彻底完蛋了！我在婚礼上出了那么大的丑，爷爷一定对我失望透了！
Julián: It is over. It is completely over! I humiliated myself at my own wedding. Grandpa must have lost all faith in me!
△卧室门被猛地推开，Eloísa脸色难看地冲了进来，直勾勾地看着Julián。
Eloísa：Julián，原来你一直都在骗我！
Eloísa: Julián, you have been lying to me all along!
△Eloísa歇斯底里地看着Julián。
Eloísa：你为什么不告诉我你只是Salazar家的养子？为什么不早说！
Eloísa: Why did you not tell me you were adopted? Why did you hide the truth?
△Julián看着Eloísa，冷哼一声，慢慢悠悠起身，脸上还带着醉意。
Julián：哈哈哈哈，你后悔了？后悔跟我搞在一起了？哈哈哈！
Julián: Hahaha! You regret it now? You regret sleeping with me? Hahaha!
△Julián笑得面容狰狞。
Julián：晚了！哈哈哈！Eloísa我早就跟你说过，Damián做到了很多男人做不到的事情，他之前是真的爱你。
Julián: Too late! Hahaha! I told you before, Eloísa. Damián did things for you that most men never would. He truly loved you.
△Julián近乎癫狂。
Julián：也就你这个蠢货不知道。你知道吗，我找你就是喜欢你有男朋友的刺激感觉。真是笑死人了，你真以为你能和Salazar家结婚吗？哈哈哈，我们一起被赶出去了！
Julián: You were simply too stupid to appreciate it. Do you know why I pursued you? Because stealing another man's girlfriend was exciting. You really thought you could become a Salazar? What a joke! Now we are both being thrown out!
△Eloísa攥紧拳头，颤抖着身体，表情不甘心。
Julián：现在，我们都是废物了。
Julián: We are both worthless now.
△Eloísa踉踉跄跄地走到Julián面前，抓住Julián的手。
Eloísa：（强装镇定）没事的，说到底你还姓Salazar，手里肯定有资产的。做不成继承人也没关系，我们可以自己开公司啊！
Eloísa: (trying to stay calm) It is all right. Your name is still Salazar, so you must have assets under your name. We can start a company together even if you are no longer the heir!
△Julián一把甩开Eloísa。
Julián：（怒）滚开啊你！你个扫把星！爷爷已经停了我的信用卡，还收回了我的各种资产！都是因为你，我变成了穷鬼，你满意了？
Julián: (furious) Get away from me, you jinx! Grandpa froze my credit cards and took back every asset I had! You made me penniless. Are you satisfied now?
△Eloísa脸色惨白，跌坐在地上。
Eloísa（VO）：（不可置信）什么？他真的变成了穷鬼？开什么玩笑！我不相信！
Eloísa (VO): (in disbelief) What? He is really broke? This cannot be happening. I refuse to believe it!
△Julián看着Eloísa，冷哼一声。
Julián：你以后就给我老老实实带孩子，我要是在爷爷面前表现得好，以后说不定还能再拿回来点资产。
Julián: From now on, stay home and take care of my son. If I can win Grandpa over again, perhaps he will return some of my assets.
△Julián踉踉跄跄摔门离开房间，Eloísa瘫坐在一旁，捂脸哭泣。

第四十九集
49-1夜 内 Isadora卧室
人物：Damián、Isadora
△Damián和Isadora穿着睡衣躺在床上，Damián搂着Isadora。
Isadora：（淡淡）怎么样？你前女友结婚，新郎不是你，你心里难受吗？
Isadora: (flatly) So? Your ex-girlfriend married another man. Does it hurt?
△Damián笑着亲了口Isadora的额头。
Damián：我怎么可能不高兴呢？我可太希望他们永远在一起了。
Damián: Why would it? I could not be happier. I hope those two stay together forever.
△Isadora抬头看了眼Damián，勾起嘴角，翻身压在Damián身上。
△Isadora勾住Damián的衣领，靠近Damián。
Isadora：其实我也不是什么好人，掌控欲很强的，还有我的占有欲、欲望……
Isadora: I am not exactly innocent either. I like being in control, and my jealousy... my desires...
△Isadora到Damián耳边吹着气。
Isadora：都很强的。小帅哥，你现在逃了还来得及。
Isadora: They are all intense. Handsome, this is your last chance to escape.
△Damián的喉结上下滚动，眼睛盯着Isadora，坏笑。
Damián：姐姐，我就喜欢你这样不老实的！
Damián: Naughty girl. I love it.
△Damián亲上Isadora。
49-2日 内 信息部办公室
人物：Damián、员工
△Damián神清气爽地走进信息部办公室，扫视了一圈，看到一个空位置。
Damián（VO）：既然结婚当富太太了，以后就不会再来了吧。
Damián (VO): Now that she is married, I suppose she will not come back to work.
△Damián一脸无所谓，刚要进办公室，就看到Mirella急匆匆跑了进来。
△Mirella脸色凝重。
Mirella：Damián!最近公司要来一位新的副总裁，可能会难为你。
Mirella: Damián! A new vice president is joining the company, and he may come after you.
△Damián一脸疑惑。
Damián：什么？为难我？他不知道我老婆是谁吗？
Damián: Come after me? Does he not know who my wife is?
△Mirella摇了摇头。
Mirella：他没去昨晚的婚礼，所以不知道，之前开除的Luis是他的人。
Mirella: He missed last night's wedding, so he does not know. Luis, the manager we fired, used to work for him.
△Damián表情疑惑。
Mirella：（嘱咐）反正你小心一下吧。
Mirella: (warningly) Just be careful.
△Damián沉思片刻。
Damián：你那里有他的资料吗？发给我一份。
Damián: Do you have his CV? Send it to me.
Mirella：（点头）好，我一会整理一下发给你。（疑惑）你要干嘛？
Mirella: (nods) I will put it together and send it shortly. (confused) What are you planning?
Damián：（神秘一笑）到时候你就知道了。
Damián: (mysteriously) You will see.
49-3日 内 公司走廊
人物：Miguel、Pablo
△Miguel穿着西装，带着几个手下人。
Pablo：副总，这位叫Damián Cortés的二十五岁。入职不到一个月就升职到了信息安全部的部长，背景来头不小。（戴着眼镜，普通男人形象）【人名：Pablo Soto ｜Miguel助手】
[Name: Pablo Soto | Miguel's assistant]
Pablo: Mr. Ángel, this is Damián Cortés, twenty-five. He became Director of Cybersecurity less than a month after joining the company, so he must have powerful connections. 
△Miguel冷哼一声。
Miguel：怕什么？（三十出头男子，精明模样）【人名：Miguel Ángel｜Elorza公司副总】
 [Name: Miguel Ángel | Vice President of Casa Elorza]
Miguel: What is there to fear?
△Pablo表情为难，小声劝解。
Pablo：副总，要不我们先了解清楚当时的情况，或者和总裁说一声吧。
Pablo: Mr. Ángel, perhaps we should find out what really happened first, or speak with Ms. Elorza.
△Miguel抬手阻拦。
Miguel：（自信）已经查清楚了，招商展览会当天的监控就看到他这个技术人员进出。先把他控制起来，再给总裁写报告。我怀疑他就是商业间谍！
Miguel: (confidently) I already investigated. Security footage from the investor showcase recorded this technician entering and leaving the control room. Detain him first, then report to the CEO. I suspect he is a corporate spy!
△Pablo还想说什么吗，Miguel张口打断。
Miguel：当时的监控还拍到了Salazar家的Julián，我有理由怀疑这人就是Salazar家的间谍。拿下间谍，总裁不会怪罪的！咱们走！
Miguel: The footage also captured Julián Salazar. That gives me reason to believe Cortés is working for the Salazars. The CEO will thank us for catching a spy. Let us go!
△Miguel带着Pablo和手下人径直走进信息安全部的办公室。
Miguel：哪个是Damián Cortés？给我滚出来！
Miguel: Which one of you is Damián Cortés? Get outta here!

第五十集
50-1日 内 信息安全部
人物：Damián、Miguel、Pablo、Mirella、员工
△Damián正跟Mirella交谈，Miguel推开办公室的门，径直走了进来，站在门口，表情轻狂地扫视了一圈。
Miguel：哪个是Damián Cortés？给我滚出来！
Miguel: Which one of you is Damián Cortés? Get outta here!
△Damián站在办公室中央，皱眉回头看向Miguel。
△员工们看着Miguel窃窃私语。
员工一：（小声）这就是新来的副总？怎么来我们这里了？
Employee 1: (quietly) Is that the new vice president? What is he doing here?
员工二：听说前两天的招商展览会就是他负责的，被Eloísa搅和了，估计是来兴师问罪的吧。可惜Eloísa根本没来。
Employee 2: I heard he was responsible for the investor showcase that Eloísa ruined. He must be here to punish someone. Too bad Eloísa did not come in today.
△Mirella皱眉看着Miguel。
Mirella：副总？你这么来了？有事应该先跟总裁沟通吧？
Mirella: Mr. Ángel? Why are you here? You should speak with Ms. Elorza first.
△Miguel冷笑一声。
Miguel：Mirella，你真是可以啊。上班时间不在总裁办，来这里晃什么？
Miguel: Mirella, this is impressive. You are supposed to be in the executive office. What are you doing down here during work hours?
△Miguel看了眼Damián。
Miguel：不会是来这里幽会的吧？
Miguel: Or did you come for a secret date?
△Mirella愤怒地看着Miguel。
Mirella：（怒）你什么意思？
Mirella: (furious) What are you implying?
△Miguel表情讥讽。
Miguel：我只是感叹一下。咱们公司现在间谍太多，一个和Salazar家结婚，另一个嘛……
Miguel: I am simply observing that our company is full of spies. One woman married a Salazar, and the other one...
△Miguel直挺挺地打量着Damián。
Miguel：哎，咱们这里的间谍都快比正常员工多了！
Miguel: At this rate, we will have more spies than honest employees!
△Damián笑出了声。
Damián：所以你认为我是间谍？还是Salazar家的间谍？
Damián: So you believe that I am a spy? A Salazar spy?
△Miguel冷笑。
Miguel：难道不是吗？你这个小间谍，破坏他人公司的正常活动，不正当竞争，你就等着吃官司吧！
Miguel: Is that not obvious? You sabotaged another company's business operations and engaged in unfair competition. Prepare to face criminal charges!
△Miguel得意地看着Damián。
△Damián毫不在乎。
Damián：（挑眉）既然你说我是间谍，那，证据呢？
Damián: (raises an eyebrow) If you say that I am a spy, then where is your evidence?
△Miguel脸色一变。
Miguel：证据？抓你这种人还要什么证据！况且我还有你那天偷偷摸摸到操控室的视频！你和那女人也很熟啊，Cortés先生。你就等着警察来带你走吧！
Miguel: Evidence? I do not need evidence to catch someone like you! Besides, I have footage of you sneaking into the control room. You were very close to that woman too, were you not, Mr. Cortés? The police will be here soon.
△周围的员工们憋着偷笑。
△Miguel一脸疑惑地看了圈员工。
Miguel：（羞恼）你们干什么？我一个副总在这说话，你们一个个的是什么意思？
Miguel: (humiliated) What is so funny? I am a vice president. How dare all of you behave this way while I am speaking?
△Miguel拿出手机。
Miguel：我现在就报警！商业犯罪也是犯罪！
Miguel: I am calling the police now! Corporate espionage is a crime!
△Miguel恶狠狠地盯着Damián，小人得志。
△Damián双手插兜，表情无所谓。
Damián：（淡淡一笑）好啊，那你就报警吧，我就在这里，哪也不去！
Damián: (faintly smiles) Go ahead. Call them. I will be right here.
△Miguel震惊地看着Damián，咬牙。
Miguel：我看你能撑到什么时候！
Miguel: Let's see how long you can keep the act!
△Miguel开始拨打电话。
△一阵高跟鞋踩地的声音传来。

第五十一集
51-1日 内 信息安全部
人物：Damián、Isadora、Miguel、Pablo、Mirella、员工
△Damián漫不经心地靠在桌子旁，似笑非笑地盯着Miguel。
△Miguel按下几个号码，刚要拨通。
△Isadora穿着西装高跟鞋大步流星走了进来，气场强大。
△Miguel震惊地看着Isadora，手僵在空中。
Miguel：（谄媚）总裁！你怎么来了？
Miguel: (fawning) Ms. Elorza! What brings you here?
△Isadora脸色阴沉。
△Miguel指着Damián，滔滔不绝。
Miguel：总裁，我知道你也很气愤，没想到公司里竟然出了两个间谍！这个Damián才来公司多久，就跟那个女人里应外合，破坏我们的活动，窃取商业机密！
Miguel: Ms. Elorza, I know you're furious too. Who would've imagined we had two spies inside the company? Damián has barely been here a month, yet he's already conspired with that woman to sabotage our event and steal trade secrets!
△Miguel又指向Mirella。
Miguel：还有Mirella！她也很可疑！我怀疑Damián就是她安排进公司的！
Miguel: And Mirella is suspicious too! I believe she is the one who brought Damián into the company!
△Mirella不可置信地指着自己。
Mirella：（无语）我？我也是间谍？
Mirella: (speechless) Me? I am a spy now too?
△Isadora面无表情，淡淡看了Damián一眼。
Isadora：副总，你说Damián是间谍，那你觉得应该怎么处置比较好呢？
Isadora: Mr. Ángel, if Damián is a spy, what do you think we should do with him?
△Miguel得意扬扬地看着Damián。
Miguel：肯定要开除他啊！然后再报警，把他抓起来！
Miguel: Fire him immediately! Then call the police and have him arrested!
△Isadora笑了一下。
△Miguel脸上的表情更加得意，挑衅地看着Damián。
Isadora：有什么证据能证明Damián是间谍吗？
Isadora: What evidence proves Damián is a spy?
△Miguel一脸得意。
Miguel：当然，我们有监控啊！还有他之前还是Eloísa的前男友！这都是证据！总裁，我们赶紧报警把他抓起来！
Miguel: We have security footage! And he used to date Eloísa! That's more than enough. Ms. Elorza, let's call the police and have him arrested!
△Isadora转过身盯着Miguel。
Isadora：（似笑非笑）好的，你的证据我已经知道了。所以你就准备拿这些东西来抓我老公？
Isadora: (with a faint smile) I see what you're talking about. So you plan to use that to arrest my husband?
△Miguel像是没听到一样，自顾自地说这话。
Miguel：总裁，这个Damián的存在完全就是在威胁我们公司的安全，必须立刻赶出去……
Miguel: Ms. Elorza, Damián is a direct threat to the company's security. He has to be removed immediately...
△Miguel的声音越来越小，僵在原地，表情震惊。
Miguel：（不可置信）什么？什么老公？
Miguel: (in disbelief) What? Whose husband?
△Miguel惊恐地看着Damián。又看了看Isadora。
Miguel：（惊慌失措）总裁的老公？
Miguel: (panicking) The CEO's husband?

第五十二集
52-1日 内 信息安全部
人物：Damián、Isadora、Miguel、Pablo、Mirella、员工
△Miguel脸色难看。
△员工们捂着嘴偷笑。
员工一：副总总算知道Damián是Isadora的老公了。
Employee 1: The vice president finally realized Damián is Isadora's husband.
员工二：这副总反应也太慢了，真是让人笑话。
Employee 2: It took him long enough. How embarrassing.
△Miguel浑身颤抖。
Miguel：我，我真不知道啊。这不是闹了个乌龙吗？（尬笑）哈哈哈，总裁，我跟你道歉。
Miguel: I-I honestly didn't know! This was all a misunderstanding. (awkward laugh) Ms. Elorza, I apologize.
△Miguel转身就要溜。
△Damián收起漫不经心的表情，站定身子，眉眼清冷地盯着Miguel。气场全开。
Damián：副总刚才那么笃定我是商业间谍，却一点事前调查都不做吗？
Damián: You were certain I was a corporate spy, yet you did not conduct even a basic investigation?
△Miguel左顾右盼，神色心虚。
△Isadora双手插着西装口袋，缓缓走到Miguel面前，居高临下，眼神冰冷。
Isadora：（霸气）你靠着臆想就当众污蔑我们公司的核心技术员工。凭借之前莫须有的关系就给人按上间谍的罪名，真是好大的胆子！
Isadora: (authoritatively) You publicly accused one of our key technology leaders based on pure imagination. You branded him a spy over an irrelevant past relationship. Quite bold, aren't you.
△Miguel表情慌乱，送兜里拿出手帕擦了擦额头上的冷汗。
Miguel：（陪笑）总裁，我不是那个意思。我，我也是为公司着想吗，万一真有间谍，那公司的机密不就泄露了吗！
Miguel: (forcing a smile) Ms. Elorza, that's not what I meant. I was only trying to protect the company! If there really were a spy, our confidential information could be leaked!
△Miguel眼睛一转，看着Damián恶狠狠地咬牙污蔑。
Miguel：就算Damián是总裁的丈夫又怎么样？他确实跟Eloísa不清不楚的！还多次意义不明地进到了技术室，行为可疑，我算是合理怀疑！
Miguel: So what if Damián is your husband? He still has a suspicious relationship with Eloísa and repeatedly entered the control room without a solid reason. My suspicions were reasonable!
△Damián轻笑一声，走到Miguel面前。
Damián：（嘲讽）是你说的间谍就要被开除，然后报警送进监狱的对吧？
Damián: (mockingly) You said a spy should be fired, arrested, and sent to prison. Correct?
△Miguel怔愣一瞬，点了点头。
Miguel：当然。
Miguel: Of course.
△Damián点了点头。
Damián：那我就放心了。
Damián: Good. That is a relief.
△Miguel一脸疑惑地看着Damián。
△Damián从容地拉开抽屉，从抽屉里拿出一份文件扔到了Miguel的面前。
Damián：那就请副总好好好解释一下，这文件里的内容吧。
Damián: Then perhaps you can explain what's inside this file, Mr. Ángel.
△Miguel狐疑地回头看了眼冷着脸的Isadora，又看了看似笑非笑的Mirella。
Miguel（VO）：（心虚）这是什么东西？不会是他们已经知道了吧……（猛地）不可能！我做得这么隐蔽！
Miguel (VO): (nervously) What is this? Could they already know? No... Impossible! I covered my tracks perfectly!
△Miguel咽了口口水，强装镇定地伸手拿起桌上的文件夹，颤抖着手缓缓打开，拿出里面的文件，脸色突然变得惨白。

第五十三集
53-1日 内 信息安全部
人物：Damián、Isadora、Miguel、Pablo、Mirella、员工
△Miguel不可置信地看了看Damián又看了看Isadora。
Miguel：你们怎么会有这些？
Miguel: How did you get this?
△Damián笑了笑。
Damián：Mirella通知我你会空降的时候我就留了个心眼。特意查了查你的消息。
Damián: The moment Mirella told me you were joining the company, I became cautious and looked into your background.
Damián（VO）：（窃喜）幸好有戒指给我的超级黑客能力。
Damián (VO): Thank goodness the ring gave me elite hacking skills.
△Damián抽出Miguel手中的文件，摊开放在桌子上，指了指其中一张地址图片，旁边还有一串加密的地址IP。
Damián：我刚入职那天，公司遭到了一次有预谋的病毒攻击，虽然被我一分钟解决了，但如果我不在，那么公司的核心机密就会在五分钟内被转移。
Damián: On my first day, the company was hit by a deliberately planted virus. I stopped it in under a minute. If it's not me, our core trade secrets would have been transferred out within five minutes.
△Miguel浑身颤抖。
Damián：之后我顺着那个病毒留下的BUG找了回去，就得到了这个IP地址。
Damián: I traced the exploit left by that virus and found this IP address.
△空间出现大量密密麻麻的代码，键盘敲击的声音响起。
△代码最终汇聚成一条线，指向图片中的地址。
△Damián盯着Miguel的眼睛。
Damián：你猜，是谁的住宅地址？
Damián: Care to guess whose home address it leads to?
△Miguel慌忙摇头。
Miguel：我，我那是被污蔑了！对，我的地址被恶意入侵了！
Miguel: I-I was framed! Yes, someone hacked my network and made it look like my address!
△Damián嗤笑了一声。
Damián：你还真是嘴硬啊。
Damián: You are remarkably stubborn.
△Damián又抽出了两张纸，上面是Miguel和Luis的转账记录和Luis向神秘人传输文件的记录。
Damián：Luis离职后我接管了他的电脑。他的记录没有删干净，我稍微动动键盘就恢复了记录。每当他转移一次客户资料时，你都会用海外账户给他赚一笔钱。
Damián: I took over Luis's computer after he left. He failed to erase all his records, so recovering them was easy. Every time he transferred client data, you paid him from an offshore account.
△Miguel顿时傻眼。
△Damián抱胸，表情疑惑。
Damián：我还说他一个信息安全部的，怎么会接待客户。现在想来也是为了方便窃取资料吧。
Damián: I always wondered why someone in Cybersecurity kept meeting with clients. Now I see it gave him easier access to their information.
△Miguel跌坐在地上，惊恐地看着Damián。
Miguel：（狡辩）这都是假的！都是P图的！
Miguel: (arguing) This is all fake! Those images were fabricated!
△Damián挑了挑眉。
Damián：我轻松破译了你的病毒，又把你的眼线Luis搞走了。所以你才决定朝我泼脏水吧。
Damián: I neutralized your virus and drove your inside man, Luis, out of the company. That is why you decided to frame me.
△Damián可惜地摇了摇头。
Damián：可惜你怎么不做背调啊？没想到我是Isadora的老公吧。
Damián: It is a shame you never ran a proper background check. You had no idea I was Isadora's husband, did you?
△Miguel表情绝望。
△Isadora冷眼看着Miguel。
Isadora：你父亲曾经帮过我爷爷，我才勉强答应爷爷让你来公司做副总。没想到，从一开始你就抱着毁了我们公司的目的！爷爷真是看错人了！
Isadora: Your father once helped my grandfather, which is the only reason I agreed to bring you in as vice president. I never imagined you intended to destroy our company from the beginning. Grandpa was wrong about you!
△Miguel猛地抬头，恶狠狠地盯着Isadora。
Miguel：（咬牙）还不是你这臭女人！在这转什么清高！
Miguel: (through gritted teeth) This is all your fault, you bitch! Stop pretending you are better than everyone!
△Isadora疑惑地看着Miguel。
第五十四集
54-1日 内 信息安全部
人物：Damián、Isadora、Miguel、Pablo、Mirella、员工
△Miguel愤恨地看着Isadora。
Miguel：我爸低三下四想让我们两个结婚，你却连见我一面都不想。
Miguel: My father humbled himself trying to arrange a marriage between us, and you even refused to meet me.
△Miguel红着眼睛，面容狠戾。
Miguel：不就是出身好点吗。我这么有才华，不用所谓的家族，也能搞垮你们家！可惜，就差一步！
Miguel: You were merely born into a better family! I am talented enough to destroy the Elorzas without any family backing. I was this close!
△Miguel从兜里掏出一把蝴蝶刀，猛地冲向Isadora。
△Isadora惊恐。
Damián：（担忧）小心！
Damián: (worriedly) Watch out!
△Damián快步上前，抓住Miguel拿着蝴蝶刀的手，稍稍用力。
△蝴蝶刀掉在地上。
△Miguel惨叫出声。
Miguel：啊！我的手！
Miguel: Ah! My hand!
△Damián一个背摔把Miguel撂倒在地，Miguel疼的不断挣扎。
△Damián快步走到Isadora身边，不断打量Isadora身上。
Damián：（担忧）他没伤到你吧？
Damián: (worriedly) Did he hurt you?
△Isadora皱眉摇了摇头。
△Damián看向Mirella，表情严肃。
Damián：刚才副总说了，要是有间谍的话就开除报警！Mirella，打电话！
Damián: The vice president said any spy should be fired and arrested. Mirella, call the police!
△Mirella点头拿出手机。
Mirella：好！
Mirella: Right away!
△Miguel脸色一变，立刻爬了起来，抓住Damián的脚，表情恳求。
Miguel：（卑微）我错了！我真的错了！别报警！我再也不敢了！你们要多少钱？我立刻给你们转，你们别报警！
Miguel: (begging) I was wrong! I am sorry! Please do not call the police! I will never do it again! How much do you want? I will pay you now--just do not report me! Please!
△Damián低着头，居高临下地看着Miguel。
Damián：（冷声）钱？你觉得我缺钱吗？
Damián: (coldly) Money? Do I look like I need your money?
△Damián用力甩开Miguel。
Damián：报警！
Damián: Call the police.
△两个保安快步走了进来，压住了Miguel。
Damián：你们先带他去安保室，警察来了直接移交！
Damián: Take him to the security and hand him over as soon as the police arrive.
△保安拖着不断挣扎的Miguel走了下去。
Miguel：（怒吼）我不会放过你们的！不会放过你们的！
Miguel: (shouting) I will make you pay! I will make all of you pay!
△Isadora看着Damián，眼神负责。
△员工们窃窃私语。
员工一：部长刚才那一招好帅啊。
Employee 1: The director looked incredible taking him down.
员工二：你们不觉得总裁也很宠吗，直接过来替部长撑腰。
Employee 2: And did you see how the CEO came down here to defend him? She adores him.
员工三：他们真是太般配了！
Employee 3: What a perfect couple!
△Isadora眼神复杂地看了眼Damián。
Isadora：（听不出情绪）Damián，你跟我来。
Isadora: (unreadable) Damián, come with me.
△Isadora说完转身离开。
△Damián有些懵看向Mirella。
△Mirella小声跟Damián说话。
Mirella：完蛋了，她这个表情就说明她生气了，你自求多福吧。
Mirella: You are in trouble. That expression means she is angry. Good luck.
Damián：（疑惑）生气？
Damián: (confused) Angry?
△Damián快步跟上Isadora。

第五十五集
55-1日 内 总裁办公室
人物：Damián、Isadora
△Isadora坐在老板椅上，冷着脸处理电脑上的工作。
△Damián贱笑着推开门走了进来。
Damián：（夹着嗓子）亲爱的，找我什么事啊？
Damián: (in a sweet voice) Darling, what did you want to see me about?
△Isadora瞥了眼Damián，飞快撤回视线。
Isadora：坐下。
Isadora: Sit down.
△Damián绕到Isadora身旁，伸手将Isadora紧箍在怀里。
Damián：（撒娇）怎么啦，怎么不开心？
Damián: (coaxingly) What is wrong? Why are you upset?
△Isadora撅了撅嘴，不开心。
Isadora：（埋怨）被人欺负了也不知道来找我，还好我路过。
Isadora: (complaining) Someone attacked you, and you never thought to call me. Luckily, I happened to pass by.
△Damián笑。
Damián：哎呀，这点小事，我可以应付的。
Damián: It was a minor problem. I could handle it.
△Isadora抓着Damián的脑袋，直视Damián。
Isadora：这次你是抓到他的把柄了，要是没抓到呢？还有，他拿着刀你也敢往上冲……
Isadora: This time you had evidence against him. What if you had not? And he had a knife, yet you still charged at him...
△Damián笑着亲上了Isadora。
△Isadora瞪大了眼睛。
△Damián含笑离开，亲昵地跟Isadora蹭了蹭笔尖。
Damián：宝贝，我保证没有下一次了。
Damián: Babe, I promise it won't happen again.
△Isadora轻哼一声。
Isadora：花言巧语。
Isadora: Sweet talker.
△Isadora低下头，勾起嘴角。
△Isadora起身拿起车钥匙。
Isadora：走吧，跟我出去一趟，
Isadora: Come on. We are going out.
Damián：（疑惑）去哪？
Damián: (confused) Where?
Isadora：Samanta今天下午有一场跆拳道比赛，你跟我一起去吧。
Isadora: Samanta has a taekwondo tournament this afternoon. You are coming with me.
△Damián假装为难。
Damián：可是我还要上班诶。
Damián: But I still have work to do.
Isadora：（不容置疑）那总裁给你批个假。
Isadora: (firmly) Then the CEO is giving you the afternoon off.
Damián：（笑）好！
Damián: (smiles) Yes, ma'am.
△Isadora拉着Damián离开。
55-2日 内 比赛场馆门口
人物：Damián、Isadora、Camila、观众
△Damián和Isadora在场馆门口排队，准备检票，旁边都是排队的观众。
△Camila站在Damián斜后方的队伍里，看到Damián眼睛一亮。
Camila：Damián？他怎么也来看比赛了？我跟Eloísa说一声。
[Name: Camila Fuentes | Eloísa's best friend]
Camila: Damián? What is he doing at the tournament? I should tell Eloísa. 
△Camila拿出电话，给Eloísa打电话。
55-3日 内 卧室
人物：Eloísa、Julián
△卧室里全是酒瓶，窗帘拉得严实。
△Julián躺在床上呼呼大睡。
△Eloísa看了眼墙上的时间，已经十一点了，小心翼翼地走到Julián身边轻轻推了推他。
Eloísa：Julián，该起床了。
Eloísa: Julián, wake up.
△Julián不耐烦地睁开眼睛，狠狠打了Eloísa一巴掌。
Julián：（怒）别吵我睡觉！滚出去！
