Julián: (furious) Stop bothering me! Fuck off!
△Eloísa红着眼睛捂着脸跑了出去。

第五十六集
56-1日 内 客厅
人物：Eloísa
△Eloísa捂着脸趴在沙发上痛哭。
△Eloísa的手机突然想起，Eloísa抽抽嗒嗒接起电话。
Camila（OS）：Eloísa，你猜我看到谁了？
Camila: Eloísa, guess who I just saw?
△Eloísa不停抽噎哭泣。
Camila（OS）：（疑惑）你怎么哭了？
Camila: (confused) Why are you crying?
Eloísa：（委屈）Julián他打我了！如果我还跟Damián在一起，他绝对不会打我的。我真的好后悔啊。
Eloísa: (hurt) Julián hit me! If I were still with Damián, he never would have laid a hand on me. I regret everything.
△Eloísa崩溃大哭。
Camila（OS）：我正要跟你说呢，我在体育馆看到Damián了，他旁边还有一个女人，应该就是他老婆。
Camila: That's what I was calling to tell you. I saw Damián at the arena, and there was a woman with him. She must be his wife.
△Eloísa停止哭泣，攥紧双手。
Eloísa：我想起来了，我这还有一张半年前他手写的无条件满足我愿望的卡，我这就过去让他满足我！
Eloísa: I just remembered--I still have that card he wrote me six months ago, promising to grant me any wish. I'm going over there right now to make him honor it!
△Camila一时语塞，语气怀疑。
Camila：不是，你们都分别结婚了，这玩意还能有用吗？
Camila: Wait. You're both married to other people now. Do you really think that card still means anything?
△Eloísa冲着电话歇斯底里。
Eloísa：你不懂！你不懂我们之间的感情！你根本不懂他有多爱我！他跟Isadora在一起也只是为了气我！
Eloísa: You don't understand! You don't understand what we had! You have no idea how much he loves me! He's only with Isadora to make me jealous!
△Eloísa红着眼睛，表情狰狞执拗。
Eloísa：（癫狂）只要我用了这张卡Damián绝对会离婚的！
Eloísa: (unhinged) The moment I use this card, Damián will divorce her!
56-2日 内 比赛场馆门口
人物：Damián、Isadora、Camila、观众
△Camila拿着电话一脸无语。
△Damián和Isadora手挽着手，手里拿着爆米花和汽水走进了场馆。
Camila（VO）：（无语）当初Damián跟你谈恋爱的时候你不珍惜，现在又要挽回，怎么可能嘛。
Camila (VO): (speechless) You never appreciated Damián when you had him, and now you want him back? As if that could ever happen.
△Camila挂了电话，一脸无语。
56-3日 内 比赛场馆内
人物：Damián、Isadora、Samanta、观众
△Samanta穿着跆拳道服站在中央的比赛场地上，坐着拉伸。
△Damián和Isadora坐到了第一排中间，距离场地有一段距离，用栏杆拦着。
△Samanta看到Isadora开心地蹦了起来，挥手打招呼。
△Isadora笑着回应。
△Damián笑着抬手刚要打招呼。
△Samanta看到Damián翻了个白眼，背过身接着拉伸。
△Damián无语地瘪了瘪嘴。
Damián：（吐槽）我说怎么刚见面就给我一脚，原来是练过的。
Damián: (teasing) So that's why you kicked me the moment we met. You're professional.
△Isadora笑着怼了怼Damián。
Isadora：（哭笑不得）我怎么不知道你这么记仇。
Isadora: (half laughing) I had no idea you could hold a grudge this long.
△两人刚落座，戒指的虚影突然出现在Damián面前。
戒指（OS）：（低沉）命运节点已记录。节点：体育馆。命运馈赠——跆拳道黑带大师。
Ring (OS): (deep) Fate checkpoint registered. Location: the arena. Reward: black-belt taekwondo mastery.
△一个金色的小身穿着跆拳道服出现在Damián面前，打着跆拳道的招式，最后小人飞进了Damián的脑海。

第五十七集
57-1日 内 比赛场馆内
人物：Damián、Isadora、Eloísa、Samanta、Camila、Ramiro、观众
△Isadora拿着一瓶水怼了怼Damián。
Isadora：你帮我给Samanta送过去。
Isadora: Take this over to Samanta for me.
△Damián接过水亲了口Isadora。
Damián：遵命。
Damián: As you wish.
△Damián拿着水离开。
△Camila坐在后面的普通席位上看着Damián和Isadora的互动，表情纠结。
△Eloísa戴着口罩凑了过来。
Eloísa：Camila，我来了。
Eloísa: Camila, I'm here.
△Camila皱着眉看着Eloísa的脸。
Camila：Eloísa，你的脸……
Camila: Eloísa, your face...
△Eloísa低下眼，摸了摸脸。
Eloísa：我的脸还肿着，所以戴了口罩。Damián呢？
Eloísa: It's still swollen, so I'm wearing a mask. Where's Damián?
△Camila指向了场中央和Samanta打打闹闹的Damián。
Camila：在那呢。Eloísa，要不还是算了。他结婚了，你也结婚了。
Camila: Over there. Eloísa, maybe you should let this go. He's married, and so are you.
△Eloísa执拗地摇了摇头。
Eloísa：不是的，他们肯定是假结婚。他们家的水平差那么多，Isadora不可能看得上他的。
Eloísa: No. Their marriage has to be fake. Their family backgrounds are worlds apart. Isadora could never be interested in him.
△Eloísa盯着往回走的Damián，双眼含泪。
Eloísa：Damián那么爱我，对我这么好，我再也找不到这么好的男人了！等他离婚后，我再去找他，你说他会不会很感动？
Eloísa: Damián loves me so much. He treated me better than anyone ever has! Once he divorces her, I'll go to him. Do you think he'll be moved?
△Camila无语地翻了个白眼。
Camila（VO）：你伤害那么多次了，之前都逃婚了，Damián疯了才会再找你。
Camila (VO): You've hurt him over and over. You even ran away from your wedding. He'd have to be out of his mind to take you back.
△Eloísa抓住Camila的手。
Eloísa：这样吧，等比赛结束后我们一起去找Damián，然后使用我的无条件卡，怎么样？
Eloísa: How about this? After the tournament, we'll go find Damián together, and I'll use my wish card. What do you think?
△Camila尴尬一笑。
△比赛开始，Samanta和Ramiro都戴着头盔，身着跆拳道服，互相鞠躬，准备开始比赛。
△Ramiro咬牙，表情紧张。
Ramiro：（压低声音）输给我，你要多少钱我都给你。【人名：Ramiro Paredes ｜Samanta对手】
[Name: Ramiro Paredes | Samanta's opponent]
Ramiro: (under his breath) Lose to me and I'll pay you whatever you want. 
△Samanta表情不屑。
Samanta：我只相信实力！
Samanta: I only believe in winning on merit!
△Samanta怒喝一声，踢向Ramiro。
△Damián和Isadora靠在一起，吃着一份爆米花。
△Eloísa坐在后面，咬着牙观察着Damián。
△Samanta快速进攻，不断踢腿踢中对面的身体，头部和四肢。
△Isadora表情骄傲。
Isadora：没想到Samanta这么厉害。
Isadora: I had no idea Samanta was this good.
Damián：（解说）击中躯干得2分，旋转踢击中躯干得3分，头部踢中得3分。
Damián: (commentating) A kick to the torso is worth two points, a spinning kick to the torso is worth three, and a kick to the head is worth three.
△Isadora震惊地看着Damián。
Isadora：跆拳道你也了解。
Isadora: You know taekwondo too?
△Damián笑着点了点头。
Damián：了解一点点。
Damián: Just a little.
△Ramiro被打得节节败退，分数班上，Samanta的分数是到了12，Ramiro依旧是零。
△铃声响起，比赛结束，裁判举起了Samanta的手。
△台下掌声欢呼不断。
△Samanta撤下头盔，刚要下场，
△Ramiro，表情不甘心地冲向Samanta，抬腿踹向Samanta的腿。
△Isadora震惊地抓住Damián的手，起身大喊。
Isadora：Samanta，小心！
Isadora: Samanta, look out!

第五十八集
58-1日 内 比赛场馆内
人物：Damián、Isadora、Eloísa、Samanta、Camila、Ramiro、观众
△Damián手撑过一排前面的栏杆，跨了过去。
△Damián飞奔到Samanta身前护住Samanta，一脚踹向Ramiro的腿。
△Ramiro踉跄一步，双手撑地，双腿跪地摔在地上。
△Samanta震惊抬头看向护着自己的Damián。
△Isadora趴在栏杆外，松了口气，急匆匆跑了过来。
△Damián低头看Samanta。
Damián：你没事吧？
Damián: Are you all right?
△Samanta怔愣地摇了摇头，回过神来，从Damián怀里挣开。
△Damián皱眉看向一旁的Ramiro。
Damián：是不是男人啊？搞背后偷袭？
Damián: What kind of man attacks someone from behind?
△下边的观众一片嘘声。
△Ramiro的脸青一阵白一阵。
△Ramiro爬了起来，阴狠地瞪着Damián。
Ramiro：你根本不知道这次机会对我有多重要！你找死！
Ramiro: You have no idea what this chance means to me! You're dead!
△Ramiro咬着牙冲向Damián，一个回旋踢踢向Damián。
△Damián毫不畏惧，一个高抬腿踹了出去。
△Ramiro直接被踹飞，倒在地上呻吟不止。
Ramiro：（哀号）我的腿！我的腿断了！
Ramiro: (wailing) My leg! You broke my leg!
△Samanta目瞪口呆地看着Damián。
Samanta：Damián竟然还会跆拳道？
Samanta: Damián knows taekwondo too?
△Isadora笑了笑。
Isadora：这个男人很神秘啊。（不解）这人说什么机会？
Isadora: That man is full of surprises. (puzzled) What did he mean by "this chance"?
△Samanta一脸无语。
Samanta：这人有个追求者，那个追求者说了，只要他赢了今天的比赛就答应跟他在一起。
Samanta: A woman he's been pursuing promised to date him if he won today's tournament.
△Isadora无语地看了眼Damián。
△Damián挠了挠头。
Damián：（尬笑）别看我啊，我才不会这样。
Damián: (awkward laugh) Don't look at me. I'd never do that.
△裁判吹着哨给了Ramiro一张红牌。
裁判：偷袭罚下！
Referee: You're disqualified for that attack!
△医疗队穿着白大褂，拿着板车抬起Ramiro下了场。
Ramiro：（哀号）我绝对不会放过你们的！
Ramiro: (wailing) You'll pay for this!
△Isadora担忧地看着Samanta。
Isadora：你没事吧？
Isadora: Are you all right?
△Samanta摇了摇头。
Samanta：没事！我还得准备下一场比赛呢！
Samanta: I'm fine! I still have to get ready for my next match!
△Damián看向Isadora。
Damián：你先回去，我去上个厕所。
Damián: You go ahead. I need to use the restroom.
△Isadora点了点头。
△Damián转身离开会场。
△看台上的Eloísa看到Damián离开，也起身走了出去。
58-2日 内 卫生间门口
人物：Damián、Eloísa
△Damián从卫生间里出来。
△Eloísa直接冲了过去，拉开口罩，露出红肿的脸。
△Damián被吓得后跳一大步。
Damián：（诧异）Eloísa？
Damián: (startled) Eloísa?

第五十九集
59-1日 内 卫生间门口
人物：Damián、Eloísa
△Eloísa从兜里掏出一张写着“无条件应答卡”，下面还有Damián名字的签名，怼到了Damián面前。
Eloísa：Damián!这是你之前答应我的！只要我拿出这张卡，无论什么要求你都会满足我的！
Eloísa: Damián! You promised me this! If I showed you this card, you would do anything I asked!
△Damián一愣。
Damián（VO）：这都是多久之前的事情了，她怎么还记得。
Damián (VO): That was ages ago. How does she still remember it?
△Damián一脸无语地看着一脸期待的Eloísa。
Damián：（冷笑）Eloísa，你别搞错了，你已经结婚了，我也结婚了。我们已经没有关系了。
Damián: (coldly) Eloísa, get this straight. You're married, and so am I. There is nothing between us anymore.
△Damián皱眉要离开。
△Eloísa伸手阻拦。
Eloísa：我不管！Damián!我不管！结婚了还可以离婚。是我先跟你在一起的！你去跟Isadora离婚，林婚以后我们在一起好不好？
Eloísa: I don't care! Damián, I don't care! Married people can get divorced. I was with you first! Divorce Isadora, and then we can be together. Please?
△Eloísa抓起Damián的手让他摸自己的脸。
Eloísa：（癫狂）Julián对我不好，我的脸都被他打肿了，你摸摸！
Eloísa: (unhinged) Julián treats me terribly. He hit me so hard my face is still swollen. Feel it!
△Damián惊恐地甩开Eloísa的手。
Damián：你疯了！你这个疯子！
Damián: You're insane! You're completely out of your mind!
△Damián嫌恶地盯着Eloísa。
Damián：你都已经跟Julián在一起了，这都是你自己的选择，为什么还在这摆出一副很可怜的样子？
Damián: You chose to be with Julián. Why are you standing here acting like you're the victim?
△Eloísa苦苦哀求。
Eloísa：要不是因为我跟Julián结婚，我怎么知道你的好呢？以后我们平等相处好不好？就像普通情侣那样！
Eloísa: I had to marry Julián to realize how good you were to me. We can be equals from now on, okay? Like a normal couple!
△Damián闭着眼睛深呼吸一口气，睁开眼睛，神色平淡。
Damián：（淡淡）滚。
Damián: (flatly) Get lost.
△Damián语气讥讽。
Damián：你可真会玩啊，自己已经结婚了，却拿着一张破纸让我跟我老婆离婚，然后继续围着你转吗？
Damián: Unbelievable. You're married, yet you wave around some worthless scrap of paper and expect me to divorce my wife so I can go back to worshipping you?
△Eloísa慌忙摆手。
Eloísa：不，不是这样的！你被Isadora骗了！她可是总裁！怎么可能喜欢你呢？只有我对你是真心的！她一直都在利用你！
Eloísa: No, that's not it! Isadora has deceived you! She's a CEO. How could she possibly love you? I'm the only one who truly cares about you! She's been using you all along!
△Eloísa扑向Damián，Damián立刻躲开。
△Eloísa直接跪了下去。
Eloísa：Damián，我们真的能回到过去的！你相信我，我真的很爱你！
Eloísa: Damián, we really can go back to the way things were! Believe me. I truly love you!
Damián：（嫌弃）Eloísa，你真的很让人恶心。
Damián: (disgusted) Eloísa, you make me sick.
Isadora（OS）：你算什么东西！敢跟我抢人？
Isadora: Who do you think you are, trying to steal my man?

第六十集
60-1日 内 卫生间门口
人物：Damián、Isadora、Eloísa
△Isadora冷着脸快步走了过来，居高临下地看着Eloísa。
Isadora：当初是你先逃婚的，现在他是我的人了，你还敢伸手抢？滚远点！
Isadora: You were the one who ran away from your wedding. He's mine now, and you still dare come after him? Stay away from us!
△Eloísa捂着脸泣不成声。
Eloísa：（崩溃）你们都欺负我！信不信我死给你们看！
Eloísa: (breaking down) You're all ganging up on me! I swear I'll kill myself!
△Damián一脸无语。
Damián：那麻烦你死远一点好吗？别脏了我的眼睛。
Damián: Then do it somewhere else. I don't want to look at you.
△Eloísa动作僵住，不可置信地看着Damián。
△Eloísa手机突然响起，是Margarita的电话，Eloísa接起。
Margarita（OS）：（怒）Eloísa！你来Salazar家庄园一趟！我们身为主人要进去，却被一个破管家拦在了门外！你老公呢？让他出来！
Margarita: (furious) Eloísa! Get over to the Salazar estate! We're family, yet some useless butler is keeping us outside! Where's your husband? Tell him to come out here!
△Eloísa攥紧了手机，深深看了眼Damián和Isadora，转身跑了出去。
△Damián无语。
Damián：真是无理取闹！
Damián: She's unbelievable.
△Damián刚要离开，被Isadora一把拉住。
△Damián一脸震惊。
△Isadora冷笑着。
Isadora：好了，现在解释解释你前女友说的那个什么卡呗？
Isadora: All right. Now explain that card your ex-girlfriend was talking about.
△Damián一脸尴尬地搂住Isadora的腰。
Damián：（撒娇）那都是我年少不懂事嘛。
Damián: (coyly) I was young and foolish...
60-2日 外 Salazar家庄园门口
人物：Margarita、Jairo、Eloísa、管家
△Salazar家庄园整体森严可怖，庄园大门紧闭，只开了个小门，管家堵在门口。
△Margarita插着腰，表情蛮横。
Margarita：（怒吼）你们什么意思？我女儿可是和你们家结婚了的！我就是这里的主人，你凭什么不让我进？
Margarita: (shouting) What is wrong with you people? My daughter married into this family! That makes me family too. What gives you the right to keep me out?
△管家压住怒火。
管家：Julián已经离开Salazar家了，以后也不会再回来，你们可以自行联系他。
Butler: Julián has left the Salazar family. He will not be returning. You may contact him yourselves.
△Jairo和Margarita疑惑地对视一眼。
Jairo：（不解）什么意思？Julián不住在这了？
Jairo: (confused) What do you mean? Julián doesn't live here anymore?
△Margarita冷哼一声，接着撒泼。
Margarita：我看你就是在骗我！狗屁百年家族，翻脸不认人，我看就是不想给我们钱！
Margarita: You're lying! So much for this distinguished old family. The moment it suits you, you turn your backs on us. You just don't want to give us any money!
△Eloísa急匆匆地赶了过来，神情狼狈。
Eloísa：爸妈，你们怎么来了？
Eloísa: Mom, Dad, what are you doing here?
△Margarita拉住Eloísa。
Margarita：Eloísa，这Salazar家怎么无缘无故跟我们翻脸了啊？
Margarita: Eloísa, why has the Salazar family suddenly turned against us?
△Eloísa苦笑一声。
Eloísa：妈，Julián根本不是Salazar家亲生的孩子，只是个养子而已。我们都被骗了。
Eloísa: Mom, Julián isn't a biological Salazar. He was adopted. We were all deceived.
△Jairo和Margarita脸色大变。
Margarita：（不可置信）什么！
Margarita: (in disbelief) What?!

第六十一集
61-1日 外 Salazar家庄园门口
人物：Margarita、Jairo、Eloísa、管家
△Jairo脸色阴沉。
Jairo：为什么？为什么会这样！
Jairo: Why? How could this happen?
△Margarita不能接受，喃喃自语。
Margarita：怎么会这样？我女儿应该跟豪门结婚的！怎么Julián是个假少爷？
Margarita: How did this happen? My daughter was supposed to marry into a rich family! How can Julián be a fake heir?
△管家一脸嫌弃地看着三人。
△Eloísa扯了扯Margarita。
Eloísa：我们先回去吧。
Eloísa: Let's go home for now.
△Margarita反应过来。
Margarita：（咬牙）对！回去！去找Julián算账！
Margarita: (through gritted teeth) Yes! Let's go home and settle this with Julián!
61-2日 内 大平层门口
人物：Julián、Eloísa、Mateo、Jairo、Margarita、保镖
△Eloísa和JairoMargarita来到大平层，就看到Julián抱着Mateo一脸憔悴地站在门口，地上放着一个行李箱。
△两个保镖堵在门口，看到Eloísa出现清了清嗓子。
保镖一：众人到齐了，那我就替先生宣布一件事。
Bodyguard 1: Now that everyone is here, I have an announcement to make on Mr. Salazar's behalf.
△保镖语气淡淡的。
保镖：Julián少爷已经不是Salazar家的人了，所以名下的所有房产和资产都将被收回，信用卡也将被冻结，不会再给你们提供资助。现在请立刻收拾东西离开。
Bodyguard: Julián is no longer a member of the Salazar family. All family-owned property and assets under his control will be reclaimed, his credit cards will be frozen, and the family will provide no further financial support. Pack your belongings and leave immediately.
△Margarita一听直接瘫软坐在了地上。
△Eloísa冲向Julián，质问Julián。
Eloísa：Julián!你说话啊！到底是怎么回事？
Eloísa: Julián! Say something! What is going on?
△Julián表情毫不在乎。
Julián：什么怎么回事，正如你们所见我的一切都没有，你们满意了？
Julián: What do you think? You can see for yourselves that I've lost everything. Happy now?
△Margarita一脸绝望。
Margarita：我的荣华富贵都没有了……
Margarita: My life of wealth and luxury is gone...
△Jairo脸色阴沉，不满地瞪着Julián和Mateo。
Jairo：（冷哼）呵，豪门没当上，拖油瓶倒是多了两个！这下怎么办？
Jairo: (scoffs) So much for marrying into a rich family. Instead, we got two more mouths to feed! What are we supposed to do now?
△Eloísa充满希望地看着Julián。
Eloísa：你难道就没有自己卡自己的钱吗？
Eloísa: Don't you have a bank acount or money of your own?
△Julián尴尬地看了Eloísa一眼。
Julián：我都是用爷爷给的卡的，没有自己卡，所以……
Julián: I always used the card my grandfather gave me. I don't have one of my own, so...
△Eloísa深感无力地翻了个白眼。
Eloísa：唉。
Eloísa: Oh, great.
△Eloísa看向Jairo和Margarita。
Eloísa：事到如今还是先租个房子吧。爸妈，你们身上还有钱吗？
Eloísa: At this point, we should rent a place first. Mom, Dad, do you have any money?
△Margarita冷下了脸。
Margarita：哪来的钱？你还指望我们给你们拿钱？Eloísa!我劝你赶紧离婚！
Margarita: What money? You expect us to support you? Eloísa, divorce him now!

第六十二集
62-1日 内 大平层门口
人物：Julián、Eloísa、Mateo、Jairo、Margarita、保镖
△Eloísa叹了口气，凑到Margarita耳边。
Eloísa：妈，Julián手里应该还有些人脉。再说刚结婚就离婚多不好啊。
Eloísa: Mom, Julián still have connections. Besides, it would look terrible to divorce right after the wedding.
△Margarita不耐烦地挥了挥手。
Margarita：随便你们！之前我们家租的那个街区肯定是不行了。Damián那个混蛋大闹一通，我们的名声都毁了！
Margarita: Do whatever you want! We can't go back to the neighborhood where we used to rent. That bastard Damián caused such a scene that our reputation there is ruined!
△Eloísa点头，看向Julián。
Eloísa：明天我会回Elorza公司上班，你也赶紧找个工作。
Eloísa: I'm going back to work at Casa Elorza tomorrow. You need to find a job too.
△Julián不在乎地点了点头。
Eloísa（VO）：豪门梦破碎了，我的工作可不能再丢了。
Eloísa (VO): My dream of marrying into money is over. I can't afford to lose my job too.
62-2日 内 办公室
人物：Damián
△Damián把腿翘在桌子上，举着手机表情甜蜜地打电话。
Damián：亲爱的，你在干嘛呀？
Damián: Honey, what are you up to?
Isadora（VO）：马上就要开会去了，你呢？在打游戏吧。
Isadora（VO）: I'm about to head into a meeting. What about you? Playing video games?
△Damián的身子猛地坐直。
Damián：（讪笑）怎么会呢，我当然是一边工作一边想念我亲爱的老婆啊！
Damián: (sheepish laugh) Of course not. I'm working while thinking about my beautiful wife!
△Isadora笑了一声。
Isadora（VO）：Damián，昨天打卡时间是八个小时，游戏页面浏览时间七个半小时。我很好奇，剩下的半个小时你可以处理什么工作啊？
Isadora（VO）: Damián, you were clocked in for eight hours yesterday, and your game was on for seven and a half of them. I'm curious--what work could you possibly have done in the other half hour?
△Damián笑得磕磕巴巴。
Damián：（慌乱）就是……这个……那个……工作嘛！
Damián: (flustered) Well... this... and that... Work stuff!
△Isadora宠溺一笑。
Isadora（VO）：行吧，那你加油哦，我去开会了。
Isadora: All right. Keep up the good work. I'm heading off to my meeting.
Damián：（笑）好。
Damián: (smiles) Okay.
△Damián刚挂断电话，打开游戏网页，就听到外面传来一阵叽叽喳喳的声音。
员工一（OS）：（恭维）Eloísa，你怎么来了？我们都以为你会去蜜月旅行呢！豪门生活好不好啊？
Employee 1（OS）: (fawning) Eloísa, what are you doing here? We thought you'd be off on your honeymoon! How's life among the rich and famous?
Damián挑眉看向声音的方向。
切场 62-3日 内 信息安全部办公室
员工二：你不会是回来辞职的吧？
Employee 2: You're not here to resign, are you?
Eloísa：（尴尬）没有，Julián他很忙的。也没时间出去玩。他为了继承Salazar家也要努力嘛。那我也不能落后，就回来上班了。
Eloísa: (awkwardly) No. Julián is very busy, so we didn't have time to travel. He has to work hard if he wants to inherit the Salazar family fortune. I can't fall behind either, so I came back to work.
切场 62-4 日 内 办公室
△Damián噗嗤一声笑了出来。
Damián：（讥讽）这Eloísa真能装啊。
Damián: (mocking) Eloísa really knows how to put on an act.
62-5日 内 信息安全部办公室
人物：Damián、Eloísa、员工
△员工们围着Eloísa，Eloísa表情有些尴尬。
Eloísa：（打发）好了好了，别聊我了，快工作吧。
Eloísa: (brushing them off) All right, enough about me. Let's get back to work.
△Damián打开部长办公室的门，笑眯眯地看着Eloísa。
△Eloísa有些紧张。
Damián：（笑）Eloísa，我渴了，去茶水间给我接点水。
Damián: (smiles) Eloísa, I'm thirsty. Go get me some water from the break room.
△Eloísa的脸色变得难看。
Eloísa：（诧异）什么？
Eloísa: (startled) What?

第六十三集
63-1日 内 信息安全部办公室
人物：Damián、Eloísa、员工
△员工们看向Eloísa。
△Eloísa深呼吸一口气，压住脾气，笑眯眯地看着Damián。
Eloísa：好的，我这就去。
Eloísa: Of course. I'll get it right away.
△Eloísa转身离开。
△员工们震惊的面面相觑，然后出言嘲讽。
员工一：（讥讽）这Eloísa还真以为我们什么都不知道呢。这能装，千方百计接过跟一个冒牌货结婚了。
Employee 1: (mocking) Eloísa really thinks we don't know the truth. All that bragging, and after pulling every trick in the book, she married a fraud.
员工二：真是笑死人了，之前那么贬低咱们科长，现在也只能给部长打打下手了。
Employee 2: It's hilarious. After all the times she looked down on our team lead, now all she can do is run errands for the director.
△Damián心情很好地勾起嘴角，关门回了办公室。
63-2日 内 茶水间
人物：Eloísa
△Eloísa一边泡着咖啡，一边擦着眼泪，表情委屈。
Eloísa：为什么会这样？我到底做错了什么？Damián、Julián都要欺负我。
Eloísa: Why is this happening? What did I do wrong? Both Damián and Julián are tormenting me.
△Eloísa一个不留神，热水浇到受伤。
Eloísa：（吃痛）啊！好疼！
Eloísa: (in pain) Ow! That hurts!
△Eloísa愤怒地拍了一下桌子。
Eloísa：你们都该死！
Eloísa: Damn you all!
63-3日 内 Damián办公室
人物：Damián、Eloísa
△Damián在办公室里打着电脑游戏。
△Eloísa小心翼翼地拿着咖啡推开了Damián办公室的门。
△Eloísa胸前衬衫的扣子被刻意松开，裙子也被提高了。
△Eloísa故意露出被烫红的位置，可怜巴巴地把咖啡递给Damián。
Eloísa：Damián，咖啡给你倒好了。
Eloísa: Damián, your coffee is ready.
△Damián一边打着游戏，一边瞥了眼Eloísa，神情淡淡的。
Damián：谁说我要喝咖啡了？
Damián: Who said I wanted coffee?
△Eloísa一时语塞。
Eloísa：不是你说渴了吗？
Eloísa: Didn't you say you were thirsty?
Damián：（淡淡）谁说渴了就喝咖啡？我可是你的领导，你起码多准备几样吧。你能力怎么这么差啊？能不能干？不能干就走人！
Damián: (flatly) Who says being thirsty means I want coffee? I'm your boss. The least you could do is bring me a few options. Why are you so incompetent? If you can't do the job, then leave!
△Eloísa低着头，双手手指搅和在一起，眼泪在眼眶里打转。
Eloísa：（委屈）那你想喝什么？
Eloísa: (hurt) Then what would you like?
Damián：（冷声）自己想，滚出去！
Damián: (coldly) Figure it out yourself. Get out!
△Eloísa僵在原地，脸色难看。
【闪回开始】
63-4夜 内 小卧室
人物：Damián、Eloísa
【字幕：半年前】
[Subtitle: Six Months Ago]
△Eloísa躺在床上敷着面膜看着杂志。
△Damián围着围裙，端着一份奶油蘑菇汤放到了床头。
Damián：（笑）宵夜好了，快趁热吃！
Damián: (smiles) Your late-night snack is ready. Have it while it's hot!
△Eloísa一脸不耐烦地把汤摔到了地上。
Eloísa：谁家大晚上吃这个啊！你要是不想做就直说！
Eloísa: Who eats this at this time of the night? If you don't want to cook, just say so!
△Damián站在一旁手足无措地看着满地的汤汁和破碎的碗，有些尴尬。
Damián：（讨好）没事，不想吃我再做别的。你把你想吃的告诉我好吗？
Damián: (placating her) That's okay. If you don't want it, I'll make something else. Just tell me what you'd like.
△Eloísa把杂志摔到一边，恶狠狠地盯着Damián。
Eloísa：滚出去，自己想！
Eloísa: Get out. Figure it out yourself!
【闪回结束】
63-5日 内 Damián办公室
人物：Damián、Eloísa
△Damián打着游戏，非常专注。
△Eloísa低着头。
Eloísa（VO）：原来被人呼来喝去这么难受啊。
Eloísa (VO): So this is how awful it feels to be ordered around.
△Eloísa离开办公室。
△Damián看了眼手机时间，已经十二点了。
△Damián起身。
Damián：（开心）下班喽！
Damián: (cheerfully) Time to go home!
△Damián哼着歌离开办公室。

第六十四集
64-1日 内 地下车库
人物：Damián、Isadora
△Damián走到地下车库，看到一旁停着一辆红色跑车。
△Isadora坐在驾驶室，摇下车窗，朝着Damián灿烂一笑。
Isadora：帅哥，上车了。
Isadora: Hey, handsome. Hop in.
Damián：（开心）来了，亲爱的！
Damián: (cheerfully) Coming, honey!
△Damián坐上副驾。
△跑车离开车库。
64-2日 内 信息安全部
人物：Eloísa、员工
△员工们站床边，看着楼下街道上的红色跑车逐渐跑远，表情羡慕。
员工一（男）：总裁可以带老公回家诶，真羡慕啊，人的运气怎么能这么好！
Employee 1 (male): The CEO gets to take her husband home with her. I'm so jealous. How can one man be that lucky?
员工二：那也是Damián有能力啊，怎么不见总裁看上你呢。
Employee 2: Damián earned it. You don't see the CEO falling for you, do you?
△Eloísa站在角落，手里还抱着一个茶壶，眼神落寞。
64-3日 内 简陋公寓
人物：Eloísa、Julián、Mateo、Margarita
△Julián和Mateo站在简陋的公寓里，眼神嫌弃地打量着周围。
△公寓里只有一个破旧的二手沙发，客厅旁边就是没有隔断的满是油污的小型厨房。
△Margarita在一旁打扫卫生。
Julián：（嫌弃）这房子也太破旧了把！跟我以前住的房子比差太多了，根本住不了啊！
Julián: (disgusted) This place is falling apart! It's nothing like the house I used to live in. How are we supposed to stay here?
△Mateo撅着嘴，发着脾气。
Mateo：我不要在这住！这阿姨太穷了，我们回家吧！不和他们住一起！
Mateo: I don't want to live here! This lady is too poor. Let's go home! I don't want to stay with them!
△Margarita愤怒地摔下手里的抹布。
Margarita：那你走啊！赶紧滚出啊！
Margarita: Then leave! Get out right now!
△Julián皱眉愤怒地看着Margarita。
△Margarita冷嘲热讽。
Margarita：Julián，你不能指望我和Jairo养你和你儿子。我劝你赶紧找个工作，要不然就滚出去！
Margarita: Julián, you can't expect Jairo and me to support you and your son. Find a job or move out!
△Julián冷哼一声。
Julián：按照我的身份和实力找一个年薪百万的工作很容易。再说，你们也别觉得是我骗了你们，明明是你女儿主动扑上来的！
Julián: With my status and abilities, finding a job that pays a million pesos a year will be easy. And don't accuse me of deceiving you. Your daughter was the one who threw herself at me!
△Margarita气得脸色阴沉。
△Eloísa一脸疲倦地走了进来。
Eloísa：Julián，你不能一直拖着啊。我们公司还缺人，要不我去跟Damián说一声，希望他看在曾经的情分上……
Eloísa: Julián, you can't keep putting this off. Our company is hiring. Maybe I can talk to Damián and ask him to help, for old times' sake...
△Julián狠狠打了Eloísa一巴掌。
△Margarita震惊地捂住嘴。
Julián：（怒）Eloísa，你别以为我不知道你在想什么！你还做梦以为Damián会再要你吗？
Julián: (furious) Eloísa, I know exactly what you're thinking! Are you still dreaming that Damián will take you back?
△Eloísa低着头捂着脸，看不清表情。
△Julián讥讽。
Julián：这样吧，你要是能让Damián离婚也好，这样我就能去追求Isadora了。说不定爷爷就会原谅我，让我重回Salazar家，你也能和Damián重修旧好。非常完美。
Julián: Actually, if you can get Damián to divorce Isadora, even better. Then I can pursue her. Grandfather might forgive me and let me return to the Salazar family, and you can get back together with Damián. Everyone wins.
△Eloísa捂着脸。
Eloísa：（冷声）你打我？
Eloísa: (coldly) You just hit me?

第六十五集
65-1日 内 简陋公寓
人物：Eloísa、Julián、Mateo、Margarita
△Margarita愤怒地看着Julián。
Margarita：你敢打Eloísa？
Margarita: How dare you hit Eloísa?
△Julián看着Eloísa冷哼一声。
Julián：对啊，又不是第一次了，你能拿我怎么样？到时候我重回Salazar家你们还不是捧着我！
Julián: Yes. It wasn't the first time. What are you going to do about it? When I return to the Salazar family, you'll all be fawning over me again!
△Julián表情嚣张。
Julián：有本事你们跟我动手啊！一群废物！
Julián: Go ahead and try to fight me! You're all useless!
△Eloísa攥紧双拳，颤抖着身子，眼泪在眼中打转。
△Julián嗤了一声，转身上了楼。
Eloísa（VO）：（后悔）Julián，我真是瞎了眼会选你这样的男人！真是没用死了。
Eloísa (VO): (regretful) Julián, I must have been blind to choose a man like you. You're completely worthless.
65-2日 内 Elorza庄园餐厅
人物：Damián、Isadora、Samanta、Beatriz、Fernando
△几人坐在桌子旁吃着饭。
△Samanta起身给Damián倒了杯酒。
Samanta：（别扭）喝酒！这是我特意让人从酒庄里拿出来的，你尝尝。
Samanta: (awkwardly) Have a drink! I had someone bring this bottle from the vineyard especially for you. Try it.
△Damián愣住，看了眼Beatriz。
Damián：（惊）妈，这是什么情况？我害怕！
Damián: (startled) Mom, what is going on? I'm scared!
△Beatriz笑了笑。
Beatriz：她这是感谢你呢。
Beatriz: This is her way of thanking you.
△Damián吞了口唾沫，表情惶恐。
Damián：该不会是下毒了吧。
Damián: Are you sure it isn't poisoned?
△Samanta无语地翻了个白眼。
Samanta：我只是想谢谢你在体育馆帮了我，爱喝不喝。
Samanta: I only wanted to thank you for helping me at the arena. Drink it or leave it.
△Samanta坐了回去。
△Isadora好奇地看着Damián。
Isadora：对了，你什么时候会的跆拳道啊？听Samanta说还很厉害。
Isadora: By the way, when did you learn taekwondo? Samanta says you're very good.
△Samanta也期待地看着Damián。
△Damián赶紧思索。
Damián：（纠结）额，我获得了一个信物，然后就会了。
Damián: (hesitating) Well, I received a token, and then I suddenly knew how.
△众人表情无语。
Samanta：不愿意说就算了！渣男！
Samanta: Fine, don't tell us! What a jerk!
△Damián傻眼。
Damián（VO）：我咋就成渣男了。
Damián (VO): How did that make me a jerk?
△Fernando看着几人，表情感慨。
Fernando：你们能跟我这儿子相处得好我就放心了。我一直担心你们会不喜欢他。
Fernando: I'm relieved to see all of you getting along with my son. I was always worried you wouldn't accept him.
△Beatriz点头附和。
Beatriz：是啊，我们老了，Elorza家之后就靠你们了。
Beatriz: That's right. We're getting older. The Elorza family will be in your hands one day.
△Samanta不以为意。
Samanta：我的考验还没结束呢！能配上我姐姐的只能说超级英雄！
Samanta: My tests aren't over yet! Only a superhero is good enough for my sister!
△Isadora拍了一下Samanta。
Isadora：（宠溺）别做梦了！
Isadora: (indulgently) In your dreams!
△Fernando看着Damián，欲言又止。
Fernando：（纠结）Damián，明天是你爷爷的生日宴，他邀请我们了，明天跟我回去一趟吧。
Fernando: (uneasy) Damián, tomorrow is your grandfather's birthday. He invited us. Come with me.
△Damián瞬间冷脸，摇头。
Damián：（不开心）爸，他只喜欢那个没有血缘关系的女儿，你这个不受宠的亲儿子回去干嘛！
Damián: (upset) Dad, he only cares about that daughter who isn't even related to him. Why should his own son, the one he never favored, go back?
△Isadora、Samanta、Beatriz表情疑惑。
Isadora：Damián，什么叫没有血缘关系？
Isadora: Damián, what do you mean she isn't related to him?

第六十六集
66-1日 内 Elorza庄园餐厅
人物：Damián、Isadora、Samanta、Beatriz、Fernando
△Damián看了看Isadora，又看了看脸色凝重的父亲叹了口气。
Damián：我跟你们说。我这个爷爷年轻的时候二婚，可这个女人不喜欢我爸。爷爷就把爸赶出了家门。就连家里的所有东西都给了那个女人的女儿。
Damián: Let me explain. When my grandfather remarried, his new wife disliked my dad, so Grandfather threw him out. He even gave everything the family owned to that woman's daughter.
△Fernando低下头。
△Beatriz心疼地握住Fernando的手。
Damián：爸爸一直在外流浪，在垃圾桶里捡到了我，我们两个一直相依为命。
Damián: Dad wandered the streets for years. Then he found me beside a dumpster, and the two of us have depended on each other ever since.
△Isadora皱眉，拉住Damián的手。
△Damián朝Isadora一笑。
Damián：现在却突然让我们回去吃饭，真是荒谬！
Damián: And now, out of nowhere, they want us to go back for dinner. It's ridiculous!
△Fernando身后是呢他呢了口气。
Fernando：你爷爷当时只是鬼迷心窍，现在已经想要悔改了，我们就回去看看吧。
Fernando: Your grandfather lost his mind back then, but now he regrets it. Let's at least go see him.
Damián：（冷哼）回去也行，不过我是不会叫他的！
Damián: (scoffs) Fine, I'll go, but I won't call him Grandfather!
Isadora：要我陪你回去吗？毕竟我们是夫妻。
Isadora: Do you want me to come with you? We are husband and wife, after all.
△Damián摇了摇头。
Damián：这件事由我来处理，那帮人还没有资格见你。
Damián: I'll handle this myself. Those people don't deserve to meet you.
△Fernando尴尬一笑。
Fernando：没事，这是小事，我带Damián回去就好。
Fernando: It's all right. This is a minor family matter. I'll take Damián.
△Damián冷着脸没说话。
66-2日 内 Damián办公室
人物：Damián、Eloísa
△Damián坐在办公桌前打着游戏。
△Eloísa穿着紧身的白透衬衣走进了办公室，手里还拿着芝士蛋糕。
△Eloísa讨好地把蛋糕放到桌子上，推给Damián。
Eloísa：Damián，我知道你最喜欢吃这个牌子的芝士蛋糕，我特意给你买的。
Eloísa: Damián, I know this is your favorite brand of cheesecake, so I bought one especially for you.
△Damián扫了一眼蛋糕，轻笑了一声。
Damián：你拿走吧，我芝士过敏，你不知道吗？
Damián: Take it away. I'm allergic to cheese. Didn't you know that?
△Eloísa愣住。
Eloísa：不是，你不喜欢吃妈？我记得……
Eloísa: What? Don't you like it? I remember...
△Damián打断Eloísa。
Damián：喜欢吃的一直是你，我说了很多次我芝士过敏，你会听吗？
Damián: You're the one who has always liked it. I told you countless times that I'm allergic to cheese, but did you ever listen?
△Eloísa脸色尴尬。
Damián：说吧，你想干什么？是后悔了，还是想从我这获得什么？
Damián: Tell me what you want. Do you regret your choice, or are you trying to get something from me?
△Eloísa摆了摆手。
Eloísa：我没……
Eloísa: I wasn't...
Damián：（语气冷硬）没事就滚出去，别在这碍眼！不然就滚蛋！
Damián: (coldly) If you have nothing to say, get out of my sight! Or get out of this company altogether!
△Eloísa强忍泪水，转身离开。
Eloísa（VO）：Damián，你总有一天会明白的，我才是那个在原地等你的那个，你一定会和Isadora离婚的！
Eloísa (VO): Damián, one day you'll understand. I'm the one who has been here waiting for you. You will divorce Isadora!

第六十七集
67-1日 内 Fernando老家
人物：Damián、Fernando、Darío、Rosalinda、Bruno
△Darío坐在餐桌的主位，Rosalinda（中年女人，精明模样）和Bruno（青年男子，肥头大耳）坐在一旁，表情不耐烦。
△众人旁边还有两个空位。
△桌上摆着蛋糕、火鸡、千层面。
Darío：Fernando还没回来，我们在等一会。毕竟他那个儿子在大企业上班。【人名：Darío Cortés｜Damián爷爷】
[Name: Darío Cortés | Damián's grandfather]
Darío: Fernando still isn't here. Let's wait a little longer. After all, his son works for a big company. 
△Rosalinda冷笑一声。
Rosalinda：大企业上班又怎么样，能给我儿子介绍工作吗？要我说我儿子这么优秀，根本用不上他们！【字幕：Rosalinda Reyes ｜Fernando继姐】
[Name: Rosalinda Reyes | Fernando's stepsister]
Rosalinda: So what if he works for a major corporation? Can he get my son a job? Not that Bruno needs their help. My son is talented enough on his own! 
△Bruno附和点了点头。
Bruno：就是，爷爷太小瞧我了。【字幕：Bruno Reyes ｜Rosalinda儿子】
Bruno: Exactly. Grandpa, don't underestimate me. [Name: Bruno Reyes | Rosalinda's son]
△Darío摇了摇头。
Darío：Damián现在有能力了，帮你是应该的。更何况今天我过生日，他们不好拒绝我。
Darío: Damián is doing well now, so he should help you. Besides, today is my birthday. He won't be able to turn me down.
△门被敲响，Rosalinda不耐烦地起身开门。
△门被打开，Damián和Fernando站在门口。
Rosalinda：（不耐烦）大忙人终于回来了，赶紧进来吧。
Rosalinda: (impatiently) The big shot finally decided to show up. Get inside.
△Rosalinda刚要关门，就看到了门口停着的红色法拉利，瞪大了眼睛。
Rosalinda：（不可置信）Fernando，你发财了？都买得起法拉利了？
Rosalinda: (in disbelief) Fernando, did you strike it rich? You can afford a Ferrari now?
△Fernando尴尬摆手。
Fernando：那不是我的，是我儿子的。
Fernando: It isn't mine. It belongs to my son.
△Rosalinda语气嫉妒。
Rosalinda：真是厉害啊，有个好儿子。不过，这车是刷爆了信用卡才买下来的吧？
Rosalinda: Impressive. It must be nice to have such a successful son. But I bet he maxed out his credit cards to buy it.
△Damián不悦地看了眼Rosalinda。
Damián：（呛声）就算是刷爆信用卡也比在家里找不到工作强。
Damián: (shooting back) Even maxing out a credit card is better than living at home without a job.
△Rosalinda狠狠瞪了眼Damián。
Rosalinda：（怒）爸！你看他这个儿子，跟小时候一样招人讨厌！
Rosalinda: (furious) Dad! Look at his son! He's just as obnoxious as he was when he was little!
△Darío没有理会Rosalinda，直勾勾地盯着Damián手里的跑车钥匙。
Darío：Damián，听说你在Elorza公司当部长？那你帮你弟弟也找个工作吧。
Darío: Damián, I hear you're a director at Casa Elorza. Find a job for your cousin too.
△Fernando站在原地，不满地看着Darío。
Fernando：（呛声）爸，你干什么？那么大公司招人是有流程的，又不是Damián一个人说了算。
Fernando: (shooting back) Dad, what are you doing? A company that large has a formal hiring process. Damián can't decide everything by himself.
△Darío压住不满。
Darío：Fernando，我知道你怨恨当年我把你赶出去的事情，那还不是因为你调皮吗。
Darío: Fernando, I know you resent me for throwing you out all those years ago, but I only did it because you were unruly.
△Damián直接笑出了声。
Damián：调皮？难道不是你硬逼着爸空手去疏通马桶下水吗？
Damián: Unruly? Didn't you force Dad to unclog the toilet drain with his bare hands?
△Rosalinda恶狠狠地盯着Damián。
Rosalinda：Damián!你闭嘴！怎么跟你爷爷说话呢！
Rosalinda: Damián! Shut up! Is that any way to speak to your grandfather?

第六十八集
68-1日 内 Fernando老家
人物：Damián、Isadora、Fernando、Darío、Rosalinda、Bruno
△Damián冷笑着看向Rosalinda。
Damián：光顾着说他了，忘了说你了。你以为你就是什么好东西吗？当年联合其他人把我爸和一个二百多斤的智障关在一起的事你以为我不知道吗！
Damián: I was so busy talking about him that I almost forgot about you. You think you’re any better? You and the others locked my dad in a room with a mentally disabled man who weighed over 100 kilos. Did you really think I didn’t know?
△Rosalinda脸色一僵。
Damián：对了，后来我爸自己逃出来了，倒是某些人被迫关了进去。
Damián: Dad managed to escape, of course. Someone else ended up locked in there instead.
△Damián看向Bruno。
△Rosalinda脸色难看。
Damián：（点头认可）看着是和那大胖子像……
Damián: (nods) Now that you mention it, he does look like that big guy...
△Darío猛地拍桌站了起来。
Darío：（脸色难看）Damián！你说什么呢！你和你爸一样，就是满口谎言！
Darío: (darkening expression) Damián! What are you talking about? You're just like your father--full of lies!
△Darío瞪着Damián。
Darío：Fernando还说你结婚了，我看根本也是骗人！你这样的废物怎么可能有女孩会喜欢你！
Darío: Fernando also claimed you were married. I bet that was a lie too! What woman would ever want a loser like you?
△Damián脸色难看，抓住Fernando的手。
Damián：爸，我们走，不在这跟他们吃饭了！
Damián: Dad, let's go. They don't deserve us!
△一辆跑车刹车声响起，众人看向门口。
△Bruno盯着跑车目瞪口呆，口水都要流出来了。
Bruno：（惊）这是限量的跑车！全球限量！
Bruno: (shocked) That's a limited-edition supercar! There are only a handful in the world!
△Isadora迈着长腿从车上走了下来，下车后甩了甩秀发，气场强大。
△Damián愣住。
Damián：怎么回事？Isadora怎么来了？
Damián: What's going on? Why is Isadora here?
△Fernando到Damián耳边小声说到。
Fernando：Isadora问我位置，我就发给她了。
Fernando: Isadora asked me for the address, so I sent it to her.
△Rosalinda不可置信地看着Damián。
Rosalinda：什么？你老婆？你老婆能开得起限量跑车？别开玩笑了！
Rosalinda: What? Your wife? Your wife can afford a limited-edition supercar? Stop pretending!
△Damián无视Rosalinda，上前一步朝Isadora招手。
Damián：亲爱的，你怎么来了？
Damián: Honey, what are you doing here?
Isadora：Damián，你爷爷过生日，我应该陪你来的。
Isadora: Damián, it's your grandfather's birthday. I should be here with you.
△Damián自然地牵起Isadora的手。
Damián：走吧，我给你介绍一下，省得有些人说你是虚构的。
Damián: Come on. I'll introduce you so certain people can stop claiming that I made you up.
△Damián拉着Isadora走到餐桌旁边坐下。
△Darío尴尬地轻咳了一下。
Darío：真结婚了啊，怎么不告诉爷爷一下？
Darío: So you really are married. Why didn't you tell your grandfather?
△Rosalinda脸色难看。
△Bruno拽了拽Rosalinda。
Bruno：妈，这车不是豪门或者贵族买不起的！
Bruno: Mom, only the wealthiest families can afford a car like this!
△Rosalinda强装镇定。
Rosalinda：什么豪门！谁知道是不是二手的或者改装的！
Rosalinda: Wealthy? Who knows? It could be secondhand or fake!
△Rosalinda打量着Isadora，眼神轻蔑。
Rosalinda：Damián，你老婆是干什么的？跟你一起打工的吧。小心别被骗了！
Rosalinda: Damián, what does your wife do? Is she a worthless worker like you? Be careful she isn't scamming you!
△Damián刚要开口，被Isadora按住了手。
△Isadora看着Rosalinda，不卑不亢。
Isadora：我叫Isadora Elorza。Elorza家族的继承人，也是Elorza公司的总裁。
Isadora: My name is Isadora Elorza. I am the heir to the Elorza family and the CEO of Casa Elorza.
△Rosalinda和Darío、Bruno表情震惊，目瞪口呆。

第六十九集
69-1日 内 Fernando老家
人物：Damián、Isadora、Fernando、Darío、Rosalinda、Bruno
△Isadora和Damián十指紧握，眼神扫过Darío、Bruno和Rosalinda。
Isadora：我也是Damián的妻子，你们还有什么问题吗？本总裁时间很紧的，有问题我就一起回答了。
Isadora: I'm also Damián's wife. Any other questions? My time is valuable, I won't be here for too long.
△众人目瞪口呆。
△气氛一片寂静。
△Damián勾起嘴角。
Damián：亲爱的，这些人怎么不说话了？是哑巴吗？
Damián: Honey, why has everyone gone quiet? Did they lose their voices?
△Fernando也跟着附和。
Fernando：Rosalinda，怎么了？心情不好啊？那就多喝点酒，别给自己气死了。
Fernando: Rosalinda, what's wrong? Bad mood? Have another drink. Don't get too angry, it's bad for your health.
△Rosalinda和Darío被气得浑身发抖。
Darío：这就是你们跟家人说话的态度吗！
Darío: Is this how you speak to your family?
△Rosalinda愤怒地拍了一下桌子。
Rosalinda：这样吧，Damián。你让你老婆把她那个车给Bruno。再给Bruno安排个工作。刚入职不用安排多大官职，我感觉你那个职位就可以，之后再慢慢升。这样我们就原谅你们。
Rosalinda: Here's what we'll do, Damián. Have your wife give Bruno her car and arrange a job for him. He doesn't need a major title right away. Your position should be fine to start with, and then he can work his way up. Do that, and we'll forgive you.
△Darío认同地点了点头。
△Fernando不可置信地看着Rosalinda和Darío。
Fernando：（怒）你们知不知道你们在说什么？Isadora的车你们张嘴就要？
Fernando: (furious) Do you even hear yourselves? You simply demand Isadora's car the moment you see it?
△Rosalinda不以为意。
Rosalinda：都是一家人，给我们开开怎么了？我们还没嫌弃你们这车是二手的呢！
Rosalinda: We're family. What's wrong with letting us drive it? We haven't even complained that the car is probably used!
△Isadora愤怒地攥紧拳头，刚要发怒。
△Damián伸手拦住Isadora。
△Isadora震惊地看着Damián。
Damián：（笑）让老公来。
Damián: (smiles) Let your husband handle this.
△Damián起身，单手掀翻了餐桌。
Damián：都别吃了！
Damián: Nobody takes another bite!
△蛋糕、菜、盘子碎片撒了一地。
△Darío被吓得猛地起身。
△Rosalinda被吓得蜷缩在一边，抱着脑袋惨叫。
△Bruno直接被吓傻了。
△Rosalinda反应过来破口大骂。
Rosalinda：Damián!你这个疯子！你要干什么！
Rosalinda: Damián! You lunatic! What are you doing?
△Darío颤抖着手指着Damián。
Darío：（气急）Damián！好！你好得很啊！
Darío: (livid) Damián! Fine! So this is who you really are!
△Darío看向Fernando。
Darío：Fernando，你今天要么跟这个Damián断绝关系，要么跟他一起滚出去，再也别进我家的大门！
Darío: Fernando, either cut all ties with Damián today or leave with him and never set foot in my house again!
△Fernando怔愣地看着Darío，表情失望。
△Darío避开Fernando的视线，表情冷酷。
△整个房间的气愤剑拔弩张。

第七十集
70-1日 内 Fernando老家
人物：Damián、Isadora、Fernando、Darío、Rosalinda、Bruno
△Fernando笑了笑，看着Darío。
Fernando：爸，我妈死得早，我早就声Damián这个一个儿子了。我什么都给不了他，但还能保证不让他被人侮辱！
Fernando: Dad, my mother died young, and Damián is the only son I have. I may not be able to give him much, but I can make sure no one humiliates him!
△Darío被气得浑身颤抖。
Darío：你这个混蛋！
Darío: You ungrateful bastard!
△Damián和Isadora起身，Damián拽住Fernando。
Damián：爸，别跟他们争论了，我们走吧，这地方以后都别来了。
Damián: Dad, don't argue with them. Let's go. We never have to come back here again.
△Fernando点了点头。
Fernando：儿子你之前说得对，有些东西一旦失去了，就不会再有了。你爷爷在你奶奶去世的时候就已经消失了，现在的只剩一个空壳了。
Fernando: Son, you were right. Once some things are lost, they can never be recovered. Your granddad disappeared the day your grandma died. The man standing here now is just an empty shell.
△Fernando转身毫不犹豫地离开了。
△Damián和Isadora也快步离开，留下一地狼藉和呆在原地的三人。
70-2日 内 Elorza庄园客厅
人物：Damián、Isadora、Fernando
△Damián和Fernando推门走进客厅，Fernando心情不好，摆了摆手径直走上了楼。
△Damián看着Fernando的背影摇了摇头。
△戒指的虚影突然出现在Damián面前。
Damián（VO）：（惊）戒指出现才想起来今天还没签到呢！
Damián (VO): (startled) The Ring's appearance just reminded me--I haven't checked in today!
戒指（OS）：（低沉）命运节点已记录。节点：Elorza庄园客厅。命运馈赠——顶级赌神能力。从此之后，赌桌上你将无人能敌！
Ring (OS): (deep) Fate checkpoint registered. Location: the Elorza estate. Reward: Gambling Mastery. From now on, no one can beat you at the tables!
△一副扑克牌出现在空中，缓缓落下。
△Damián伸出手接住扑克牌。Damián接住扑克牌的瞬间，扑克牌化作星光环绕在Damián周围，最后消失。
△Damián表情奇怪。
Damián（VO）：赌博？这个能力不太好吧……
Damián (VO): Gambling? That doesn't sound like a good thing...
△Isadora推门走了进来，看到Damián一愣。
Isadora：Damián？你怎么还不进去？
Isadora: Damián? Why haven't you gone inside?
△Damián看向Isadora。
Damián：老婆，Elorza家以前跟是不是有什么赌博生意？
Damián: Honey, didn't the Elorza family once have some gambling-related business?
△Isadora神色一凛。
Isadora：你都知道了？
Isadora: You know about that?
△Damián不动声色。
Isadora：我们家之前确实有个分支接触过赌博。虽然刚开始赚了很多，但没撑过一代，第二代就开始赔钱，而且越赔越多。
Isadora: One branch of our family did get involved in gambling. They made a great deal of money at first, but it didn't last beyond the first generation. The second generation began losing, and the losses kept growing.
△Isadora低着头。
Isadora：刚才管家跟我说，他们今天来要钱了……
Isadora: The butler just told me that they came here today demanding money...
△Damián笑着抓住Isadora的手。
Damián：来就来。刚才你帮我了，那现在我也要帮你才行啊。
Damián: Let them. You stood up for me just now, so it's my turn to help you.
△Isadora感动地看着Damián，点了点头。
Isadora：好，那我们一起进去！
Isadora: All right. Let's go in together!

第七十一集
71-1日 内 Elorza庄园客厅
人物：Damián、Isadora、Facundo、Marisol
△两人刚走进去，就听到Facundo挑剔的声音传来。
Facundo（OS）：听说Isadora的老公是随便找的，那人不能是骗子吧！
Facundo (OS): I heard Isadora picked some random man to be her husband. What if he's a con artist?
△两人快步走进客厅。
△Facundo一身浮夸的西装，双腿搭在茶几上吃着水果。【人名：Facundo Elorza｜Isadora表哥】
Facundo Elorza | Isadora's cousin
△Marisol坐在一旁，眼珠一直在滴溜溜地转。【人名：Marisol Elorza｜Isadora阿姨】
Marisol Elorza | Isadora's aunt
△管家恭敬地站在一旁没有搭话。
△Isadora和Damián走了进来，Isadora摆了摆手，管家点头离开。
△Facundo看到Isadora一动没动，接着吃着水果。
△Marisol看到Isadora立刻起身凑了上去。
Marisol：Isadora，你回来了啊！
Marisol: Isadora, you're back!
△Marisol表情不满。
Marisol：不知道你妈在忙什么，都不出来见人！
Marisol: I don't know what your mother is so busy with. She won't even come out to see us!
Isadora：（不屑）是我不让她出来的。她那么心软，你来要钱，肯定就给你了。
Isadora: (dismissively) I told her not to come out. She's too softhearted. If you asked her for money, she would give it to you.
△Marisol脸色难看。
△Facundo不乐意地瞪向Isadora。
Facundo：Isadora，你什么意思？我是你哥！她是你阿姨，都是Elorza家的人，从自己家里拿点钱怎么了？
Facundo: What is that supposed to mean, Isadora? I'm your cousin, and she's your aunt! We're all Elorzas. What's wrong with taking a little money from our own family?
△Facundo看到一旁的Damián，目光不善地打量一番。
Facundo：你不给我们花，难道要留着给小白脸？
Facundo: If you won't spend it on us, are you saving it for your boy toy?
△Isadora护住Damián。
Isadora：（冷静沉声）要是我没记错的话，这二十年，你们家已经陆陆续续从庄园里拿出了将近一亿的钱，没错吧？
Isadora: (calm but stern) If I remember correctly, your family has taken nearly 100 million pesos from this estate over the last twenty years. Isn't that right?
△Facundo和Marisol一听到数目脸色立刻变得怪异。
△Damián震惊地瞪大了眼睛。
Damián（OS）：（不可置信）一个亿！Elorza家真有钱啊！
Damián (OS): (in disbelief) A hundred million pesos! The Elorza family really is loaded!
△Facundo张嘴想辩解。
△Isadora眼神犀利地看向Facundo。
Isadora：别想狡辩，庄园对于每一笔支出都会记录的！
Isadora: Don't try to deny it. The estate keeps a record of every expense!
△Facundo张了张嘴没说出话。
△Marisol不乐意地站了出来。
Marisol：Isadora，你说这话我就不愿意听了，你爷爷都没意见，你凭什么拦我？更何况这次我们只要一百万而已！这对你来说就是很小的一笔钱，你连这点小事都不愿意帮忙？
Marisol: Isadora, I don't appreciate that tone. Your grandfather never objected, so what gives you the right to stop me? Besides, we're only asking for one million pesos this time! That's pocket change to you. You won't even help us with something this small?
△Isadora看着Marisol冷哼一声。
Isadora：这些年我们家也算仁至义尽了，再说，你们上次来的时候我们就说清楚了，绝对不会再给你们一点钱！你们现在赶紧离开我家！
Isadora: Our family has already done more than enough for you. And the last time you were here, we made it clear that you would never receive another peso from us! Leave my home now!
△Marisol的脸色越来越难看，冷哼一声翘起二郎腿。
Marisol：（怒）你们守着那些钱有什么用？就不怕最后都被某些人抢走吗？我们起码是亲戚，给我们总比给陌生人要好！
Marisol: (furious) What good does hoarding all that money do you? Aren't you afraid someone else will take it all in the end? At least we're family. Better us than a stranger!
△Isadora脸色难看。

第七十二集
72-1日 内 Elorza庄园客厅
人物：Damián、Isadora、Facundo、Marisol
△Damián适时开口。
Damián：我花我老婆的钱怎么了？你嫉妒的话也让你儿子找个有钱的老公啊。
Damián: What's wrong with spending my wife's money? If you're jealous, tell your son to find himself a rich husband too.
△Marisol被气得猛地站起身，浑身颤抖。
Marisol：真是没教养！
Marisol: You have no manners!
Damián：对啊，但我再没教养也不会到别人家伸手要钱。
Damián: That's true, but even I wouldn't come into someone else's home with my hand out.
△Marisol被气得面红耳赤。
△Facundo猛地起身，朝Isadora跪了下去，双手合十。
△Isadora被吓了一跳，猛地后退一步。
△Damián上前伸手护住Isadora。
Facundo：（崩溃）Isadora这次你真的要帮帮我！求求你，我借了高利贷，要是这一百万还不上的话他们真的会杀了我！
Facundo: (breaking down) Isadora, you have to help me this time! Please! I borrowed from loan sharks. If I don't repay this one million pesos, they'll kill me!
△Isadora皱眉看着Facundo。
Isadora：（生冷）我不是我妈，卖卖惨就能让我心软。如果你需要工作我可以帮你介绍。但钱的话免谈！更何况连高利贷你都敢碰，说明你已经没救了。
Isadora: (coldly) I'm not my mother. A sob story won't soften me. If you need a job, I can help you find one. But money is out of the question! The fact that you went to loan sharks proves you're beyond saving.
△Facundo爬着向前想抓住Isadora的腿。
Facundo：表妹！帮帮我！求求你！如果你不帮我，那我就死给你看！
Facundo: Cousin! Help me! Please! If you don't, I'll kill myself!
△Marisol在一旁帮腔。
Marisol：Isadora，难道你就这么冷血？看你的哥哥去死吗！
Marisol: Isadora, are you really so heartless that you'll stand by and watch your cousin die?
△Damián头疼地揉了揉太阳穴。
Damián（VO）：不对，他不是喜欢赌吗？刚好戒指给了我赌王的能力，干脆用赌局把他们整走吧！
Damián (VO): Wait. Doesn't he love to gamble? The Ring just gave me the skill of a master gambler. I'll use a game to make sure they never come back!
△Damián挡在Isadora身前，居高临下地看着Facundo。
Damián：阿姨和表哥对吧？这样，我可以给你们钱。
Damián: So you're my aunt and cousin? All right. I can give you the money.
△Isadora震惊地拽了拽Damián。
△Facundo兴奋地蹦了起来。
Facundo：好啊！好啊！
Facundo: Yes! That's great!
Damián：（比三）我可以给你三百万。
Damián: (holds up three fingers) I can give you three million pesos.
Isadora：（不可置信）你干什么？这种赌鬼你还要帮他？你这是要害死他啊！
Isadora: (in disbelief) What are you doing? You're going to help a gambling addict? You'll destroy him!
△Damián示意Isadora放心。
△Facundo激动地握住Damián的手。
Facundo：你真是我的救命恩人啊！我们以后一定要常联系！
Facundo: You saved my life! We should stay in touch from now on!
△Damián甩开Facundo的手。
Damián：但我有一个条件。那就是我有三百万本金，你也有三百万本金，咱们对赌。如果你输完了，那之后就不要再来Elorza庄园，但你要是赢了，这六百万你全部拿走。
Damián: But on one condition. I'll start with three million pesos, and so will you. We'll gamble against each other. If you lose it all, you can never come back to the Elorza estate. But if you win, you take all six million.
△Facundo激烈点头。
Facundo：好好好！Damián，你早说你也是同道中人啊！敢跟我赌一会可别哭啊！哈哈啊哈！
Facundo: Deal! Damián, you should've told me you were a fellow gambler! Don't start crying when you lose! Ha-ha-ha!

第七十三集
73-1日 内 Elorza庄园客厅
人物：Damián、Isadora、Facundo、Marisol
△Damián和Facundo分别站在茶几的两侧，Marisol和Isadora坐在一旁的沙发上。
△Damián拿出一副扑克牌。
△Damián熟练地洗着牌，Isadora震惊地看着Damián。
Facundo：（嗤笑）假把式。
Facundo: (scoffs) All show, no skill.
△Damián没说话，把牌递给Isadora。
Damián：我们也不玩难的，让Isadora洗牌发牌，第一轮我们比大小。
Damián: Let's keep it simple. Isadora will shuffle and deal, and for the first round, the high card wins.
△Facundo激动点头。
Facundo：行，这个简单！
Facundo: Fine. That's easy!
△Damián盯着Facundo勾起嘴角。
Damián：这一把，我出五十万。
Damián: I'm betting 500,000 pesos on this hand.
△Damián拿出五十万的筹码。
Facundo：（兴奋）这么大？行！我跟你！
Facundo: (excited) That much? Fine! I'll match it!
△Facundo也拿出五十万。
△Damián看向Isadora。
Damián：发牌吧。
Damián: Deal the cards.
△Isadora点头，打乱了扑克牌，一人发了三张。
△Damián看了眼自己的牌，分别是红桃3、方块6和黑桃5。
△Damián又看向Facundo手里的牌。
△牌突然变成了透视模样，分别是黑桃9、方块10和黑桃A。
△Facundo表情开心，恶狠狠地盯着Damián，倒扣着推出黑桃A的那张牌。
Facundo：Damián，这一场我就不放水了。
Facundo: Damián, I won't go easy on you this time.
△Damián毫不在意地倒扣着推出了方块6。
△Isadora看了看Facundo，又看了看Damián。
Isadora：双方一起揭开排面。
Isadora: Both players, turn over your cards.
△Damián和Facundo同时翻牌，Facundo和Marisol同时大笑。
Facundo：哈哈哈！这一百万我就收下了！
Facundo: Ha-ha! This million pesos is mine!
△Facundo伸手拿过桌上的筹码。
Marisol：（得意）还是我儿子厉害！轻轻松松赚了五十万。
Marisol: (smugly) That's my son! He just made 500,000 pesos without breaking a sweat.
△Damián表情毫无变化。
△Isadora担忧。
Damián：没事，还有下一把呢，再来。
Damián: It's all right. There's another hand. Let's go again.
△Damián看着Facundo。
Damián：下一把还是比大小，我出一百万，你敢不敢跟？
Damián: High card again. I'm betting one million pesos. Can you match it?
△Facundo表情癫狂。
Facundo：当然！谁怕谁！
Facundo: Of course! I'm not afraid of you!
△Isadora又给两人分发了三张牌。
△Damián看牌，分别是梅花7、梅花9、黑桃8。
△Damián看向正在偷偷摸摸看牌的Facundo。
△排面上分别是红桃A、方块7和梅花A。
△Facundo哈哈大笑。
Facundo：我真是想输都难啊！Damián，这把的钱我也收下了！
Facundo: I couldn't lose if I tried! Damián, I'll take the money from this hand too!
△Facundo大笑着推出红桃A。
△Damián推出梅花9.
Isadora：双方翻牌。
Isadora: Both players, turn over your cards.
△两人分别亮出牌。
△Facundo和Marisol直接激动地挑了起来。
Facundo：哈哈哈！又赢了！三百万了！哈哈哈！
Facundo: Ha-ha! I won again! Three million pesos! Ha-ha-ha!
△Marisol激动地鼓掌。
Marisol：我儿子太厉害了！哈哈哈！
Marisol: My son is incredible! Ha-ha-ha!
△Damián表情淡淡的，把筹码推给Facundo。
Damián：再来最后一把，我梭哈！
Damián: One last hand. I'm all in!

第七十四集
74-1日 内 Elorza庄园客厅
人物：Damián、Isadora、Facundo、Marisol
△Isadora震惊地抓住Damián的手。
Isadora：Damián，到此为止吧，大不了我把钱给他们。
Isadora: Damián, stop now. I would rather give them the money.
△Damián刚要开口，Facundo出声阻拦。
Facundo：去去去！别在这耽误我挣钱！
Facundo: Get out of here! Don't get in the way of me making money!
△Damián盯着Facundo，表情隐晦不定。
Facundo：（得意）下一把还是比大小吗？我也梭哈！
Facundo: (smugly) High card again? I'm all in too!
△Facundo把筹码全部推到了桌面上。
△Marisol出声阻拦。
Marisol：真要把这四百五全赌上吗？
Marisol: Are you really going to bet all 4.5 million pesos?
△Facundo有些疑虑。
Damián：这样吧，翻倍，这把我要是输了给你我现在筹码的两倍，也就是三百万。当然你要是输了，也要给我四百五十万的双倍，也就是九百万，怎么样？
Damián: Let's double the stakes. If I lose this hand, I'll pay you twice the value of my chips--three million pesos. If you lose, you pay me twice your 4.5 million--nine million pesos. What do you say?
△Facundo红了眼。
Facundo：来！一会你可别哭鼻子！
Facundo: Deal! Just don't cry when you lose!
△Facundo看向Isadora。
Facundo：Isadora发牌！
Facundo: Isadora, deal the cards!
△Isadora张嘴想劝阻，最后还是发了牌。
△Damián看向手里的三张牌，分别是方片A、方片J、方片Q。
△Damián看向Facundo手里的牌，透视出来的分别是梅花K、红桃K和黑桃K。
△Facundo看了眼牌，表情异常兴奋。
Facundo（VO）：刚才那两把我已经拿到了三张A，就Damián的手气肯定拿不到最后一张A，我赢定了！
Facundo (VO): I already drew three aces in the last two hands. With Damián's luck, there's no way he drew the last one. This hand is mine!
Facundo：哈哈哈！我这拿什么输！Damián准备好你的钱吧！哈哈哈哈！
Facundo: Ha-ha! How could I possibly lose? Damián, get my money ready! Ha-ha-ha-ha!
△Facundo随手推出黑桃K。
△Damián似笑非笑地推出方片A。
Damián（VO）：终于等到鱼咬钩了。
Damián (VO): The fish has finally taken the bait.
Isadora：双方翻牌。
Isadora: Both players, turn over your cards.
△Facundo猖狂地翻过牌摔在桌子上。
Facundo：黑桃K！你拿什么打我！
Facundo: King of spades! You can't beat that!
△Damián慢条斯理翻牌露出了方片A。
△Facundo顿时傻了眼。
△Isadora和Marisol也震惊地张大了嘴巴。
△Damián表情波澜不惊。
Facundo：（不可置信）这，真不可能！你倒霉了那么多把，怎么这把把唯一的方片A抽到了？你出老千！
Facundo: (in disbelief) This can't be happening! You had terrible luck in every other hand. How did you draw the only ace of diamonds? You cheated!
△Facundo指着Damián。
Facundo：你跟Isadora串通好了！你出老千！
Facundo: You and Isadora planned this together! You cheated!
△Marisol脸色惨白。
Marisol：（惊恐）竟然一把就把四百多万输没了，还倒欠了九百多万……
Marisol: (terrified) He lost more than four million pesos in one hand and still owes over nine million pesos...
△Damián淡淡地看着Facundo。
Damián：愿赌服输，况且你妈一直坐在Isadora旁边，Isadora动没动手脚她应该看得很清楚。
Damián: A bet is a bet. Besides, your mother sat beside Isadora the entire time. She had a clear view and would have seen if Isadora tampered with anything.
△Facundo浑身瘫软跌坐在地上。
Facundo：（呆滞）完了，全完了……
Facundo: (dazed) It's over. I've lost everything...

第七十五集
75-1日 内 Elorza庄园客厅
人物：Damián、Isadora、Facundo、Marisol、保安
△Damián起身走到Facundo面前。
Damián：愿赌服输，除掉我给你的本钱，就算你欠我们家七百万吧。按照我们之前约定的，从今以后你们母子不许再出现在 Elorza庄园。
Damián: A bet is a bet. After deducting the three million pesos I gave you to start, you owe our family seven million. And as agreed, neither you nor your mother may ever set foot on the Elorza estate again.
△Damián眼神狠戾。
Damián：听到了没有！
Damián: Did you hear me?
△Facundo身子一颤。
Facundo：（浑浑噩噩）我知道了……
Facundo: (dazed) I understand...
△Marisol眉头一皱，刚想开口。
Isadora：（高声）管家！带人把他俩赶出去！以后不允许再放进庄园！
Isadora: (loudly) Butler! Have them thrown out! They are never to be admitted to this estate again!
△两个保安上前，押着Facundo和Marisol走了出去。
△Damián松了口气瘫坐在沙发上。
△Isadora审视地看着Damián，抬腿骑坐在Damián身上，伸手抵住Damián的胸膛。
Isadora：你早就设好了局？
Isadora: You planned this from the beginning, didn't you?
△Damián拉起Isadora的手，笑了笑。
Damián：是啊。这两个人就是卖狗皮膏药。给了一次钱就会有第二次。最好的办法就是一次性解决。下次他们再敢来，就让他们先把这五百万还了。
Damián: I did. Those two are leeches. Give them money once, and they'll keep coming back for more. The best solution was to end it once and for all. If they dare return, tell them to repay the five million pesos first.
△Isadora挑起Damián的下巴。
Isadora：有时候我真觉得你身上挺多秘密的。居然连出老千都会。
Isadora: Sometimes I think you're hiding a great many secrets. You even know how to stack the deck.
△Isadora伸手把Damián袖子里的几张扑克牌A抽了出来。
Damián：亲爱的，你放心，我这辈子只赌一次。
Damián: Don't worry, honey. I'll only gamble once in my life.
△Isadora挑眉。
Isadora：哦？刚才的那一次？
Isadora: Oh? You mean just now?
△Damián笑着摇了摇头。
Damián：当然不是。我人生最大的豪赌就是跟我美丽的老婆结婚。
Damián: Of course not. The biggest gamble of my life was marrying my beautiful wife.
△Isadora冷哼。
Isadora：哦？那你是不是后悔了？
Isadora: Oh? Do you regret it?
△Isadora不开心地起身，要离开。
Isadora：如果你后悔了现在走也来得及。
Isadora: If you do, there's still time to leave.
△Damián笑着一把抓住Isadora的手，将她拉了回来。
Damián：（笑）你想得美！
Damián: (smiles) Nice try!
△Damián把Isadora压在沙发上，抚摸着Isadora的脸，眼神眷恋。
△Damián摸着Isadora的脸亲了上去。
△Isadora搂住Damián的脖子。
△Samanta从外面走了进来，看到沙发上的两个人，捂着眼睛大叫。
Samanta：啊！你们在干嘛！
Samanta: Ah! What are you doing?
△Isadora推开Damián，脸红。
△Damián有些尴尬。
△Isadora看着Samanta有些嗔怒。
Isadora：你不会假装没看到啊！
Isadora: Couldn't you pretend you didn't see us?
△Samanta无语。
Samanta：家里还有别人呢！你们赶紧回房间！
Samanta: There are other people in this house! Go to your room!
△Samanta红着脸跑上楼。
△Isadora撒娇似的打了Damián一下。
△Damián笑了一下，公主抱起Isadora，亲了Isadora一口。
Damián：算了，我们回房继续。
Damián: Fine. Let's go back to our room and continue.

第七十六集
76-1日 内 卧室
人物：Damián、Isadora
△Damián光着膀子从床上起来，表情酸爽，不停地揉着自己的腰。
Damián：（吸气）这年纪大的女人就是不好整啊！也太凶悍了！
Damián: (inhales sharply) Older women are hard to handle. She's terrifying!
△Isadora穿着浴衣从套房里的卫生间里走了出来，靠在门框上冷冷地盯着Damián。
Isadora：说什么呢？
Isadora: What did you say?
△Damián被吓了一跳，连忙摆手。
Damián：没什么。
Damián: Nothing.
△Isadora冷哼一声。
Isadora：一起去公司？
Isadora: Want to go to the office together?
Damián：（臭屁）好嘞！感谢亲爱的送我上班！
Damián: (smugly) Absolutely! Thank you for driving me to work, honey!
△Isadora翻了个白眼。
Isadora：赶紧收拾！
Isadora: Hurry up and get ready!
Damián：（笑）好！
Damián: (smiles) Okay!
76-2日 内 信息安全部
人物：Damián、Eloísa、员工
△员工们在信息安全部上着班。
△Eloísa老老实实地坐在工位上。
△Damián一脸开心哼着歌走了进来。
Damián：（开心）大家检查一下公司的网络安全，最近不要松懈！
Damián: (cheerfully) Everyone, run a full check of the company's network security. Stay alert over the next few days!
△员工们点了点头。
△Eloísa看着开心的Damián，咬住了嘴唇。
△Damián开心地走进了办公室。
△Eloísa手机响起，是Julián的电话。
△Eloísa看着来电显示，皱眉，表情抗拒，纠结了一下，拿起手机出了办公室。
76-3日 内 公司走廊
人物：Eloísa
△Eloísa站在窗边接电话。
Eloísa：（皱眉）你给我打电话干嘛？
Eloísa: (frowning) Why are you calling me?
△Julián阴狠的声音从电话那边传来。
Julián（OS）：现在我交给你一件事，你必须做好！做好了，我们就能重回Salazar家！
Julián (OS): I have a job for you, and you have to get it right! If you do, we can return to the Salazar family!
△Eloísa震惊地瞪大了眼睛。
Eloísa：重回Salazar家？真的吗！我们还能回去吗？
Eloísa: Return to the Salazar family? Really? We can go back?
Julián（OS）：当然，还要你配合我。
Julián (OS): Of course, but I'll need your help.
△Eloísa四下看了看，压低声音。
Eloísa：怎么配合？
Eloísa: What do you need me to do?
76-4日 内 小黑屋
人物：Julián
△Julián坐在电脑前，电脑上是Damián的照片。
△Julián眼神狠戾地盯着电脑，手上拿着电话。
Julián：（偏执）爷爷把我们赶出来的原因就是Damián！他多次让Salazar家尴尬，所以只要报复了他，爷爷就会开心，我们就能回去了！
Julián: (obsessively) Grandfather threw us out because of Damián! He humiliated the Salazar family again and again. If we take revenge on him, Grandfather will be pleased and let us return!
△Julián面露畅想。
Julián：黑客我已经找好了，你只要配合我在Elorza公司的内网制造一个小漏洞，等黑客成功入侵，我们就能回到Salazar家了！你就又是豪门了！
Julián: I've already hired a hacker. All you have to do is create a small vulnerability in Casa Elorza's internal network. Once the hacker gets in, we can return to the Salazar family, and you'll be rich again!
△Eloísa没说话。
Julián：（催促）你还在想什么！
Julián: (pressing her) What are you waiting for?
Eloísa（VO）：（纠结）可要是我被发现了，是要被开除的……Julián，你想过失败后我会被怎么样吗？
Eloísa (VO): (conflicted) But if they catch me, I'll be fired... Julián, have you even thought about what will happen to me if this fails?

第七十七集
77-1日 内 小黑屋
人物：Julián
△Julián表情不耐烦。
Julián：你怕什么？就算被发现了不是还有我吗！爷爷知道我们做的事肯定也会帮我们的！你难道不想回到Salazar家吗！
Julián: What are you afraid of? Even if they catch you, you'll still have me! Once Grandfather realizes what we did, he'll help us. Don't you want to return to the Salazar family?
△Eloísa沉默一瞬。
Eloísa（VO）：（语气坚定）好！Julián，我答应你！你需要我怎么做？
Eloísa : (firmly) Fine! Julián, I'll do it! What do you need me to do?
△Julián挂了电话，盯着电脑上Damián的照片，咬牙狂笑。
Julián：Damián，我看你这会拿什么跟我争！
Julián: Damián, let's see how you compete with me now!
77-2日 内 信息安全部
人物：Eloísa、员工
△员工们坐在椅子上工作，办公室里全是键盘敲击的声音。
△Eloísa坐在工位上，鬼鬼祟祟地扫视了一圈，发现没人关注她。
△Eloísa在键盘上敲击了几下，电脑弹出一个窗口。
△Eloísa的手搭在鼠标上，表情纠结。
Eloísa（OS）：Damián，这都是你逼我的，我必须回到Salazar家！
Eloísa (OS): Damián, you forced me to do this. I have to get back into the Salazar family!
△Eloísa一咬牙，按下了删除键。
△Eloísa拿出手机给Julián发了一条消息，消息内容：OK。
77-3日 内 Damián办公室
人物：Damián
△Damián坐在电脑前。
△突然一声闷响，电脑开始出现乱码。
△Damián震惊地看着电脑屏幕。
Damián：（不可置信）糟了！是黑客入侵！我可是设置过新的防火墙的！
Damián: (in disbelief) Damn! We've been hacked! But I installed a new firewall!
△Damián猛地起身推门走了出去。
77-4日 内 信息安全部
人物：Damián、Eloísa、员工
△办公室里员工们的电脑也都被乱码占据。
△员工们表情慌乱。
△整个办公室全是疯狂敲击键盘的声音。
△Damián愤怒地推开门。
Damián：（怒）什么情况！这到底是怎么回事？
Damián: (furious) What is happening? How did this happen?
△员工们表情慌乱。
员工一：（焦头烂额）对不起，我们正在处理！请再给我们一点时间！
Employee 1: (frantic) I'm sorry. We're working on it! Please give us a little more time!
员工二：（欲言又止）Damián，这次黑客的进攻跟之前不一样，对手似乎知道我们的弱点一样，瞬间就击溃了我们的防火墙。
Employee 2: (hesitating) Damián, this attack is different from the last one. The hacker seems to know our weaknesses. They breached the firewall almost instantly.
△Damián皱眉。
Damián：（不可置信）什么？难道我们有内鬼？
Damián: (in disbelief) What? Do we have someone on the inside?
△Eloísa立刻低下了头。

第七十八集
78-1日 内 信息安全部
人物：Damián、Eloísa、员工
△Damián就近走到一个员工身后。
Damián：（干脆）你起来，我来看看！
Damián: (decisively) Move. Let me take a look!
△员工立刻让开身子，Damián坐了下去。
△其他的员工也纷纷看向Damián。
△Eloísa也盯着Damián，攥紧了拳头，表情紧张。
△Damián敲击着键盘，眼睛死死盯着电脑。
Damián：我知道了，这是利用了WEB的漏洞，然后入侵进来之后就俗套了。
Damián: I see. They exploited a web vulnerability to get in. Once they breached the network, the rest was pretty standard.
△员工们目瞪口呆地看着Damián的操作。
员工一：（惊）BUG在逐渐被修复。
Employee 1: (startled) The bug is being patched!
△Damián敲下了一串代码，刚要按下回车键，手突然僵住。
Damián（VO）：让我来看看你们是什么人！
Damián (VO): Now let's see who you really are!
△Damián删除了几行代码，又敲了新的输入了进去。
员工二：（惊呼）部长这是什么意思？为什么还要把BUG暴露在对手面前？
Employee 2: (shocked) What is the director doing? Why is he exposing the vulnerability to the attacker again?
员工三：（震惊）对手会盯着这个BUG疯狂进攻的啊！难道他是故意的？
Employee 3: (stunned) They'll keep hammering that vulnerability! Is he doing this on purpose?
△Damián看着电脑上的代码变换，勾起嘴角。
Damián：（自信）成了！
Damián: (confidently) Got them!
△Eloísa突然提起一口气，表情紧张。
Eloísa（VO）：什么成了？我怎么有种不好的预感？
Eloísa (VO): What did he get? Why do I have such a bad feeling about this?
△Damián笑着按下了回车键，电脑屏幕上出现一个文件夹。
Damián：（自信）对方的ip，使用的设备，部分个人信息，我已经完成了反盗取，现在看你们怎么跟我斗！
Damián: (confidently) I have their IP address, device information, and some personal data. The counter-trace is complete. Let's see how they fight me now!
△众人震惊地张大了嘴巴，紧接着爆发出一阵阵欢呼声。
△Eloísa脸色惨白。
Damián：（笑）接下来就是把这些东西交给警察了。
Damián: (smiles) Now we hand all of this over to the police.
△Damián起身，松了口气。
Damián：解决了。
Damián: Problem solved.
△打印机传来打印的声音，一个员工把一沓文件递给Damián。
员工：部长，这是你盗取的对方的文件。
Employee: Director, these are the files you extracted from the attacker.
△Damián接过，看了几眼，脸色逐渐落了下来。
△Eloísa被吓得瑟瑟发抖。
△Damián冷着脸看向Eloísa。
Damián：Eloísa，我以为你只是对感情不忠，没想到你连基本的职业道德都没有！
Damián: Eloísa, I thought you were only unfaithful in love. I never imagined you had no professional integrity either!
△众人震惊地看向Eloísa。
△Eloísa强装镇定，摆了摆手，勉强扯出笑容。
Eloísa：Damián，你在说什么啊？我听不懂啊，我什么都没干啊。
Eloísa: Damián, what are you talking about? I don't understand. I didn't do anything.
△Damián讥讽一笑。
Damián：我没说你干了什么吧？
Damián: I never said you did anything, did I?
△Eloísa的笑僵在脸上。
△Damián慢条斯理地整理文件，从里面抽出一张，上面是一张照片，照片内容是Eloísa在修改内网时电脑对Eloísa面容抓拍。
△Damián把照片摔在桌上，Eloísa被吓了一激灵。
Damián：刚才是一场酣畅淋漓的防攻战，可惜，我们之间出现了叛徒。
Damián: That was an intense cyber battle. Unfortunately, there is a traitor among us.
△众人看着Eloísa窃窃私语。
员工一：没想到她竟然是这样的人。
Employee 1: I never thought she was capable of something like this.
员工二：应该是她自己想当豪门没当上，所以就怨恨Damián呗。
Employee 2: She failed to marry into a wealthy family, so she must blame Damián for it.
员工三：这太吓人了，赶紧开除她吧，真让人恶心！
Employee 3: This is terrifying. Fire her now! She's disgusting!
△Eloísa脸色惨白。
Eloísa：（装委屈）我没有！Damián，我不知道你是什么时候截的图，但我真的没有！Damián就算我们分手，你也不能污蔑我啊！
Eloísa: (pretending to be hurt) I didn't do it! Damián, I don't know when you took those screenshots, but it wasn't me! We may have broken up, but that doesn't give you the right to frame me!
△Damián无奈地看着Eloísa，表情失望。
Damián：Eloísa，承认自己的错误很难吗？难道非要我把你整个作案过程公布出来你才满意吗？
Damián: Eloísa, is admitting your mistake really that difficult? Do I have to show everyone exactly how you committed the crime?

第七十九集
79-1日 内 信息安全部
人物：Damián、Eloísa、员工
△Eloísa还在强撑。
△Damián耸了耸肩。
Damián：算了，我直接报警吧。
Damián: Forget it. I'll call the police.
△Damián刚拿出手机，Eloísa直接扑到Damián脚边，抱着Damián的大腿。
Eloísa：对不起！我错了！我只是一时鬼迷心窍被Julián诱惑了！都是Julián让我这么做的！是他跟我说这么做就能回到Salazar家的。Damián，都是Julián害我的！
Eloísa: I'm sorry! I was wrong! I lost my head and let Julián manipulate me! He made me do it! He said it was the only way back into the Salazar family. Damián, this is all Julián's fault!
△Eloísa抓着Damián的腿痛哭流涕。
Eloísa：都是Julián！都是他挑拨我们之间的关系，我们才会到今天这个地步！
Eloísa: It was all Julián! He drove us apart. He's the reason we ended up like this!
△Damián厌恶地踹开Eloísa。
Damián：Eloísa，你还是这样！习惯性推卸责任！明明是你贪心，却非说是别人的错！滚开！
Damián: Eloísa, you haven't changed at all! You always blame everyone else. This happened because of your own greed, yet you still insist it was someone else's fault! Get out of my way!
△Damián拨打电话。
Damián：是警局吗？我这里有商业间谍，麻烦你们派人来一下吧。
Damián: Is this the police? We have a case of corporate espionage. Please send officers to our office.
△Eloísa脸色一白，晕了过去。
79-2日 内 小黑屋
人物：Julián、警察
△Julián得意扬扬地坐在小黑屋里看着电脑。
Julián：Damián，这会你死定了！哈哈哈！
Julián: Damián, you're finished this time! Ha-ha-ha!
△Julián拿起手机表情不耐烦。
Julián：怎么回事？黑客不回消息，Eloísa也不回消息，他们在干嘛？
Julián: What's going on? The hacker isn't responding, and neither is Eloísa. What are they doing?
△小黑屋的大门突然被踹开，警察拿着枪冲了进来，二话不说就把Julián压在桌子上。
警察：（怒吼）不许动！
Police Officer: (shouting) Don't move!
△Julián浑身颤抖着要挣扎。
Julián：（怒）你们知道我爷爷是谁吗？Alberto Salazar!你们敢动我，我爷爷不会放过你们的！
Julián: (furious) Do you know who my grandfather is? Alberto Salazar! If you touch me, he will make you pay!
△警察没有理会Julián，拿出手铐把挣扎的Julián铐住了。
△Julián表情慌乱。
Julián：你们不能抓我！你们不能！
Julián: You can't arrest me! You can't!
△Julián被警察拖着拽了出去。
79-3日 内 Salazar庄园书房
人物：Damián、Isadora、Alberto、管家
△Damián和Isadora坐在一起。
△Alberto坐在桌子后面。
△管家恭敬地站在一旁。
△Alberto盯着Damián，眼神有些复杂。
△管家轻咳一声，Alberto回过神来。
Alberto：这次Julián能做出这样的事我是真的不知道。他的所作所为都是自发地，与我们Salazar家无关。
Alberto: I truly had no idea Julián was capable of doing this. He acted entirely on his own. The Salazar family had nothing to do with it.
△Isadora冷着脸。
Isadora：这不是他能随意进攻我们公司防火墙的原因。
Isadora: That does not excuse his attack on our company's firewall.
△Isadora盯着Alberto。
Isadora：Salazar先生，我很尊重你，但要是Damián再晚一点解决BUG那对我们公司将会造成不可挽回的损失！
Isadora: Mr. Salazar, I respect you, but if Damián had taken even a little longer to patch that vulnerability, our company would have suffered irreparable damage!
△Alberto一时语塞，从抽屉里拿出一沓文件推给Isadora。
Alberto：这是我们Salazar家的诚意，你们可以看看。
Alberto: This is a gesture of good faith from the Salazar family. Please take a look.
△Isadora接过文件，文件上写的是葡萄山庄地皮转让。
Isadora：（震惊）这是做什么？
Isadora: (shocked) What is this?


第八十集
80-1日 内 Salazar庄园书房
人物：Damián、Isadora、Alberto、管家
△Alberto表情愧疚。
Alberto：是我没教好Julián，才让他对你们造成了困扰，这是我的一点补偿。我只有一个要求，那就是别声张这件事。
Alberto: I failed to raise Julián properly, and his actions caused you serious trouble. Please accept this as compensation. I have only one request: keep this matter out of the public eye.
△Isadora和Damián对视一眼。
Alberto：我会让Julián和Eloísa正常服刑，等他们出来之后我会把他们送出国。以后不会再打扰你们。
Alberto: Julián and Eloísa will serve their sentences. Once they are released, I will send them abroad. They will never bother you again.
△Damián握住Isadora的手。
Isadora：（点头）好。
Isadora: (nods) Very well.
△Damián和Isadora起身刚要离开，Alberto张口。
Alberto：Damián请稍等一下，我有点事要跟你说。
△Damián和Isadora疑惑对视一眼，Damián拉住Isadora，眼神坚定的看着Alberto。
Damián：有什么事就说吧，不用避着Isadora。
Damián: Just tell me what's on your mind – there's no need to hide it from Isadora.
△Alberto叹了口气。
△Damián和Isadora再次坐了下来。
△Alberto递上一份亲子鉴定文件给Damián。
Alberto：你们看看这个。
Alberto: Take a look at this.
△Damián疑惑接过，翻开看了一眼，整个人瞬间僵住。
△Isadora疑惑上前查看，表情震惊。
Isadora：（不可置信）什么？Damián是Salazar家的亲孙子？
Isadora: (In disbelief) What? Damián is the Salazar family's biological grandson?
△Damián面色凝重。
△Alberto看着Damián，表情动容。
Alberto：我知道这件事对你来说有很大冲击，第一时间让你回到Salazar家也不现实。可我希望，你不要排斥我。
Alberto: II know this is a lot to take in. It wouldn't be realistic to expect you to return to the Salazar family right away. But I hope you won't shut me out.
△Isadora担忧的看着Damián。
△Alberto拿出一张银行卡递给Damián。
Alberto：这是我的一点诚意，里面有一个亿，之后每个月也会有固定的一百万打进去，算是Salazar家对你的补偿，希望你能收下，不要……
Alberto: This is just a token of my sincerity. There's one hundred million in the account, and I'll have another one million deposited every month as compensation from the Salazar family. I hope you'll accept it. Please don't...
△Alberto语气动容。
Alberto：不要拒绝爷爷。
Alberto: Don't refuse Grandpa!
△Damián看了眼卡，又看了眼银行卡，深呼吸了一口气，推了回去。
△Alberto的表情僵在脸上。
Damián：（叹气）事情我知道了，我需要时间接受。
Damián: (Sighs) I understand what's going on; I need some time to come to terms with all of this.
△Isadora握住Damián的手。
△Alberto表情忧伤。
Damián：（话锋一转）但是，我之后会来常见你的，爷爷。
Damián:  But I'll come visit you often from now on, Grandpa.
△Alberto一惊，惊喜的抬头看着Damián。
Alberto：（热泪盈眶）好。
Alberto: (tears welling up in his eyes) OK!
80-2夜 内 卧室
人物：Damián、Isadora
△Damián和Isadora穿着睡衣躺在床上。
△Isadora看了眼Damián爬到Damián怀里。
Isadora：没想到你就是Salazar家找了那么久的孙子。
Isadora: I can't believe you're the grandson the Salazar family has been searching for all these years.
△Damián看着Isadora，抱紧了Isadora。
Damián：怎么了？更爱我了？
Damián: What is it? Do you love me even more now?
△Isadora翻了个白眼。
Isadora：你是Salazar家的孙子，证明我们原本就是要结婚的。这是上帝早就定好的。
Isadora: You're the Salazar family's grandson. That proves we were always meant to be together. God must have planned it this way from the very beginning.
△Damián亲了口Isadora，到Isadora耳边吹气。
Damián：这就说明，谁都不会将我们分开。
△Isadora笑着捏了捏Damián的鼻子。
Isadora：那Salazar家的孙子想不想当公司的副总？
Isadora: So, does the Salazar heir want to be the company's vice president?
△Damián脸色大变，翻身压住了Isadora。
Damián：不要，当部长已经很累了，我还是喜欢被你养。
Damián: No, thanks. Being a director is exhausting enough. I'd rather let you support me.
△Damián亲上Isadora的嘴。
△Damián松开Isadora，两人脸色泛红。
△Damián为Isadora整理头发，眼神眷恋。
Damián：Isadora，我爱你。
Damián: Isadora, I love you.
△Isadora幸福地看着Damián。
Isadora：（笑）我也爱你。
Isadora: (smiles) I love you too.
△两人再次亲在一起。
